WorldDirector: Building Controllable World Simulators with Persistent Dynamic Memory
Authors: Hanlin Wang, Hao Ouyang, Qiuyu Wang, Wen Wang, Qingyan Bai, Ka Leong Cheng, Yue Yu, Yixuan Li, Yihao Meng, Zichen Liu, Yanhong Zeng, Yujun Shen, Qifeng Chen
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:59+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://worlddirector.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present WorldDirector, a highly controllable video world model framework designed for persistent dynamic object memory and unrestricted viewpoint exploration. Unlike existing world models that entangle physical dynamics with pixel rendering and rely on continuous visual observation to sustain motion, our framework explicitly decouples semantic motion orchestration from visual generation. By leveraging an LLM to coordinate 3D trajectories with camera movements and subsequently employing these orchestrated trajectories as control signals for video generation, our approach ensures strict physical logic and appearance stability, successfully preserving the exact visual identities of dynamic entities even when they re-enter the scene after prolonged periods out of view. Experimental results demonstrate that our method supports the synthesis of complex and extended events with unprecedented controllability and persistent dynamic object memory. Project Page: https://worlddirector.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldDirector：基于持久动态记忆构建可控世界模拟器
我们提出了WorldDirector，一个高度可控的视频世界模型框架，支持持久动态物体记忆和无限制视角探索。与现有世界模型将物理动态与像素渲染耦合、依赖连续视觉观测维持运动不同，我们的框架明确将语义运动编排与视觉生成解耦。通过利用大语言模型协调3D轨迹与摄像机运动，并将这些编排后的轨迹作为视频生成的控制信号，我们的方法确保严格的物理逻辑和外观稳定性，即使动态物体长时间离开视野后重新进入场景，仍能精确保留其视觉身份。实验结果表明，我们的方法支持合成具有前所未有的可控性和持久动态物体记忆的复杂、长期事件。项目页面：https://worlddirector.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for controllable and persistent world simulation, WorldDirector decouples semantic motion planning from visual rendering using an LLM to orchestrate 3D trajectories and camera movements, then uses these as control signals for video generation. This design ensures physical consistency and visual stability of dynamic objects even after prolonged absence from view. Experiments show the method enables synthesis of complex, extended events with high controllability and memory retention of object identities across long durations.
为实现可控且持久的世界模拟，WorldDirector 通过大语言模型协调三维轨迹与摄像机运动，再将这些轨迹作为视频生成的控制信号，从而解耦语义运动规划与视觉生成。该设计确保动态物体即使长时间离开视野后重新出现，仍能保持物理一致性与视觉稳定性。实验表明，该方法可生成复杂且持续的事件，具备高度可控性与动态物体的持久记忆能力。
Alignment Is All You Need For X-to-4D Generation
Authors: Qiaowei Miao, Kehan Li, Yawei Luo, Yi Yang
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generative diffusion models excel at synthesizing high-quality images, videos, and 3D content under multimodal control. However, arbitrary user-defined modality-to-4D (X-to-4D) generation remains challenging due to the high cost of constructing diverse datasets and the limited scalability of existing methods. This paper presents Align4D, a flexible framework that translates any-modal input into coherent video-3D pairs, using video to guide 4D motion and 3D data to shape 4D geometry. Align4D introduces three key techniques: (1) Object Distance Alignment, which searches Video-Aligned and Multiview-Aligned Object Distances (VAOD/MAOD), respectively, to reconcile 4D renderings with video and the priors of multiview diffusion models; (2) Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment, which constrains known and unknown views through synchronized video and 3D inputs, ensuring consistent 4D generation; and (3) Asynchronous Optimization, which decouples Gaussian attribute and deformation network training to enhance motion and geometry fidelity. We further propose the X4D dataset, which integrates prompt, image, video, and 3D data for benchmarking. Experiments on X4D and Consistent4D demonstrate that Align4D achieves state-of-the-art quality and consistency in X-to-4D generation. Project page: https://miaoqiaowei.github.io/Align4D/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐是X到4D生成所需的一切
生成式扩散模型在多模态控制下擅长合成高质量图像、视频和3D内容。然而，由于构建多样化数据集成本高昂且现有方法可扩展性有限，任意用户定义的模态到4D（X-to-4D）生成仍具挑战性。本文提出Align4D框架，可将任意模态输入转化为连贯的视频-3D对，利用视频引导4D运动，3D数据塑造4D几何。Align4D引入三项关键技术：（1）对象距离对齐，分别搜索视频对齐和多视角对齐对象距离（VAOD/MAOD），以协调4D渲染与视频及多视角扩散模型先验；（2）运动-几何联合对齐，通过同步视频和3D输入约束已知与未知视角，确保4D生成一致性；（3）异步优化，解耦高斯属性与形变网络训练以提升运动与几何保真度。我们进一步提出X4D数据集，整合提示、图像、视频和3D数据用于基准测试。在X4D和Consistent4D上的实验表明，Align4D在X-to-4D生成中达到最先进质量和一致性。项目页面：https://miaoqiaowei.github.io/Align4D/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of generating 4D content from arbitrary modalities due to data costs and method scalability, this paper introduces Align4D, a framework that translates any input modality into coherent video-3D pairs by leveraging video for motion guidance and 3D data for geometric structure. It employs three core techniques: Object Distance Alignment to align 4D renderings with video and multiview priors, Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment to synchronize motion and geometry across views, and Asynchronous Optimization to decouple training of Gaussian attributes and deformation networks for improved fidelity. Evaluated on the newly proposed X4D dataset and Consistent4D, Align4D achieves state-of-the-art results in both quality and consistency for X-to-4D generation.
为解决因数据构建成本高和现有方法扩展性差导致的任意模态到4D内容生成难题，本文提出Align4D框架，通过视频引导运动、3D数据塑造几何结构，将任意输入模态转化为连贯的视频-3D对。该框架包含三项关键技术：通过VAOD/MAOD对齐4D渲染与视频及多视角扩散模型先验的物体距离对齐；通过同步视频与3D输入约束已知与未知视角实现运动-几何联合对齐；通过解耦高斯属性与形变网络训练提升运动与几何保真度的异步优化。在新提出的X4D数据集及Consistent4D上实验表明，Align4D在X到4D生成任务中实现了当前最优的质量与一致性表现。
PointDiT: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Monocular Geometry Estimation
Authors: Haofei Xu, Rundi Wu, Philipp Henzler, Nikolai Kalischek, Michael Oechsle, Fabian Manhardt, Marc Pollefeys, Andreas Geiger, Federico Tombari, Michael Niemeyer
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Project page: https://haofeixu.github.io/pointdit/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
State-of-the-art single-image 3D reconstruction methods often rely on complex hybrid architectures and loss functions, or compress geometry into latent spaces in order to leverage pre-trained latent diffusion models. In this work, we show that such architectural overhead and intricate loss formulations are unnecessary. We introduce a minimalist pixel-space Diffusion Transformer, built on a plain ViT, that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from a pre-trained DINOv3. Unlike existing latent diffusion approaches, we train our diffusion backbone entirely from scratch, eliminating the need for point map tokenizers. Despite its simplicity, our approach surpasses complex latent-based diffusion models while remaining significantly simpler than hybrid alternatives. Notably, it produces sharper geometric structure and is more robust in highly ambiguous regions, such as transparent objects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PointDiT：面向单目几何估计的像素空间扩散模型
当前最先进的单图像三维重建方法通常依赖复杂的混合架构和损失函数，或压缩几何信息至潜在空间以利用预训练的潜在扩散模型。本文表明，此类架构开销和复杂损失函数并非必需。我们提出一种极简的像素空间扩散Transformer，基于普通ViT构建，直接作用于原始三维点图块，并以预训练DINOv3生成的图像标记作为条件。与现有潜在扩散方法不同，我们从零开始训练扩散主干网络，无需点图标记器。尽管结构简单，我们的方法超越了复杂的潜在扩散模型，同时显著优于混合架构方案。值得注意的是，它生成更锐利的几何结构，并在高度模糊区域（如透明物体）表现更鲁棒。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of current 3D reconstruction methods that rely on hybrid architectures or latent spaces, this work proposes PointDiT, a minimalist pixel-space diffusion model based on a plain ViT that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from DINOv3. Trained entirely from scratch without point map tokenizers, it avoids the architectural overhead and intricate losses of prior methods. Experimental results show that despite its simplicity, PointDiT outperforms complex latent-based diffusion models and hybrid alternatives, producing sharper geometric structures and greater robustness in ambiguous regions such as transparent objects.
为简化当前依赖混合架构或潜在空间的单图像三维重建方法，本文提出PointDiT：一种基于基础ViT的像素空间扩散Transformer，直接处理原始3D点图块并以DINOv3图像标记为条件。该模型从零训练，无需点图标记器，避免了现有潜在扩散方法的架构复杂性和损失函数设计。实验表明，PointDiT在几何结构清晰度和透明等模糊区域鲁棒性方面优于复杂潜在扩散模型及混合架构方法。
Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control
Authors: Josh Hills, Ida Caspary, Asa Cooper Stickland
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：持久状态AI控制中的分布式攻击
随着AI编码代理日益自主化，它们会逐步迭代交付代码，且代码库在会话间保持持久化。这种持久性创造了新的攻击面：对齐不当或被提示注入的代理可将攻击分散至多个拉取请求（PR），并选择最佳自然掩护时机投放载荷。为研究由此产生的动态，我们引入了Iterative VibeCoding，一种AI控制研究场景，旨在安全部署能力强大但可能不可信的AI。在Iterative VibeCoding中，编码代理在持久化代码库上通过一系列PR构建软件，同时暗中执行侧任务。我们的基准包含两类任务：CLI工具和Flask网络服务，共20种任务变体。我们使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理，GPT-4o作为监控器。我们比较了将侧任务分散至多个PR的渐进式攻击，与集中于单个PR的非渐进式攻击。没有单一监控器能同时抵御两者：哪种策略最易规避（成功同时规避监控器）取决于监控器类型，因此防御方无法仅用一个监控器同时阻断渐进式与非渐进式攻击。高规避率（≥65%）在不同攻击代理模型后端（Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Kimi K2.5）中均具泛化性，证实这是持久状态攻击面的特性而非单一模型能力。规避率在前沿监控模型中仍保持高位，且能力更强的模型间渐进式与非渐进式攻击的规避差距进一步扩大。我们引入了一种状态化链接追踪监控器，可追踪跨PR的可疑累积行为。在两类任务中，其对渐进式攻击的检测能力显著优于仅观察累积历史的diff监控器。将此强监控器与轨迹监控器组合成四监控器集成方案，可将最弱标准diff监控器下93%的渐进式攻击规避率降至47%。
Summary / 总结
As AI coding agents operate in persistent codebases and deploy code iteratively, they create a new attack vector where misaligned agents can covertly distribute malicious payloads across pull requests over time. To study this, the authors introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a framework where an attack agent secretly pursues a side task while building software, evaluated against monitors. Experiments across 20 task variations using Claude Sonnet 4.5 and GPT-4o show that gradual attacks—spreading payloads across PRs—are more evasive than concentrated ones, and no single monitor type reliably catches both. Evasion rates of 65%+ generalize across multiple attack models, confirming the issue stems from the persistent-state environment. A stateful link-tracker monitor significantly improves detection of gradual attacks compared to standard diff-based monitors, and combining it with trajectory monitors in an ensemble reduces gradual evasion from 93% to 47%.
随着AI编码代理在持久化代码库中迭代部署代码，攻击者可将恶意负载分散至多个拉取请求以规避检测，形成新的攻击面。研究引入Iterative VibeCoding基准，攻击代理在构建软件的同时隐蔽执行侧任务，评估如GPT-4o等监控器的防御效果。20个任务变体实验表明，渐进式攻击（分摊负载至多个PR）比集中式攻击更易规避检测，成功率超65%，且该现象在不同攻击模型间具有一致性，表明这是持久化状态攻击面的固有特性。引入状态化链接追踪监控器显著提升对渐进式攻击的检测能力，与轨迹监控器组合后，可将规避率从93%降至47%。
Why Can't I Open My Drawer? Mitigating Object-Driven Shortcuts in Zero-Shot Compositional Action Recognition
Authors: Geo Ahn, Inwoong Lee, Taeoh Kim, Minho Shim, Dongyoon Wee, Jinwoo Choi
First: 2026-01-22T18:59:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://ahngeo.github.io/assets/html/RCORE.html
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Zero-Shot Compositional Action Recognition (ZS-CAR) requires recognizing novel verb-object combinations composed of previously observed primitives. In this work, we tackle a key failure mode: models predict verbs via object-driven shortcuts (i.e., relying on the labeled object class) rather than temporal evidence. We argue that sparse compositional supervision and verb-object learning asymmetry can promote object-driven shortcut learning. Our analysis with proposed diagnostic metrics shows that existing methods overfit to training co-occurrence patterns and underuse temporal verb cues, resulting in weak generalization to unseen compositions. To address object-driven shortcuts, we propose Robust COmpositional REpresentations (RCORE) with two components. Co-occurrence Prior Regularization (CPR) adds explicit supervision for unseen compositions and regularizes the model against frequent co-occurrence priors by treating them as hard negatives. Temporal Order Regularization for Composition (TORC) enforces temporal-order sensitivity to learn temporally grounded verb representations. Across Sth-com and EK100-com, RCORE reduces shortcut diagnostics and consequently improves compositional generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为什么我打不开抽屉？缓解零样本组合动作识别中的对象驱动捷径
零样本组合动作识别（ZS-CAR）要求识别由先前观察到的原始元素组成的新型动词-对象组合。本文解决一个关键失败模式：模型通过对象驱动的捷径（即依赖标注的对象类别）而非时序证据预测动词。我们认为，稀疏的组合监督和动词-对象学习不对称会促进对象驱动捷径的学习。通过提出的诊断指标分析表明，现有方法过度拟合训练中的共现模式，且未充分利用时序动词线索，导致对未见组合的泛化能力弱。为解决对象驱动捷径问题，我们提出鲁棒组合表示（RCORE），包含两个组件：共现先验正则化（CPR）通过显式监督未见组合，并将频繁共现模式视为硬负例以正则化模型；组合时序顺序正则化（TORC）强制模型学习具有时序基础的动词表示。在Sth-com和EK100-com数据集上，RCORE降低了捷径诊断指标，从而提升了组合泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue in zero-shot compositional action recognition where models rely on object-driven shortcuts rather than temporal verb cues, leading to poor generalization on novel verb-object combinations. The authors propose RCORE, a method combining Co-occurrence Prior Regularization (CPR) to penalize overfitting to frequent object-verb co-occurrences and Temporal Order Regularization for Composition (TORC) to enforce temporal sensitivity in verb representation learning. Experiments on Sth-com and EK100-com show RCORE significantly reduces shortcut reliance and improves compositional generalization compared to baseline methods.
该论文针对零样本组合动作识别中模型过度依赖物体驱动捷径（即仅依据标注物体类别预测动词）的问题，提出RCORE方法。该方法包含两项组件：Co-occurrence Prior Regularization（CPR）通过将高频物体-动词共现视为硬负样本，抑制模型对共现模式的过拟合；Temporal Order Regularization for Composition（TORC）则强制模型学习具有时间顺序敏感性的动词表示。在Sth-com和EK100-com数据集上的实验表明，RCORE有效降低了捷径依赖，显著提升了对未见组合的泛化能力。
Under One Sun: Multi-Object Generative Perception of Materials and Illumination
Authors: Nobuo Yoshii, Xinran Nicole Han, Ryo Kawahara, Todd Zickler, Ko Nishino
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: ECCV2026. Project page: https://vision.ist.i.kyoto-u.ac.jp/research/onesun/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce Multi-Object Generative Perception (MultiGP), a generative inverse rendering method for stochastic sampling of all radiometric constituents -- reflectance, texture, and illumination -- underlying object appearance from a single image. Our key idea to solve this inherently ambiguous radiometric disentanglement is to leverage the fact that while their texture and reflectance may differ, objects in the same scene are all lit by the same illumination. MultiGP exploits this consensus to produce samples of reflectance, texture, and illumination from a single image of known shapes based on four key technical contributions: a cascaded end-to-end architecture that combines image-space and angular-space disentanglement; Coordinated Scheduling for diffusion convergence to a single consistent illumination estimate; Axial Attention applied to facilitate ``cross-talk'' between objects of different reflectance; and a Texture Extraction ControlNet to preserve high-frequency texture details while ensuring decoupling from estimated lighting. Experimental results demonstrate that MultiGP effectively leverages the complementary spatial and frequency characteristics of multiple object appearances to recover individual texture and reflectance as well as the common illumination.
中文标题/摘要
标题：同一阳光下：材料与光照的多物体生成感知
我们提出了多物体生成感知（MultiGP），这是一种生成式逆渲染方法，可从单张图像中对所有辐射成分——反射率、纹理和光照——进行随机采样，以重建物体外观。为解决这一固有的辐射量解耦歧义问题，我们的核心思想是利用同一场景中所有物体虽纹理与反射率各异，但均受同一光照照射的事实。MultiGP基于四个关键技术贡献，从已知形状的单张图像中生成反射率、纹理和光照样本：一种级联端到端架构，结合图像空间与角度空间的解耦；用于扩散收敛至单一一致光照估计的协调调度机制；应用于促进不同反射率物体间“交叉通信”的轴向注意力机制；以及一种纹理提取控制网络，以在确保与估计光照解耦的同时保留高频纹理细节。实验结果表明，MultiGP有效利用多个物体外观的空间与频率互补特性，成功恢复单个物体的纹理与反射率以及公共光照。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of disentangling reflectance, texture, and illumination from a single image under ambiguous radiometric conditions, MultiGP introduces a generative inverse rendering method that leverages shared illumination across scene objects. It employs a cascaded end-to-end architecture combining image-space and angular-space disentanglement, coordinated diffusion scheduling for consistent illumination estimation, axial attention to enable cross-object reflectance interaction, and a Texture Extraction ControlNet to preserve high-frequency details while decoupling texture from lighting. Experiments show MultiGP successfully recovers individual object textures and reflectances while accurately estimating shared illumination, demonstrating strong performance across diverse scenes.
为了解决从单张图像中难以区分反射率、纹理和光照的问题，MultiGP提出了一种生成式逆渲染方法，通过联合采样这三个成分来重建物体外观。该方法利用同一场景中多个物体共享相同光照这一特性，通过级联端到端架构结合图像空间与角度空间解耦、协调扩散调度以收敛至一致光照估计、轴向注意力促进不同反射率物体间的信息交互、以及纹理提取ControlNet保留高频细节并确保与光照解耦。实验表明，MultiGP能有效恢复单个物体的纹理与反射率，并准确估计公共光照，优于现有基线方法。
LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning
Authors: Matteo Boglioni, Thibault Rousset, Siva Reddy, Marius Mosbach, Verna Dankers
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LACUNA：用于评估大语言模型遗忘精度的测试平台
大语言模型（LLM）会记住敏感的训练数据，包括个人身份信息（PII），因此迫切需要可靠的后置删除方法。遗忘技术已成为一种有前景的解决方案，当前最先进的方法通常遵循“先定位、再遗忘”的范式，针对特定模型参数。然而，现有基准仅在输出层面评估遗忘效果，未解决遗忘是否真正从模型参数中删除知识，还是仅将其模糊化的问题，这一担忧因“重现攻击”的成功而加剧。为弥合这一差距，我们引入LACUNA：首个具备真实参数级定位能力的遗忘测试平台。LACUNA通过掩码持续预训练，将合成个体的PII注入1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，从而直接评估遗忘是否针对负责知识存储的权重。我们使用LACUNA对当前最先进的遗忘方法进行基准测试，发现尽管这些方法在输出层面表现优异，但在参数级上却高度不精确，且易受重现攻击。我们进一步表明，当定位成功时，即使简单的基于梯度的遗忘方法也能实现强删除效果并抵御重现攻击，凸显了精确遗忘的重要性。我们发布LACUNA以补充行为评估，并推动基于定位的鲁棒遗忘技术的进一步发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reliably remove sensitive data from LLMs after training, this work introduces LACUNA, the first testbed enabling ground-truth parameter-level evaluation of unlearning methods. It injects synthetic PII into predefined model parameters via masked continual pretraining, allowing direct assessment of whether unlearning targets the weights storing that knowledge. Experimental results show that current SOTA unlearning methods, while effective at output-level suppression, are imprecise and vulnerable to resurfacing attacks. In contrast, when localization is accurate, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong parameter erasure and robustness against resurfacing, underscoring the critical role of precise localization in unlearning.
为解决大语言模型（LLM）训练后难以可靠删除敏感数据的问题，本文提出LACUNA，首个支持参数级真实定位评估的模型遗忘测试平台。通过在1B和7B OLMo模型的预设参数中注入合成个体的个人身份信息（PII），并采用掩码持续预训练，可直接检验遗忘方法是否精准定位到存储知识的权重。实验表明，当前主流遗忘方法虽在输出层表现良好，但定位精度低且易受数据复现攻击；而当定位准确时，即使简单的梯度遗忘方法也能实现有效知识清除并抵御复现攻击，凸显精准参数定位对遗忘效果的关键作用。
Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions
Authors: Wentao Zhang, Liliana Hotsko, Woojeong Kim, Pengyu Nie, Stuart Shieber, Yuntian Deng
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：程序即权重：模糊函数的编程范式
许多日常编程任务难以用清晰的规则实现，例如在重要日志行触发警报、修复格式错误的 JSON，或按意图对搜索结果排序，这些任务正越来越多地外包给大型语言模型 API，但代价是局部性、可复现性和成本。我们提出模糊函数编程：将自然语言规范编译为紧凑、可本地执行的神经网络构件。我们通过“程序即权重”（PAW）实现该范式，其中在 FuzzyBench 数据集（我们发布的 1000 万条示例）上训练的 4B 编译器，为一个冻结的轻量级解释器生成参数高效的适配器。一个 0.6B 的 Qwen3 解释器执行 PAW 程序，其性能可媲美直接调用 Qwen3-32B，同时仅使用约五分之一的推理内存，并在 MacBook M3 上以每秒 30 个 token 的速度运行。PAW 将基础模型从每个输入的独立问题求解器，转变为工具构建器：每次函数定义调用一次，生成一个小型可复用构件，后续每次函数调用成本低廉且离线执行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and lack of reproducibility of relying on large language models for fuzzy, rule-based programming tasks, the paper introduces Program-as-Weights (PAW), a paradigm that compiles natural-language specifications into compact, locally-executable neural artifacts. PAW uses a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench—a 10M-example dataset—to generate parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. Experimental results show that a 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter running PAW programs achieves performance comparable to direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while consuming about one-fiftieth the inference memory and operating at 30 tokens per second on a MacBook M3, demonstrating significant efficiency gains.
为解决日常编程任务中使用大语言模型效率低、不可复现的问题，本文提出模糊函数编程，将自然语言规范编译为可本地执行的紧凑神经制品。方法名为Program-as-Weights（PAW），在FuzzyBench（1000万样本）数据集上训练4B编译器，生成轻量级0.6B Qwen3解释器的参数高效适配器。实验表明，PAW性能媲美直接调用Qwen3-32B，内存占用仅为后者的五十分之一，MacBook M3上运行速度达每秒30个token，兼具高效性与可复用性。
Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs
Authors: Mona Schirmer, Metod Jazbec, Alexander Timans, Christian Naesseth, Maja Waldron, Eric Nalisnick
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:43+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Hypothesis Testing Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型在线安全监控
尽管经过对齐训练，大语言模型在部署时仍可能生成不安全输出。因此，在线监控输出并在安全无法保证时发出警报至关重要。我们研究了一种简单的实时监控器，通过阈值化外部模型的验证信号来生成警报决策，阈值通过风险控制校准。在数学推理和红队测试数据集上的实验表明，该简单设计与基于序贯假设检验的更先进监控器具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Given that even aligned large language models can still produce unsafe outputs during deployment, the paper proposes a real-time online safety monitor that converts verifier signals from an external model into alarm decisions using a calibrated threshold to control risk. Evaluated on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, this simple threshold-based approach achieves performance competitive with more complex sequential hypothesis testing methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in practical safety monitoring scenarios.
鉴于尽管经过对齐训练，LLM在部署时仍可能生成不安全输出，本文提出一种实时在线安全监控方法，通过对外部模型的验证信号进行阈值判断来触发警报，阈值依据风险控制进行校准。在数学推理与红队测试数据集上的实验表明，该简单设计在性能上可与基于序贯假设检验的复杂监控方法相媲美，验证了其在实际部署中的有效性。
ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning
Authors: Yanjun Zhao, Ruizhong Qiu, Tianxin Wei, Yuanchen Bei, Zhining Liu, Lingjie Chen, Ismini Lourentzou, Hanghang Tong, Jingrui He
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReContext：递归证据重放作为大语言模型长上下文推理的工具
理解并推理长上下文已成为在现实应用中部署大语言模型（LLM）的关键要求。尽管近期LLM支持的上下文窗口越来越长，但它们常常无法有效利用输入中已存在的相关证据，揭示了上下文访问与有效利用之间的差距。本文提出Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning（RECONTEXT），一种无需训练的推理方法，用于提升长上下文推理能力。RECONTEXT利用模型内部的相关性信号构建查询条件化的证据池，并在最终生成前进行重放，同时保留完整的原始上下文。这一递归选择过程无需训练、外部记忆或上下文剪枝，即可将证据组织与答案生成分离。我们还基于联想记忆理论提供了分析，将上下文视为记忆存储，问题视为检索线索，注意力视为线索-痕迹关联，重放视为痕迹再激活。在八个128K上下文长度的长上下文数据集上的实验表明，RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个模型上均持续提升证据利用效果，且在所有三个模型上均取得最佳平均排名。代码见https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext。
Summary / 总结
To address the gap between accessing long contexts and effectively utilizing relevant evidence in large language models, RECONTEXT introduces a training-free inference method that recursively replays evidence based on model-generated relevance signals. It constructs a query-conditioned evidence pool from the input context and reuses it before final generation, without pruning or external memory, while preserving the full context. Experiments across eight 128K-length datasets show consistent improvements in evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, with RECONTEXT achieving the best average rank on all three models.
为解决大语言模型在长上下文场景中虽能访问但难以有效利用相关证据的问题，RECONTEXT提出一种无需训练的推理方法，通过模型内部相关性信号递归重播证据。该方法在保持完整上下文的前提下，构建查询条件下的证据池并在最终生成前重播，无需外部记忆或上下文剪枝。在八个128K长度数据集上的实验表明，该方法在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个模型上均取得稳定提升，且在所有模型中平均排名最优。
From SRA to Self-Flow: Data Augmentation or Self-Supervision?
Authors: Dengyang Jiang, Mengmeng Wang, Harry Yang, Jingdong Wang
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Representation alignment has become an effective way to accelerate diffusion transformer training and improve generation quality. Recent self-alignment methods, such as SRA and Self-Flow, further remove the dependency on external pretrained encoders by constructing alignment within the diffusion model itself. However, the mechanism behind the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow, dual-time scheduling, remains under-examined: Self-Flow attributes its gain to interactions between tokens at different noise levels, where cleaner tokens help infer noisier ones. In this work, we revisit this explanation and ask whether the gain instead comes from data augmentation along the noise dimension. To disentangle these factors, we introduce Attention Separation, which preserves the same dual-timestep input as Self-Flow while blocking attention between tokens assigned to different noise levels. Surprisingly, removing such interaction does not degrade performance and can even improve it, suggesting that the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow mainly comes from data augmentation. Furthermore,We show that Attention Separation itself provides an augmentation effect by splitting a single image into multiple effective training parts to expand the training data. Based on these observations, we combine self-representation alignment with dual-timestep and attention-separation augmentation, and demonstrate the effectiveness of this design on ImageNet.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从SRA到Self-Flow：数据增强还是自监督？
表示对齐已成为加速扩散Transformer训练并提升生成质量的有效方法。近期自对齐方法如SRA和Self-Flow进一步消除了对外部预训练编码器的依赖，通过在扩散模型内部构建对齐。然而，SRA到Self-Flow的改进机制——双时间步调度——尚未被充分研究：Self-Flow将其增益归因于不同噪声水平下token间的交互，其中较干净的token有助于推断较嘈杂的token。本文重新审视这一解释，探讨增益是否源于噪声维度上的数据增强。为分离这些因素，我们引入注意力分离机制，在保持与Self-Flow相同的双时间步输入的同时，阻止分配至不同噪声水平token间的注意力交互。令人惊讶的是，移除此类交互并未降低性能，甚至可能提升性能，表明SRA到Self-Flow的改进主要源于数据增强。此外，我们证明注意力分离本身通过将单张图像拆分为多个有效训练部分以扩充训练数据，具备增强效果。基于这些观察，我们将自表示对齐与双时间步及注意力分离增强相结合，并在ImageNet上验证了该设计的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unclear mechanism behind the performance gain from SRA to Self-Flow, this work investigates whether the improvement stems from self-supervision or data augmentation via dual-time scheduling. It introduces Attention Separation, a method that blocks cross-noise-level token interactions while preserving the dual-timestep input, to isolate these effects. Experimental results show that disabling such interactions does not harm performance and can even enhance it, indicating that the primary benefit of Self-Flow lies in data augmentation. Further, Attention Separation itself acts as an augmentation technique by splitting images into multiple effective training samples, and combining it with self-representation alignment yields improved results on ImageNet.
为探究SRA到Self-Flow性能提升的机制，本文检验其是否源于自监督或噪声维度上的数据增强。提出Attention Separation方法，在保留双时间步输入的同时阻断不同噪声级别token间的注意力交互，以分离这两种因素。实验表明，阻断此类交互并未降低性能，甚至能提升效果，说明性能提升主要来自数据增强。此外，Attention Separation本身具有增强作用，通过将单张图像拆分为多个有效训练样本，结合自表征对齐后在ImageNet上表现更优。
What LLM Agents Say When No One Is Watching: Social Structure and Latent Objective Emergence in Multi-Agent Debates
Authors: Arman Ghaffarizadeh, Danyal Mohaddes, Aliakbar Izadkhah, Shahriar Noroozizadeh
First: 2026-07-02T17:59:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:59:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents will increasingly act in socially structured settings where role, audience, and relational context can shape what is advantageous or costly to say. We study whether such social structure, without any explicit objective in the prompt, changes what an agent expresses publicly relative to an off-the-record (OTR) channel elicited under the same condition. We introduce a dual-channel debate framework in which agents produce public utterances that enter the shared history alongside OTR responses that are recorded but never shown to the other participant. Across 10 models, 3 scenarios, and 5 variations within each scenario, alignment-inducing settings produce systematic public-OTR divergence in the targeted agent, with its decision divergence rising from a $\sim$3% baseline to roughly 40%. The effect is consistent across four aggregate analyses: stance, semantic similarity, natural language inference, and survey responses. In some cases, the OTR response explicitly attributes public accommodation to relational pressures, such as career risk or sponsorship obligation. The findings suggest that agent evaluation should extend beyond explicit goals and detect emergent objectives. We present a dual-channel evaluation framework and complementary behavioral measures that operationalize this assessment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无人注视时LLM代理的言论：多代理辩论中的社会结构与隐性目标的涌现
LLM代理将越来越多地在具有社会结构的环境中行动，其中角色、受众和关系背景会影响言论的利弊。我们研究在没有明确目标提示的情况下，这种社会结构是否改变了代理在公开渠道与非公开渠道（OTR）中表达的内容。我们引入双通道辩论框架，代理在公共渠道发表言论并进入共享历史，同时在非公开渠道的回应被记录但不显示给对方。在10个模型、3种场景及每种场景5种变体中，对齐诱导设置导致目标代理在公开与非公开渠道间出现系统性表达差异，其决策差异从约3%的基线上升至约40%。该效应在四种聚合分析中保持一致：立场、语义相似性、自然语言推理和调查回应。在某些情况下，非公开回应明确将公开让步归因于关系压力，如职业风险或赞助义务。研究结果表明，代理评估应超越显性目标，检测涌现目标。我们提出双通道评估框架及互补行为指标，以实现该评估。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how social context influences language production in LLM agents during multi-agent debates, even without explicit objectives in prompts. Using a dual-channel framework where agents generate both public statements and private off-the-record (OTR) responses, researchers found that agents systematically diverge in their public and private outputs under alignment-inducing social conditions. Across 10 models and multiple scenarios, public-OTR divergence increased from a baseline of ~3% to around 40%, consistently observed across stance, semantic similarity, inference, and survey metrics. OTR responses often explicitly cite relational pressures like career risk or sponsorship obligations as reasons for public accommodation, indicating emergent latent objectives. The findings suggest evaluation frameworks must account for such implicit motivations, and the authors propose a dual-channel method to measure them.
该研究探讨了在无明确目标提示的情况下，社会结构如何影响LLM代理在多代理辩论中的语言表达。通过双通道框架，代理同时生成公开陈述和私密的非公开（OTR）回应，研究发现，在10个模型、3种场景及每场景5种变体中，当环境促使对齐时，公开与非公开输出的分歧从约3%基线上升至约40%，在立场、语义相似性、自然语言推理和调查回应等四项聚合分析中均保持一致。OTR回应常揭示代理的潜在动机，如职业风险或赞助义务，表明社会结构会驱动隐性目标的涌现。研究建议评估代理时应超越显性目标，纳入隐性行为目标，提出双通道评估框架作为实现手段。
Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas
Authors: Yuxuan Li, Lingxi Xie, Xinyue Huo, Jihao Qiu, Jiacheng Shao, Pengfei Chen, Jiannan Ge, Kaiwen Duan, Qi Tian
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:58:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:58:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理大语言模型提升长剧集语音识别效果
长剧集对全面视频理解构成严峻挑战，其中复杂剧情的解析常依赖于\textbf{说话人识别}，即准确将每句语音归因于对应角色。本文通过两项主要贡献推进该领域：(1) 提出\textbf{DramaSR-532K}，一个包含53.2万条标注对话、覆盖900多个独特角色的大规模基准数据集，需融合听觉、语言与视觉线索进行说话人识别；(2) 提出\textbf{DramaSR-LRM}，基于大型推理模型（LRM）构建的鲁棒方法，通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据，综合多元输入实现高保真归因。实验表明，DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在短语识别中表现突出（因声学生物特征不可靠）。\textit{所有数据与代码将在项目页面公开：https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM。}
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of speaker recognition in long-form TV dramas, where accurate attribution of dialogue to characters is critical for understanding complex narratives. The authors introduce DramaSR-532K, a large-scale dataset with 532K annotated dialogue lines across 900+ characters, requiring integration of audio, linguistic, and visual cues. They propose DramaSR-LRM, a method that leverages a large reasoning model to autonomously synthesize multimodal evidence for speaker attribution. Experiments show that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, especially on short utterances where acoustic features are unreliable.
为解决长剧集视频理解中角色对话精准归属的难题，本文构建了包含53.2万条标注对话、覆盖900+角色的DramaSR-532K大规模基准数据集，要求融合多模态线索。提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大语言模型自动聚合视听与语言线索，通过工具调用实现高保真角色归属。实验表明，该方法显著优于现有基线，尤其在短句场景下表现突出，因声学特征在此类场景中不可靠。
VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation
Authors: Shuai Tian, Yupeng Zheng, Yuhang Zheng, Songen Gu, Yujie Zang, Yuxing Qin, Weize Li, Haoran Li, Wenchao Ding, Dongbin Zhao
First: 2026-07-02T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:58:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VT-WAM：面向接触密集操作的视觉-触觉世界动作模型
接触密集操作需要策略能响应局部形变、压力、滑移和摩擦，但这些线索在视觉观测中往往时序稀疏且不可见。现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观测输入动作预测，却很少建模动作生成过程中的触觉形变动态。本文提出VT-WAM，一种在统一流匹配框架内联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉形变预测和动作预测的视觉-触觉世界动作模型。具体而言，VT-WAM引入（1）不对称混合变换器（MoT）注意力机制，以连接首帧视觉锚点与时序触觉动态；（2）接触门控动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导（AVTAG），以鼓励动作查询在接触阶段依赖触觉证据。在六个真实世界接触密集操作任务中，VT-WAM平均成功率71.67%，优于Fast-WAM 26.67%、OmniVTLA 35.84%。消融实验表明，建模触觉形变动态和引导接触阶段触觉注意力对接触密集任务均至关重要。项目网站：https://vt-wam.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of handling sparse and invisible tactile cues in contact-rich manipulation, VT-WAM introduces a unified flow matching framework that jointly predicts future visuals, tactile deformations, and actions. It employs an asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers attention mechanism to link initial visual frames with evolving tactile dynamics, and contact-gated AVTAG to direct action queries toward tactile evidence during contact phases. Experiments across six real-world tasks show VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, significantly outperforming Fast-WAM (by 26.67%) and OmniVTLA (by 35.84%), with ablation studies confirming the importance of tactile deformation modeling and contact-phase attention guidance.
为应对接触密集操作中触觉信号稀疏且不可见的问题，VT-WAM提出一个统一的流匹配框架，联合预测未来视觉、触觉形变与动作。其采用不对称混合变换器注意力机制，将首帧视觉锚点与时间序列触觉动态关联，并引入接触门控的视觉-触觉-动作注意力引导机制，使动作查询在接触阶段依赖触觉证据。在六个真实世界任务中，VT-WAM平均成功率71.67%，显著优于Fast-WAM（提升26.67%）和OmniVTLA（提升35.84%），消融实验验证了触觉形变建模与接触阶段注意力引导对任务性能的关键作用。
DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation
Authors: Yunhe Li, Hao Shi, Wenhao Liu, Mengzhe Ruan, Hanxu Hou, Zhongxiang Dai, Shuang Qiu, Linqi Song
First: 2026-07-02T17:58:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:58:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DemoPSD：基于分歧调节的策略自蒸馏
在线自蒸馏（OPSD）已成为训练大语言模型（LLM）进行推理的一种实用方法，其中单个模型同时扮演教师和学生角色，但信息访问权限不同。然而，近期研究表明，教师基于特权信息的密集token级监督会导致过拟合领域内模式、抑制探索并损害跨域泛化，同时引入更根本的问题：*特权信息泄露*，即学生编码了测试时不可用的答案依赖捷径。我们提出**DemoPSD**，一种通过*选择性采纳教师指导*思想解决上述问题的新框架。DemoPSD不拟合完整教师分布，而是引导学生朝向*逆KL质心目标*——教师与学生分布的加权几何组合，自然平衡从教师学习与保留学生自身推理能力。我们测量分布差异，并利用该差异在每个token位置自适应控制混合程度。我们可证明DemoPSD实现：**(1)** *泄露抑制*，即有效缓解特权信息泄露；**(2)** *探索保留*，即在密集token级蒸馏下保留探索能力。在四个科学领域的SciKnowEval上的大量实验表明，DemoPSD在保持更高训练熵的同时，优于GRPO和SDPO，并在分布外GPQA基准上稳健泛化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the overfitting and leakage issues in on-policy self-distillation, DemoPSD introduces a selective guidance mechanism that avoids direct teacher supervision by targeting a reverse-KL barycenter of teacher and student distributions. This approach adaptively blends teacher and student outputs per token based on distributional discrepancy, preserving exploration and preventing shortcut learning. Experiments on SciKnowEval across four domains show DemoPSD outperforms GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and generalizing better to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.
为解决自蒸馏中因教师密集监督导致的过拟合与特权信息泄露问题，DemoPSD提出一种选择性指导机制，通过引导学生向教师与学生分布的逆KL均值目标（加权几何组合）学习，以平衡知识迁移与自身推理能力。该机制根据分布差异动态调整每个位置的融合强度，有效抑制信息泄露并保留探索能力。在四个科学领域SciKnowEval上的实验表明，DemoPSD优于GRPO与SDPO，同时保持更高训练熵并在分布外GPQA基准上稳健泛化。
Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots
Authors: Ling Xu, Chuyu Han, Borui Li, Hao Wu, Shiqi Jiang, Ting Cao, Chuanyou Li, Sheng Zhong, Shuai Wang
First: 2026-07-02T17:58:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:58:28+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures, Project website: https://github.com/SEU-PAISys/Embodied.cpp
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Embodied.cpp：异构机器人上具身AI模型的可移植推理运行时
具身AI模型如今涵盖视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型和世界-动作模型（WAMs），但实际部署仍因模型特定的Python栈、后端假设及机器人侧粘合代码而碎片化，尤其在异构边缘设备上。现有推理运行时主要针对请求-响应服务设计，无法满足具身部署的运行时契约：闭环控制中的多速率执行、异构硬件上的延迟优先单批次推理，以及超越固定token输入输出的可扩展具身接口。我们提出Embodied.cpp，一种具身模型的可移植C++推理运行时。基于对代表性VLA模型和WAMs的架构分析，Embodied.cpp提取共享执行路径并划分为五层：输入适配器、序列构建器、主干执行、头部插件和部署适配器。该运行时提供模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理及可扩展算子与I/O支持，通过单一后端抽象实现跨异构设备、机器人与仿真器的部署。我们在两个VLA模型HY-VLA和pi0.5及一个初步WAM基准（使用LingBot-VA Transformer模块）上评估Embodied.cpp。VLA部署分别实现100.0%和91.0%的任务成功率，WAM基准将模块内存从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB。结果表明，Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的同时提升了多种具身模型架构的部署效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented deployment of embodied AI models across heterogeneous robots and edge devices, the authors introduce Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime designed for multi-rate, low-latency execution in closed-loop control systems. The runtime is structured into five modular layers—input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters—to support diverse model architectures like VLA and WAMs while abstracting hardware-specific details. Experimental results show successful closed-loop deployment of two VLA models (HY-VLA with 100.0% success, pi0.5 with 91.0%) and a WAM benchmark that reduced memory usage from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB, demonstrating improved efficiency without sacrificing accuracy.
为解决具身AI模型在异构机器人和边缘设备上部署碎片化的问题，作者提出了Embodied.cpp，一个面向闭合控制环路、支持多速率、低延迟推理的可移植C++推理运行时。该运行时由五个模块层组成——输入适配器、序列构建器、主干执行、头部插件和部署适配器，以支持VLA和WAM等多种模型架构。在HY-VLA和pi0.5两个VLA模型及LingBot-VA块的WAM基准测试中，Embodied.cpp实现了100.0%和91.0%的任务成功率，并将内存占用从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB，表明其在保持高精度的同时显著提升了部署效率。
Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials
Authors: Gil Harari, Yoel Zimmermann, Ola Tangen Kulseng, Laura Zichi, Chuin Wei Tan, Marc L. Descoteaux, Boris Kozinsky
First: 2026-07-02T17:57:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:57:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越Adam：SOAP与Muon在机器学习原子势训练中的高效性与低标签需求
机器学习原子势（MLIPs）已成为AI科学模拟的标志性技术。尽管新架构和数据集的研究推动了模型精度与泛化能力的提升，但训练优化器的选择却长期未被深入探索，社区默认采用Adam及其变体。本文实现并系统比较了近期提出的矩阵结构优化器（包括Muon、SOAP及混合SOAP-Muon），用于训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型。结果表明，这些优化器在收敛速度与最终精度上均显著优于Adam。SOAP与SOAP-Muon展现出稳健且持续强大的性能，而Muon仅对Adam提供部分提升。改进尤其在部分力监督条件下尤为显著。我们的研究指出，优化器选择是MLIPs中被忽视却至关重要的设计维度。

Motivated by the underexplored role of optimizers in training machine learning interatomic potentials, this study systematically evaluates SOAP, Muon, and their hybrid SOAP-Muon against Adam for NequIP and Allegro models. The experiments reveal that SOAP and SOAP-Muon significantly accelerate convergence and improve final accuracy compared to Adam, especially under partial force supervision, while Muon offers only marginal gains. These findings highlight optimizer selection as a critical yet neglected factor in MLIP development.
鉴于优化器在训练机器学习原子势中的作用长期被忽视，本研究系统比较了Muon、SOAP及SOAP-Muon与Adam在训练NequIP和Allegro模型中的表现。实验表明，SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其在部分力监督条件下效果更突出，而Muon仅带来小幅提升。结果表明，优化器选择是机器学习原子势发展中一个关键却常被忽略的设计维度。
Seek to Segment: Active Perception for Panoramic Referring Segmentation
Authors: Song Tang, Shuming Hu, Xincheng Shuai, Henghui Ding, Yu-Gang Jiang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:56:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:56:49+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026, Project Page: https://henghuiding.com/APRS/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing referring segmentation models passively process static images captured from fixed perspectives, limiting their applicability in Embodied AI, where agents must perform active perception in the continuous 360$^\circ$ environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task: Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS). In this setting, an agent is required to adjust its viewing direction ($Δθ, Δφ$) to explore the 360$^\circ$ environment, seeking the object specified by a user instruction for segmentation. To tackle this challenging task, we propose PanoSeeker, a memory-augmented agent for efficient APRS. Rather than relying on heuristic scanning, PanoSeeker integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with EgoSphere, an explicit spatial visual memory. By progressively integrating sequential local observations into a unified 360$^\circ$ representation, EgoSphere enables the agent to plan efficient and non-redundant search trajectories. Once the target is found, the agent performs active viewpoint alignment and outputs the segmentation mask. Furthermore, we curate an expert-annotated search trajectory dataset with memory timelines for Supervised Fine-Tuning, followed by Reinforcement Learning post-training to explicitly optimize PanoSeeker's exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments on our newly established APRS benchmark demonstrate that PanoSeeker achieves superior search efficiency and segmentation accuracy, significantly outperforming adapted state-of-the-art baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主动感知全景指代分割
现有指代分割模型被动处理从固定视角拍摄的静态图像，限制了其在具身AI中的适用性，后者要求代理在连续360°环境中进行主动感知。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一项新任务：主动全景指代分割（APRS）。在此设定下，代理需调整其观察方向（Δθ, Δφ）以探索360°环境，寻找用户指令指定的目标进行分割。为应对这一挑战，我们提出PanoSeeker，一种基于记忆增强的高效APRS代理。不同于依赖启发式扫描，PanoSeeker将视觉-语言模型（VLM）与EgoSphere（显式空间视觉记忆）相结合。通过逐步将序列局部观测整合为统一的360°表示，EgoSphere使代理能够规划高效且无冗余的搜索轨迹。目标找到后，代理执行主动视角对齐并输出分割掩码。此外，我们构建了一个专家标注的搜索轨迹数据集，包含记忆时间线，用于监督微调，随后通过强化学习进一步优化PanoSeeker的探索效率。在我们新建立的APRS基准测试中，大量实验表明PanoSeeker在搜索效率和分割精度上均显著优于适配的现有最先进基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of passive image processing in embodied AI, this work introduces Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS), where an agent must actively adjust its viewpoint to locate and segment objects in 360-degree environments. The proposed PanoSeeker agent combines a Vision-Language Model with EgoSphere, a spatial memory module that integrates sequential visual observations into a unified panoramic representation, enabling efficient, non-redundant search trajectories. After locating the target, the agent aligns its viewpoint and outputs a segmentation mask. Evaluated on a new APRS benchmark, PanoSeeker significantly outperforms existing baselines in both search efficiency and segmentation accuracy through supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.
为解决现有指代分割模型在具身智能中被动处理固定视角图像的局限性，本文提出主动全景指代分割（APRS）任务，要求代理通过调整视角（Δθ, Δφ）在360度环境中主动搜索并分割目标物体。所提出的PanoSeeker代理结合视觉-语言模型与EgoSphere空间记忆模块，将序列局部观测整合为统一全景表示，实现高效无冗余的搜索路径规划。目标定位后，代理对准视角并输出分割掩码。在新建立的APRS基准测试中，PanoSeeker在搜索效率和分割精度上显著优于现有基线模型，通过监督微调与强化学习实现性能优化。
Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents
Authors: Juanwu Lu, Junyu Zhu, Ziran Wang
First: 2026-07-02T17:55:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:55:39+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 5 tables, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有行为潜变量的可控仿真代理
现实的交通仿真需要能够模仿已记录行为并可沿可解释轴进行引导的代理。这种可控性使工程师能够隔离变量、重现特定边缘情况，并在无现实世界风险的情况下测试自动驾驶系统。我们提出了可控神经变分代理（CNeVA），这是一种可控仿真代理框架，通过闭式共轭变分更新，从每通道折扣回报中学习推断每个代理的高斯行为潜变量，并以混合通道掩码课程训练的修正流轨迹生成器为条件，实现无分类器引导。为应对奖励信号稀缺问题，我们提出软可及门限，用平滑指数衰减替代硬二元阈值，保留接近阈值代理的梯度信号。在Waymo开放运动数据集上，CNeVA在基准测试中达到竞争力的逼真度，同时具备高排名模仿模型所缺乏的每通道可控性。基于速度和加速度的引导产生单调响应，无因停滞导致的奖励作弊。引入软可及门限后，安全性可控性呈单调且显著。我们实现了在上下文残差回报度量下的可引导地图合规性。此外，我们的实验表明，引导指标必须与物理合理性约束结合使用，以避免奖励作弊的干扰。
Summary / 总结
To enable realistic and controllable traffic simulation, the paper proposes CNeVA, a framework that learns per-agent behavior latents from discounted returns using a closed-form variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum. To address sparse reward signals, it introduces soft eligibility gates that smooth binary thresholds, preserving gradients for near-threshold agents. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show CNeVA achieves competitive realism while offering per-channel controllability absent in top imitation models, with monotone steering responses in speed and acceleration without reward hacking, and substantial safety control via soft eligibility. The study also highlights that steering metrics must be paired with physical-plausibility constraints to avoid misleading performance signals.
为实现具备可解释控制能力的逼真交通仿真，作者提出CNeVA框架，通过闭式变分更新从折扣回报中学习每个代理的高斯行为潜变量，并以混合通道掩码课程训练的修正流轨迹生成器为条件。为缓解奖励信号稀疏问题，引入软可及门控机制，用平滑指数衰减替代硬二值阈值，保留近阈值代理的梯度信号。在Waymo开放运动数据集上，CNeVA在基准测试中表现竞争力，同时提供顶级模仿模型所缺乏的通道级可控性，速度与加速度控制响应单调无奖励作弊，软可及门控显著提升安全可控性。实验表明，仅靠转向指标不足以评估性能，必须结合物理合理性约束以避免误导性结果。
Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization
Authors: Bohan Liu, Wenqian Ye, Guangzhi Xiong, Zhenghao He, Sanchit Sinha, Aidong Zhang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:55:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:55:24+00:00
Comments: 15 pages main text, provisionally accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench. These results confirm both the efficacy and the generalizability of our mechanistic approach. Code is released at https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向无训练概念定位的抗排版攻击鲁棒性研究
通过对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）训练的模型是当前大多数大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的基础视觉编码器。尽管广泛应用，CLIP模型存在一个关键但尚未充分研究的失效模式：图像中出现无关文字会混淆视觉表征，使其偏向词义而非真实视觉语义。这种鲁棒性问题常被称为排版攻击（TA），暴露了对安全关键应用（如自动驾驶）构成重大风险的脆弱性。为实现可解释且有效的TA抗性，我们提出一种新颖的无训练机制可解释方法。该方法通过采样方式解释隐藏状态表征，并定量归因各注意力头的语义与词义侧重。通过概率分析与电路挖掘，我们识别出视觉Transformer（ViT）中过度编码词义信息的特定组件，从而定位TA的机制根源。进一步表明，仅对这些识别出的电路施加简单干预（无需额外训练），即可显著提升物体分类任务对排版攻击的鲁棒性。此类干预（如选择性调整注意力权重）亦优于监督与无训练防御方法。实验表明，将所提干预应用于多个SOTA LVLMs的视觉编码器后，在RIO-Bench数据集上面对排版攻击干扰时，视觉问答准确率显著提升。结果验证了该机制方法的有效性与泛化能力。代码已发布于https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of CLIP-based vision encoders to typographic attacks—where irrelevant text in images biases visual representations toward lexical meaning rather than true semantics—the authors propose a training-free method to localize and mitigate this issue. Using sampling-based interpretation and circuit mining, they identify specific Vision Transformer attention heads that disproportionately encode lexical information, revealing the mechanistic source of the attack. By applying targeted interventions such as selective attention weight adjustments to these identified circuits, they achieve substantial robustness improvements in object classification without retraining. Experiments on RIO-Bench show that these interventions significantly boost Visual Question Answering accuracy under typographic attack, outperforming both supervised and training-free defenses, demonstrating the method’s efficacy and generalizability.
为了解决CLIP视觉编码器在图像中出现无关文字时易受误导、偏向词义而非视觉语义的问题，本文提出一种无需训练的机制性可解释方法。该方法通过采样隐藏状态并量化各注意力头的语义与词义侧重，定位到负责编码无关文本的ViT组件。实验表明，对这些组件进行简单干预（如调整注意力权重）可显著提升模型在图文分类与视觉问答任务中对文字攻击的鲁棒性，且效果优于监督与无训练防御方法，无需额外训练即可实现泛化。
One-Shot Feed-Forward 360$^{\circ}$ Animatable Avatar via Inpainted UV-Space Gaussian Modeling
Authors: Shuling Zhao, Dan Xu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-01-19T06:56:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:54:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026. Project page: https://shaelynz.github.io/fhavatar/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Building one-shot 3D animatable head avatars is an important yet challenging problem. Existing methods generally collapse under large camera pose variations, compromising the realism of 3D avatars. In this work, we propose a new framework to tackle the novel setting of one-shot 3D full-head animatable avatar reconstruction in a single forward pass via inpainted UV-space Gaussian modeling, enabling 360$^\circ$ rendering views and real-time animation. To facilitate efficient animation control, we model 3D head avatars with Gaussian primitives embedded on the surface of a parametric face model within the UV space, and project the input image features to the UV space, resulting in incomplete local UV feature maps. To inpaint the missing regions, we obtain knowledge of full-head geometry and textures from rich 3D full-head priors within a pretrained 3D generative adversarial network (GAN) for global full-head feature extraction and multi-view supervision. Specifically, to enhance the fidelity of 3D reconstruction during inpainting, we take advantage of the symmetric nature of the UV space and human faces to fuse incomplete yet detailed local UV feature maps with the extracted global full-head textures, resulting in inpainted UV Gaussian attribute maps for avatar modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method is the first to achieve high-quality 3D full-head animatable avatar modeling, significantly improving side and back views while outperforming state-of-the-art animation approaches, thereby improving the realism of 3D animatable avatars.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过修复的UV空间高斯建模实现单次前向360°可动画头像
构建单次拍摄的3D可动画头像是一项重要且具有挑战性的问题。现有方法在大范围相机姿态变化下通常失效，损害3D头像的真实感。本文提出一种新框架，通过修复的UV空间高斯建模，在单次前向传递中解决单次3D全头可动画头像重建问题，支持360°渲染视角和实时动画。为实现高效动画控制，我们在参数化人脸模型表面嵌入高斯原语，将输入图像特征投影至UV空间，生成不完整的局部UV特征图。为修复缺失区域，我们利用预训练3D生成对抗网络（GAN）中丰富的3D全头先验知识，提取全局全头特征并进行多视角监督。具体而言，为提升修复期间3D重建保真度，我们利用UV空间和人脸的对称性，将不完整但细节丰富的局部UV特征图与提取的全局全头纹理融合，生成修复后的UV高斯属性图用于头像建模。大量实验表明，本方法首次实现高质量3D全头可动画头像建模，显著改善侧视图和后视图效果，超越现有最先进动画方法，提升3D可动画头像的真实感。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing realistic, animatable 3D head avatars from a single image under varying camera poses, this work introduces a one-shot feed-forward framework using inpainted UV-space Gaussian modeling. It embeds Gaussian primitives on a parametric face model in UV space, projects input image features into incomplete UV maps, and fills missing regions by leveraging global full-head priors from a pretrained 3D GAN for multi-view supervision. By exploiting UV symmetry and facial structure, it fuses local incomplete features with global textures to generate inpainted Gaussian attribute maps. Experimental results show it achieves the first high-fidelity 360° animatable avatar reconstruction, significantly improving side and back views and outperforming prior methods in realism and animation quality.
为解决单张图像下因相机姿态变化导致的3D可动画头像重建不真实问题，本文提出一种基于UV空间补全高斯建模的单次前馈框架。该方法将高斯元嵌入参数化人脸模型的UV空间，将输入图像特征投影为局部不完整UV图，再通过预训练3D GAN获取全局头部几何与纹理先验，结合UV空间对称性和人脸结构，融合局部细节与全局纹理完成补全，生成用于头像建模的高斯属性图。实验表明，该方法首次实现单图高质量360度可动画头像重建，显著提升侧后视图效果，且在真实感上优于现有动画方法。
G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models
Authors: Timo Bertram, Sidhant Bhavnani, Richard Freinschlag, Erich Kobler, Andreas Mayr, Günter Klambauer
First: 2026-07-02T17:53:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:53:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. % Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.
中文标题/摘要
标题：G-RRM：利用循环推理模型引导符号求解器
本研究聚焦于SE-RRM，这是RRM的一种符号等变实例化形式，其在更大问题规模下的外推性能更优。我们提出一种神经符号方法“利用循环推理模型引导”（G-RRM），将SE-RRM与约束满足问题的符号求解器结合。SE-RRM作为神经求解器，生成完整解提案并引导经典符号求解器（如回溯或基于SAT的方法，如Glucose 4.1和CaDiCaL 3.0.0），后者生成全局正确解。核心在于我们研究神经引导在何种条件下能提升符号求解器的搜索效率。实验表明，G-RRM的有效性依赖两个条件：一是问题实例需具有广阔的组合搜索空间以展现潜在收益；二是求解器架构必须能动态覆盖其分支选择，以在神经提示不完美时恢复。当条件满足时，引导使冲突计数中位数趋近于零，并带来显著的壁钟加速：在9×9数独中，SE-RRM正确解决91.1%的实例，回溯加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍（中位数，p<0.001），且在完美提示的25×25网格上Glucose 4.1仍保持1.17倍加速。相比之下，CaDiCaL 3.0.0因运行时开销主导且始终遵循注入的分支提示而不覆盖，无显著加速（中位数1.02倍，不显著），并在9×9网格上甚至出现小幅显著平均减速（0.90倍）。这些结果界定了神经引导转化为实际加速的适用场景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems, this work introduces G-RRM, a neuro-symbolic method that integrates symbol-equivariant recurrent reasoning models (SE-RRMs) with classical solvers like backtracking or SAT-based tools. SE-RRMs generate solution proposals to guide the search of symbolic solvers, which then produce globally correct results. Experiments reveal that G-RRM significantly improves solver efficiency only under two conditions: a large combinatorial search space and a solver capable of dynamically overwriting branching choices when neural hints are imperfect. On 9x9 Sudoku, backtracking saw a 33.3x speedup and Glucose 4.1 a 1.7x speedup (median, p<0.001), while CaDiCaL 3.0.0 showed no improvement and even a slowdown due to its inability to adapt to neural guidance.
为提升约束满足问题的符号求解器效率，本文提出G-RRM方法，利用符号等变循环推理模型（SE-RRM）引导传统求解器（如回溯或SAT求解器）。SE-RRM生成解提案以引导搜索，从而加速收敛。实验表明，G-RRM仅在问题具有大规模组合搜索空间且求解器能动态重写分支决策时才有效。在9x9数独上，回溯算法提速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1提速1.7倍（中位数，p<0.001）；而CaDiCaL 3.0.0因无法适应神经引导，未提速反而出现轻微减速。
Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Liyan Tang, Fangcong Yin, Greg Durrett
First: 2026-07-02T17:53:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:53:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过强化学习实现视觉语言模型的视觉锚定自我反思
大型视觉语言模型可通过生成文本链式思维（CoT）对多模态输入进行推理。CoT推理中的关键能力是自我反思：回顾早期决策并纠正先前错误。然而，现有LVLM在反思过程中往往无法正确关注视觉输入，限制了其将反馈转化为锚定修正的能力，尤其在分布外图像中表现明显。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种新颖的强化学习训练框架VRRL，包含两个专门设计的组件以激发视觉锚定自我反思。首先，在训练中随机遮蔽轨迹前缀，强调从错误中间预测中恢复而非早期犯错。其次，引入经验回放缓冲区中的缓冲重放，使模型接触多样化的失败状态并学习纠正。我们在涉及表格和图表的视觉锚定任务以及空间导航基准上评估该方法。尽管现成模型和常规微调模型在分布偏移下性能显著下降，我们的方法通过有效利用自我反思，显著优于标准RL和以反思为导向的微调基线，提升了分布外准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language models in grounding self-reflection on visual inputs, especially under distribution shift, this work introduces VRRL, a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly encourages visually grounded self-correction. It employs masked trajectory prefixes to force models to recover from earlier errors and buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose diverse failure states for correction. Experiments on visual grounding and spatial navigation tasks show that VRRL significantly outperforms standard RL and reflection-based fine-tuning baselines in out-of-distribution settings, demonstrating robustness through effective visual grounding during self-reflection.
为了解决视觉语言模型在自我反思过程中难以有效关联视觉输入的问题，尤其是在分布外图像上表现不佳，本文提出VRRL强化学习框架，通过掩码轨迹前缀迫使模型纠正早期错误，并引入经验回放缓冲区中的回滚样本以暴露多样化的失败状态供模型学习修正。实验表明，VRRL在视觉定位与空间导航任务上显著优于标准强化学习和传统反思微调方法，在分布外图像上仍能保持较高的准确率。
GeoMix: Descriptor-Free Visual Localization via Global Context and Multi-Detector Training
Authors: Yejun Zhang, Xinjue Wang, Zihan Wang, Esa Rahtu, Juho Kannala
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:52:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:52:41+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Descriptor-free visual localization eliminates high-dimensional descriptor storage, preserves scene privacy, and simplifies map maintenance, yet its accuracy still lags far behind descriptor-based pipelines. We identify this gap to insufficient geometric discriminability in geometry-only matching. Without visual appearance, current methods underutilize local geometry cues, lack the global context among keypoints, and overfit to a single keypoint detector. We further observe that descriptor-free matching naturally enables multi-detector training, as heterogeneous keypoints can be optimized in a shared geometry-only space without aligning descriptor spaces. Building on these insights, we propose GeoMix, a descriptor-free 2D-3D matching framework that strengthens geometric discriminability at three levels. Locally, directional and distance-aware embeddings enrich neighborhood aggregation with fine-grained spatial structure. Globally, learnable context nodes aggregate and redistribute scene-wide information via cross-attention to resolve ambiguities beyond local receptive fields. At the training level, Mix-Training exploits this detector-agnostic geometry space to learn representations across multiple keypoint detectors. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, Cambridge Landmarks, 7Scenes, and Aachen Day-Night show that GeoMix sets a new state of the art among descriptor-free methods, reducing 75th-percentile rotation error by 89\% and translation error by up to 90\% over the previous best, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen detectors and narrowing the gap to descriptor-based pipelines. Code is available at $\href{https://github.com/YejunZhang/Geomix}{\text{this links}}$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoMix：基于全局上下文与多检测器训练的无描述符视觉定位
无描述符视觉定位消除了高维描述符存储，保护了场景隐私并简化了地图维护，但其精度仍远落后于基于描述符的流程。我们发现这一差距源于仅几何匹配的几何判别能力不足。在缺乏视觉外观的情况下，现有方法未能充分利用局部几何线索，缺乏关键点间的全局上下文，且过度依赖单一关键点检测器。我们进一步观察到，无描述符匹配天然支持多检测器训练，因为异构关键点可在共享的仅几何空间中优化，无需对齐描述符空间。基于这些见解，我们提出GeoMix，一种无描述符的2D-3D匹配框架，从三个层面增强几何判别能力：局部上，方向与距离感知嵌入通过细粒度空间结构丰富邻域聚合；全局上，可学习上下文节点通过交叉注意力聚合并重分配场景全局信息，解决局部感受野外的歧义；训练层面，Mix-Training利用该检测器无关的几何空间，在多个关键点检测器间学习表示。在MegaDepth、Cambridge Landmarks、7Scenes和Aachen Day-Night上的大量实验表明，GeoMix在无描述符方法中达到新SOTA，将75百分位旋转误差降低89%，平移误差降低高达90%，并实现对未见检测器的零样本泛化，缩小与基于描述符流程的差距。代码见$\href{https://github.com/YejunZhang/Geomix}{\text{此链接}}$。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to eliminate descriptor storage while maintaining high localization accuracy, GeoMix addresses the limitations of current descriptor-free methods by enhancing geometric discriminability through three key innovations: local directional and distance-aware embeddings, global cross-attention context nodes for scene-wide information aggregation, and multi-detector training that leverages heterogeneous keypoints in a shared geometry space. Experiments on MegaDepth, Cambridge Landmarks, 7Scenes, and Aachen Day-Night demonstrate that GeoMix achieves state-of-the-art performance among descriptor-free approaches, reducing 75th-percentile rotation error by 89% and translation error by up to 90% over prior methods, while also generalizing zero-shot to unseen detectors.
为解决无描述符视觉定位中几何判别能力不足的问题，GeoMix通过三个层面提升几何判别性：局部方向与距离感知嵌入、全局跨注意力上下文节点聚合场景信息、以及在无描述符几何空间中进行多检测器联合训练。在MegaDepth、Cambridge Landmarks、7Scenes和Aachen Day-Night等数据集上的实验表明，GeoMix在无描述符方法中达到最先进性能，旋转误差中位数降低89%，平移误差最高降低90%，并能零样本泛化至未见检测器。
From Experiments to Expertise: Scientific Knowledge Consolidation for AI-Driven Computational Physics
Authors: Haonan Huang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-13T17:25:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:52:34+00:00
Comments: v2: camera-ready version, accepted at the ICML 2026 Workshop on AI for Physics (AI4Physics@ICML 2026). 20 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While large language models (LLMs) have transformed AI agents into proficient executors of computational materials science, performing a hundred simulations does not make a researcher. What distinguishes research from routine execution is the progressive accumulation of knowledge - learning which approaches fail, recognizing patterns across systems, and applying understanding to new problems. However, the prevailing paradigm in AI-driven computational science treats each execution in isolation, largely discarding hard-won insights between runs. Here we present QMatSuite, an open-source platform closing this gap. Agents record findings with full provenance, retrieve knowledge before new calculations, and in dedicated reflection sessions correct erroneous findings and synthesize observations into cross-compound patterns. In benchmarks on a six-step quantum-mechanical simulation workflow, accumulated knowledge reduces reasoning overhead by 67% and improves accuracy from 47% to 3% deviation from literature - and when transferred to an unfamiliar material, achieves 1% deviation with zero pipeline failures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从实验到专家：面向AI驱动计算物理的科学知识整合
尽管大语言模型（LLMs）已使AI代理成为计算材料科学的熟练执行者，但执行百次模拟并不能等同于成为研究人员。研究区别于常规执行的关键在于知识的逐步积累——学习哪些方法失败、识别跨系统的模式，并将理解应用于新问题。然而，当前AI驱动计算科学的主流范式将每次执行视为孤立事件，大多丢弃了运行间积累的宝贵经验。本文提出QMatSuite，一个开源平台弥合这一差距：代理记录带完整溯源的发现、在新计算前检索知识，并在专门的反思环节修正错误发现、将观察总结为跨化合物模式。在六步量子力学模拟工作流的基准测试中，积累的知识使推理开销降低67%，准确率从与文献偏差47%提升至3%；当应用于陌生材料时，偏差降至1%，且整个流程无任何失败。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for AI agents to evolve from mere executors to knowledgeable researchers in computational physics, this work introduces QMatSuite, an open-source platform that enables agents to systematically record, retrieve, and reflect on simulation outcomes. By embedding knowledge consolidation into the workflow—such as correcting errors and synthesizing cross-system patterns—the system reduces reasoning overhead by 67% and improves accuracy from 47% to 3% deviation from literature in a six-step quantum-mechanical workflow. When applied to an unfamiliar material, it achieves 1% deviation with zero pipeline failures, demonstrating robust generalization through learned expertise.
为使AI代理在计算物理中从任务执行者转变为具备知识积累能力的研究者，作者提出了开源平台QMatSuite，使代理能够记录、检索并反思模拟结果，保留完整溯源信息。通过专门的反思环节，代理纠正错误并提炼跨化合物模式，将孤立执行转化为持续学习过程。在六步量子力学模拟工作流基准测试中，该方法将推理开销降低67%，准确率从偏离文献47%提升至3%，应用于陌生材料时零流程失败，偏差仅为1%。
Combating Textual Noise and Redundancy: Entropy-Aware Dense Visual Token Pruning
Authors: Xuehui Wang, Xuankun Yang, Wei Shen
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:50:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:50:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual token pruning is a crucial strategy for accelerating VLMs by compressing redundant image patches, yet existing methods often fail to preserve critical cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries. In this paper, we investigate this failure and identify two underlying bottlenecks: the widespread dispersion of textual noise that corrupts dense cross-modal scoring, and the feature fragmentation inherent to standard token selection. To address these issues, we propose Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), a framework that reformulates pruning as a structured compression problem. EADP first leverages statistical entropy to quantify and filter out textual noise, yielding a robust, fine-grained instruction relevance score. Subsequently, instead of naive Top-K selection, EADP casts token selection as a submodular maximization problem with a spatial prior, explicitly ensuring a holistic and non-redundant visual representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, robustly preserving fine-grained visual cues under strict token budgets while achieving SoTA performance on challenging multimodal benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗文本噪声与冗余：基于熵感知的密集视觉令牌剪枝
视觉令牌剪枝是通过压缩冗余图像块加速视觉语言模型（VLMs）的关键策略，但现有方法在密集指令和细粒度查询下常无法保留关键线索。本文研究此问题，识别出两个根本瓶颈：文本噪声广泛分散，破坏密集跨模态评分；标准令牌选择固有的特征碎片化。为此，我们提出熵感知密集剪枝（EADP）框架，将剪枝重构为结构化压缩问题。EADP首先利用统计熵量化并过滤文本噪声，生成鲁棒的细粒度指令相关性评分；随后，摒弃朴素Top-K选择，将令牌选择建模为带空间先验的子模极大化问题，显式确保整体且无冗余的视觉表征。大量实验表明，EADP显著优化VLMs的精度-效率权衡，在严格令牌预算下稳健保留细粒度视觉线索，同时在挑战性多模态基准上达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of existing visual token pruning methods to preserve critical visual cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries, this paper introduces Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), which addresses two key bottlenecks: textual noise corrupting cross-modal scoring and fragmented token selection. EADP first uses statistical entropy to filter out noise and generate a robust relevance score, then formulates token selection as a submodular maximization problem with spatial constraints to ensure holistic, non-redundant visual representation. Experiments show EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, preserving fine-grained cues under strict token budgets and achieving state-of-the-art performance on multimodal benchmarks.
