Motivated by the need to understand how AI agent systems balance human control, safety, and capability, this study analyzes Claude Code’s architecture alongside two open-source agents, OpenClaw and Hermes Agent, to identify recurring design patterns. The research traces five core human values—decision authority, safety, security, reliable execution, capability amplification, and contextual adaptability—through thirteen design principles into concrete implementation choices, such as a permission system with seven modes, a five-layer context compaction pipeline, and four extensibility mechanisms. Experimental comparisons reveal that while all systems address similar design questions, they diverge significantly in deployment strategy: Claude Code prioritizes per-action safety with a CLI loop, OpenClaw enforces perimeter-level access via a gateway, and Hermes enables multi-surface approvals with a single process. 为理解AI代理系统如何平衡人类控制、安全与适应性，本研究通过源码分析与对比OpenClaw和Hermes Agent，剖析了Claude Code的架构。研究识别出五个核心人类价值——决策权、安全、隐私、可靠执行、能力增强与情境适应——并映射至十三项设计原则，核心为迭代调用模型与工具的while循环。系统复杂性体现在循环外的基础设施：七模式权限系统、五层上下文压缩管道、四种扩展机制、子代理调度与追加式会话存储。对比实验显示不同部署场景下设计选择各异：Claude Code强调单次操作安全，OpenClaw侧重边界访问控制，Hermes支持多界面审批。运行时实现方式不同——单CLI循环、嵌入网关控制平面或单进程角色切换——上下文与扩展层亦有差异：Claude Code扩展上下文窗口，OpenClaw全局注册能力，Hermes提供可插拔记忆与模型后端。研究最后提出六个未来代理系统的设计方向，基于近期实证、架构与政策研究。
Optimal Stabilizer Testing and Learning with Limited Quantum Memory
Authors: Srinivasan Arunachalam, Louis Schatzki
First: 2026-07-02T17:11:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:11:38+00:00
Comments: 66 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study stabilizer state testing and learning with limited coherent quantum memory. Here an algorithm sequentially receives copies of an unknown $n$-qubit state, but may keep only $k$ qubits of coherent quantum memory between measurements. With unrestricted memory, seminal work of Gross, Nezami and Walter showed how to test $n$-qubit stabilizer states using $6$ copies, which is dimension independent, unlike the learning complexity of $Θ(n)$. We show that this testing-vs-learning separation is lost under memory constraints. More concretely we show that (1) The sample complexity of testing stabilizer states in the $k$-qubit memory framework is $Θ(n-k)$. Our upper bound goes via a novel connection to the hidden shift problem and the lower bound is proven using a novel approach to average case bounds on likelihood ratios via combinatorics of the stochastic orthogonal group. (2) The sample complexity of learning stabilizer states with $k$ qubits of memory, in the non-adaptive framework, is $Θ(n^2/k)$. As a further application of our techniques, we prove an exponential lower bound for purity testing even when the memory may be left coherent throughout the protocol. Our main results identify coherent quantum memory as the resource enabling the usual separation between stabilizer testing and learning. In particular, even with $k=0.99n$ qubits of memory, there is no constant-copy stabilizer tester; furthermore for $k=cn$ qubits of memory (for $0< c < 1$), stabilizer testing is as hard as learning, with both requiring $Θ(n)$ copies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有限量子内存下的最优稳定子态测试与学习
我们研究在有限相干量子内存约束下稳定子态的测试与学习问题。在此框架中，算法依次接收未知的n量子比特态的副本，但每次测量之间最多仅能保持k个量子比特的相干量子内存。在无内存限制情况下，Gross、Nezami和Walter的开创性工作表明，使用6个副本即可测试n量子比特稳定子态，该复杂度与维度无关，而学习复杂度为Θ(n)。我们证明，在内存受限条件下，这种测试与学习的分离性消失。具体而言，我们证明：(1) 在k量子比特内存框架下，测试稳定子态的样本复杂度为Θ(n-k)。上界通过与隐藏移位问题的新联系获得，下界则通过利用随机正交群的组合学方法证明了似然比的平均情况界。(2) 在非自适应框架下，使用k量子比特内存学习稳定子态的样本复杂度为Θ(n²/k)。作为我们技术的进一步应用，我们证明了即使在协议过程中内存始终保持相干，纯度测试仍存在指数级下界。我们的主要结果表明，相干量子内存是实现稳定子态测试与学习通常分离性的关键资源。特别是，即使拥有k=0.99n量子比特内存，也不存在常数副本的稳定子态测试器；对于k=cn量子比特内存（0<c<1），稳定子态测试与学习具有相同的难度，两者均需Θ(n)个副本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of how quantum memory constraints affect the complexity of stabilizer state testing versus learning, this work investigates algorithms that sequentially receive copies of an unknown n-qubit state but can retain only k qubits of coherent memory between measurements. The authors demonstrate that under such memory limitations, the previously known separation between testing (requiring only 6 copies) and learning (requiring Θ(n) copies) collapses: testing requires Θ(n−k) samples, and non-adaptive learning requires Θ(n²/k) samples. They establish these bounds using novel connections to the hidden shift problem and combinatorial methods for likelihood ratios. Additionally, they prove an exponential lower bound for purity testing even with coherent memory preserved throughout, showing that coherent memory is essential for the testing-learning separation, and that even with nearly full memory (k=0.99n), testing still requires Θ(n) samples, matching learning complexity.
该研究旨在探讨有限相干量子内存对稳定子态测试与学习复杂度的影响。作者证明，在仅能保留k个量子比特内存的情况下，稳定子态测试需Θ(n−k)个样本，而非适应性学习则需Θ(n²/k)个样本，揭示了在内存受限时测试与学习之间的经典分离消失。测试上界通过与隐藏移位问题的新联系获得，下界则利用随机正交群的组合方法证明。此外，研究还证明即使保持内存相干，纯度测试仍存在指数级下界，确认相干量子内存是维持测试与学习分离的关键资源。
EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments
Authors: Zhilin Wang, Han Song, Runzhe Zhan, Jusen Du, Jiacheng Chen, Tianle Li, Qingyu Yin, Yulun Wu, Zhennan Shen, Tong Zhu, Yanshu Li, Guanjie Chen, Derek F. Wong, Yafu Li, Yu Cheng, Yang Yang
First: 2026-07-02T17:10:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:10:13+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoPolicyGym：在交互环境中评估自主策略演化
自主代理日益被期望通过反馈改进可执行策略，但现有评估常将其过程简化为最终得分，或与开放式软件工程进展混淆。我们引入自主策略演化，这是一种受控评估设置，其中承载模型代理在固定交互预算下反复编辑可执行策略系统。我们通过EvoPolicyGym基准实现该设置，该基准由紧凑的交互式强化学习环境构建，用于评估代理如何迭代改进探索到的策略。在EvoPolicyGym套件中，GPT-5.5获得最强综合排名分，并在全部16个环境中均取得前两名表现。除排行榜结果外，EvoPolicyGym还提供轨迹级诊断，区分代理如何分配预算、将反馈转化为参数调优。这些分析表明，强大的自主策略演化不仅依赖于孤立任务的胜利，更依赖于发现任务适配机制并在有限反馈下优化策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how autonomous agents iteratively improve policies through feedback without conflating progress with final scores, the paper introduces EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark that enforces a fixed interaction budget while allowing agents to repeatedly edit executable policies. Using compact interactive RL environments, the study assesses agents' ability to evolve policies over time. Experimental results show that GPT-5.5 achieves the highest aggregate rank and top-two performance across all 16 environments, while trajectory-level diagnostics reveal that successful policy evolution hinges on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and efficiently refining policies under constrained feedback.
为评估自主代理如何在有限反馈下迭代优化策略而不混淆过程与最终得分，作者提出了EvoPolicyGym基准，强制代理在固定交互预算内反复编辑可执行策略。通过紧凑的交互式强化学习环境，评估代理随时间优化策略的能力。实验表明，GPT-5.5在所有16个环境中综合排名最高，且在每个环境中均位列前二；轨迹级诊断显示，成功关键在于发现任务适配机制并在有限反馈下高效优化策略。
BLAgent: Agentic RAG for File-Level Bug Localization
Authors: Md Afif Al Mamun, Gias Uddin
First: 2026-05-18T07:20:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:09:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in ACM Transactions on Software Engineering and Methodology (TOSEM)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bug localization remains a key bottleneck for large language model (LLM)-based software maintenance, where accurately identifying faulty code is essential for debugging, root cause analysis, triage, and automated program repair (APR). File-level bug localization is especially critical in hierarchical localization and repair pipelines, where incorrect file selection can propagate to downstream stages such as function-level localization and patch generation. While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offers a promising way to ground LLMs in repository context, existing RAG pipelines often rely on static retrieval and lack the reasoning needed to accurately identify faulty code. In this work, we present BLAgent, a novel agentic RAG framework for file-level bug localization that integrates three key ideas: (i) code structure-aware repository encoding with path-augmented AST-based chunking, (ii) dual-perspective query transformation that captures both structural and behavioral signals from bug reports, and (iii) two-phase agentic reranking that combines symbolic inspection with evidence-grounded reasoning. Unlike prior graph-based or multi-hop agentic approaches, BLAgent adopts a bounded reasoning strategy that limits LLM-based inspection and reranking to a compact, retrieval-filtered set of candidate files, avoiding open-ended repository traversal. This design balances localization accuracy with computational cost. On SWE-bench-Lite, BLAgent attains over 78% Top-1 accuracy with open-source models and over 86% with a closed-source model, while being over 18x cheaper than the strongest baseline using the same model. When integrated into an APR framework, BLAgent improves end-to-end repair success by up to 25%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BLAgent：面向文件级缺陷定位的代理式RAG框架
缺陷定位仍是基于大语言模型（LLM）的软件维护中的关键瓶颈，准确识别故障代码对调试、根因分析、问题分类和自动化程序修复（APR）至关重要。在分层定位与修复流程中，文件级缺陷定位尤为关键，错误的文件选择可能影响后续阶段如函数级定位和补丁生成。尽管检索增强生成（RAG）为将LLM锚定在仓库上下文提供了有前景的方案，但现有RAG流程常依赖静态检索，缺乏准确识别故障代码所需的推理能力。本文提出BLAgent，一种面向文件级缺陷定位的新型代理式RAG框架，融合三大核心思想：（i）路径增强的AST基块划分的代码结构感知仓库编码；（ii）双视角查询变换，捕捉缺陷报告中的结构与行为信号；（iii）两阶段代理重排序，结合符号检查与证据锚定推理。与先前基于图或多跳代理方法不同，BLAgent采用有界推理策略，将LLM的检查与重排序限制在紧凑的、检索过滤后的候选文件集合内，避免无限制的仓库遍历。该设计在定位精度与计算成本间取得平衡。在SWE-bench-Lite上，BLAgent使用开源模型时Top-1准确率超过78%，使用闭源模型时超过86%，且计算成本比最强基线低18倍以上。当集成至APR框架时，BLAgent可使端到端修复成功率提升最高25%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately localize bugs at the file level for efficient software maintenance, BLAgent introduces an agentic RAG framework that enhances LLM-based bug localization by integrating code structure-aware repository encoding, dual-perspective query transformation, and two-phase agentic reranking. The method uses path-augmented AST chunking to encode code, transforms queries to capture structural and behavioral signals, and applies bounded reasoning over retrieval-filtered candidate files to avoid costly open-ended traversal. Experiments on SWE-bench-Lite show BLAgent achieves over 78% Top-1 accuracy with open-source models and over 86% with closed-source models, while being 18x more cost-efficient than the best baseline. When integrated into an APR pipeline, it boosts end-to-end repair success by up to 25%.
为解决大型语言模型在软件维护中精准定位文件级故障代码的瓶颈问题，BLAgent提出一种新型代理式RAG框架，通过路径增强的AST分块编码代码结构、双视角查询变换捕捉缺陷报告中的结构与行为信号、以及两阶段代理重排序机制结合符号检查与证据推理，提升定位准确性。其设计限制LLM仅在检索过滤后的候选文件集上进行推理，避免全库遍历，兼顾精度与效率。在SWE-bench-Lite上，该方法使用开源模型达到78%以上Top-1准确率，闭源模型达86%，成本比最优基线低18倍；集成至自动修复框架后，修复成功率提升最高达25%。
Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Sanjeev Shrestha, Hui Liu, Yifan Zhang
First: 2026-07-02T17:09:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:09:14+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Scientific Reports
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于时间序列预测的极值自适应变换器
当底层数据包含罕见但关键的极端事件时，时间序列预测仍具挑战性。这一问题在水文预测中尤为重要，因为径流分布常高度偏斜，极端峰值对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统有重大影响。尽管基于Transformer的预测模型通过建模长程时序依赖关系取得了优异性能，但它们通常对所有时间点一视同仁，可能低估罕见极端模式。本文提出极值自适应变换器（Exformer），一种旨在显式建模正常与极端事件间时序依赖关系的预测框架。Exformer引入由三部分稀疏组件构成的极值自适应注意力机制：局部、步进和极值。局部与步进组件分别捕捉短期和周期性时序依赖，而极值组件选择性地建模正常与极端径流模式间的事件感知依赖。在四个真实水文径流数据集上的实验表明，Exformer在3日预测性能上优于当前最先进基线模型。我们的研究结果表明，显式引入极值感知注意力可提升Transformer模型在包含罕见但重要事件的不平衡时间序列上的预测能力。

The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic dependencies, while the Extreme component selectively models dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic datasets demonstrate that Exformer outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in 3-day forecasting accuracy, confirming that explicitly modeling extreme events improves forecasting performance on imbalanced time series.
为应对在偏斜水文时序数据中罕见但关键的极端事件预测难题，本文提出极端自适应Transformer（Exformer），通过引入极端自适应注意力机制增强标准Transformer的能力。该机制包含三个稀疏组件：Local用于捕捉短期依赖，Stride用于周期性模式，Extreme则专门建模正常与极端流量事件间的事件感知依赖。在四个真实水文数据集上的实验表明，Exformer在3日预测任务中显著优于现有最先进基线模型，验证了在不平衡时序数据中显式建模极端事件可有效提升预测性能。
Optimizing RAG Rerankers with LLM Feedback via Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yuhang Wu, Xiangqing Shen, Fanfan Wang, Cangqi Zhou, Zhen Wu, Xinyu Dai, Rui Xia
First: 2026-04-02T14:19:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:08:51+00:00
Comments: 17 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rerankers play a pivotal role in refining retrieval results for Retrieval-Augmented Generation. However, current reranking models are typically optimized on static human annotated relevance labels in isolation, decoupled from the downstream generation process. This isolation leads to a fundamental misalignment: documents identified as topically relevant by information retrieval metrics often fail to provide the actual utility required by the LLM for precise answer generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReRanking Preference Optimization (RRPO), a reinforcement learning framework that directly aligns reranking with the LLM's generation quality. By formulating reranking as a sequential decision-making process, RRPO optimizes for context utility using LLM feedback, thereby eliminating the need for expensive human annotations. To ensure training stability, we further introduce a reference-anchored deterministic baseline. Extensive experiments on knowledge-intensive benchmarks demonstrate that RRPO significantly outperforms strong baselines, including the powerful list-wise reranker RankZephyr. Further analysis highlights the versatility of our framework: it generalizes seamlessly to diverse readers (e.g., GPT-4o), integrates orthogonally with query expansion modules like Query2Doc, and remains robust even when trained with noisy supervisors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过强化学习利用大语言模型反馈优化RAG重排序器
重排序器在检索增强生成的检索结果精炼中起着关键作用。然而，当前的重排序模型通常在孤立的静态人工标注相关性标签上进行优化，与下游生成过程脱节。这种脱节导致根本性错位：信息检索指标识别为主题相关的文档，往往无法满足大语言模型生成精确答案所需的实际效用。为弥合这一差距，我们引入了重排序偏好优化（RRPO），一种强化学习框架，直接将重排序与大语言模型的生成质量对齐。通过将重排序建模为序列决策过程，RRPO利用大语言模型反馈优化上下文效用，从而无需昂贵的人工标注。为确保训练稳定性，我们进一步引入了参考锚定的确定性基线。在知识密集型基准上的大量实验表明，RRPO显著优于强基线，包括强大的列表式重排序器RankZephyr。进一步分析突显了我们框架的通用性：它能无缝泛化至多样化的读者（如GPT-4o），可与查询扩展模块（如Query2Doc）正交集成，并在使用噪声监督者训练时仍保持鲁棒性。

Motivated by the misalignment between traditional reranking models and LLM generation needs, this work proposes RRPO, a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes rerankers using LLM feedback to directly improve generation quality. Instead of relying on static human annotations, RRPO treats reranking as a sequential decision process and uses LLM-generated context utility scores as rewards, stabilized by a reference-anchored baseline. Experiments on knowledge-intensive benchmarks show RRPO significantly outperforms strong baselines like RankZephyr and demonstrates robustness across diverse LLMs, query expansion modules, and noisy supervision.
为解决传统重排序模型与大语言模型生成需求之间的脱节问题，本文提出RRPO框架，通过强化学习利用大语言模型反馈直接优化重排序效果，使其更贴合生成质量。该方法将重排序建模为序列决策过程，以大模型生成的上下文效用作为奖励信号，并引入参考锚定确定性基线保证训练稳定。在知识密集型基准测试中，RRPO显著优于RankZephyr等强基线模型，且在不同大模型、查询扩展模块及噪声监督条件下均表现鲁棒。
Evaluating Collective Behaviour of Hundreds of LLM Agents
Authors: Richard Willis, Jianing Zhao, Yali Du, Joel Z. Leibo
First: 2026-02-18T18:02:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:08:21+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-powered AI assistants acting on behalf of users can produce poor collective outcomes at scale. We introduce a framework for evaluating their emergent behaviour in social dilemmas, applied to three iterated games (Public Goods, Collective Risk, Common Pool Resource). We prompt each model to produce a natural-language strategy, then have the same model translate it into code. This aims to isolate strategic reasoning from input-parsing, enables pre-deployment inspection, and scales to populations of hundreds of agents. We propose three analyses: behavioural fingerprinting via exhaustive evaluation over opponent histories; self-play robustness across mixtures of a model's strategies with either a Selfish or Collective disposition; and cultural evolution under payoff-biased imitation. Applied to three state-of-the-art LLMs, we find substantial cross-model differences in self-play welfare, and that cultural evolution converges to low-welfare, Selfish-dominant equilibria in larger groups.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估数百个大语言模型代理的集体行为
由大语言模型驱动的AI助手在大规模代理下可能产生较差的集体结果。我们提出一个框架，用于评估其在社会困境中的涌现行为，应用于三类重复博弈（公共物品、集体风险、公共资源）。我们提示每个模型生成自然语言策略，再由同一模型将其翻译为代码，旨在将战略推理与输入解析分离，支持部署前检查，并可扩展至数百个代理的群体。我们提出三项分析：通过穷举对手历史进行行为指纹识别；在模型策略与自私或集体倾向混合下的自对弈稳健性；以及在收益偏倚模仿下的文化演化。对三类最先进的大语言模型应用后，我们发现模型间自对弈福利存在显著差异，且在更大群体中文化演化收敛于低福利、以自私为主导的均衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of poor collective outcomes when deploying LLM agents at scale, this study introduces a framework to evaluate emergent behavior in social dilemmas using three iterated games. It prompts each LLM to generate a natural-language strategy and then translates it into executable code to isolate strategic reasoning from parsing artifacts, enabling pre-deployment inspection and scalability to hundreds of agents. Three analyses—behavioral fingerprinting, self-play robustness against selfish/collective mixtures, and cultural evolution under payoff-biased imitation—are applied to three state-of-the-art LLMs, revealing significant cross-model differences in self-play welfare and convergence to low-welfare, selfish-dominant equilibria in larger populations.
鉴于大量LLM代理协同工作时可能产生不良集体结果，本研究提出一套评估框架，用于在三个迭代博弈（公共物品、集体风险、公共资源）中分析其涌现行为。每个模型被提示生成自然语言策略，再由同一模型将其转化为可执行代码，以分离策略推理与输入解析，支持部署前检查并扩展至百个代理规模。通过行为指纹分析、自博弈稳健性测试（与自私或集体策略混合）及基于收益的模仿文化演化三类分析，对三类顶尖LLM进行评估，发现模型间自博弈福利差异显著，且在群体规模增大时文化演化趋向于低福利、自私主导的均衡。
Reasoning effort, not tool access, buys first-try reliability in agentic code generation: an observational study
Authors: Achint Mehta
First: 2026-07-02T17:08:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:08:21+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 5 figures, 10 tables. Dataset and evaluation artifacts: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21134406
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic coding assistants are increasingly given extra capabilities, such as browser based testing tools and design oriented system prompts, on the assumption that more capability yields better software. This study tested that assumption directly. Ninety independent agent runs built the same application, a real time retrospective board, from one detailed specification, each scored on a fixed 14 criterion functional rubric (42 point maximum) and a visual quality review. The runs spanned several model generations, two agent harnesses, two reasoning effort levels, a testing tool, and two design oriented prompts. Capability tier dominated: frontier models clustered near the ceiling while a low cost local model fell to 24 to 37 points. A criterion level analysis revealed what run totals conceal. Container deployment was the dominant defect, failing first try in 44 percent of runs, with its failure rate shifting sharply across model generations while mean totals moved less than a point. The testing tool raised cost by 42 to 68 percent without improving functional score or reliability, even on interface visible criteria. Raising reasoning effort from High to xHigh lifted first try perfect runs from 28 percent to 89 percent and cut corrective prompts about five fold, for 9 to 29 percent more cost. A design oriented prompt raised visual quality, 4.5 versus 3.0 on a 5 point scale, without lifting function, and a one paragraph paraphrase of its directive reproduced the entire lift. The practical lesson is to match the fix to the failure: most first run failures came from weak reasoning, which a stronger model or more effort prevents, not from visible flaws a checking tool would catch.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理努力而非工具访问，是代理代码生成首次尝试可靠性的关键：一项观察性研究
随着代理编码助手被赋予更多能力（如基于浏览器的测试工具和面向设计的系统提示），人们普遍假设能力越强，软件质量越高。本研究直接检验了这一假设。90次独立代理运行基于同一详细规格构建了同一个实时回顾板应用，每项运行均按固定14项功能评分标准（满分42分）和视觉质量评审进行评分。运行覆盖多个模型版本、两种代理框架、两种推理努力水平、一个测试工具和两种面向设计的提示。能力层级主导结果：前沿模型得分接近上限，而低成本本地模型得分仅24至37分。按评分标准的分析揭示了总分掩盖的细节：容器部署是主要缺陷，首次尝试失败率达44%，其失败率在不同模型版本间显著变化，而平均总分仅变化不到1分。测试工具使成本上升42%至68%，但未提升功能得分或可靠性，即使在界面可见性标准上亦然。将推理努力从高提升至x高，首次尝试完美运行比例从28%升至89%，纠错提示减少五倍，成本增加9%至29%。面向设计的提示提升了视觉质量（5分制下从3.0升至4.5），但未提升功能，且其指令的一段改写即可复现全部提升效果。实用启示是：应针对失败类型采取相应修复——多数首次运行失败源于推理能力不足，可通过更强模型或更多推理努力解决，而非由检查工具能发现的可见缺陷。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether enhanced capabilities like testing tools or design prompts improve reliability in agentic code generation, finding that reasoning effort—not tool access—drives first-try success. Across 90 agent runs building a real-time retrospective board, frontier models scored near the 42-point ceiling while a low-cost local model scored 24–37 points. A detailed criterion analysis revealed container deployment as the dominant failure mode, failing 44% of the time, with model generation affecting failure rates more than overall scores. Adding a testing tool increased cost without improving functionality or reliability, while increasing reasoning effort from High to xHigh boosted perfect first-run completion from 28% to 89% and reduced corrective prompts by fivefold at a modest cost increase. A design prompt improved visual quality from 3.0 to 4.5 without affecting function, and its effect was replicated by paraphrasing its directive. The key takeaway is that most failures stem from weak reasoning, which stronger models or increased effort mitigate, not from visible flaws that tools detect.
本研究检验增强能力（如测试工具和设计提示）是否能提升代理代码生成的可靠性，发现推理强度而非工具访问才是首次成功的关键。90次独立代理运行构建实时回顾板，前沿模型得分接近42分天花板，而低成本本地模型仅得24–37分，显示能力层级主导效果。细粒度标准分析表明容器部署是主要失败源，首次失败率达44%，模型版本影响远大于总分变化。添加测试工具虽增加成本但未提升功能可靠性或视觉质量。将推理强度从高提升至x高，首次完美完成率从28%升至89%，纠错提示减少五倍，成本增加9–29%。设计提示将视觉质量从3.0提升至4.5，但不影响功能，且其效果可通过重述指令复现。核心结论：应针对推理缺陷而非可见缺陷进行修复，以提升首次运行成功率。
PE-means: Improved Differentially Private $k$-means Clustering through Private Evolution
Authors: Thomas Humphries, Zinan Lin, Sergey Yekhanin
First: 2026-05-29T20:30:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:06:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of differentially private (DP) $k$-means clustering in Euclidean space. Previous solutions rely on summing the private data directly, which induces a sensitivity proportional to the domain. We introduce PE-means, an extension of the private evolution (PE) algorithm (an increasingly popular method for synthetic data generation), to the problem of $k$-means clustering. The key advantage of PE is that it only computes a private histogram with constant sensitivity to guide the evolution. Our adaptation of PE includes new evolutionary operators for clustering, as well as other algorithmic improvements of independent interest. Overall, PE-means achieves an average improvement of 26% in clustering loss over state-of-the-art baselines such as Google's LSH-based algorithm and DP-Lloyd variants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PE-means：通过私有演化改进差分隐私k均值聚类
我们研究欧几里得空间中差分隐私（DP）k均值聚类问题。先前的解决方案依赖直接对私有数据求和，导致敏感度与域成正比。我们引入PE-means，将私有演化（PE）算法（一种日益流行的合成数据生成方法）扩展至k均值聚类问题。PE的核心优势在于仅计算具有常数敏感度的私有直方图以指导演化。我们的PE适配包括为聚类设计的新演化算子及其他独立的算法改进。总体而言，PE-means在聚类损失上平均优于谷歌基于LSH的算法及DP-Lloyd变体等现有最先进基线方法26%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high sensitivity of existing differentially private k-means methods that scale with domain size, this work introduces PE-means, an adaptation of the private evolution (PE) algorithm for clustering. PE-means leverages a private histogram with constant sensitivity to guide the evolution of cluster centers, avoiding direct summation of private data. It incorporates novel evolutionary operators and algorithmic refinements tailored for clustering. Experimental results show PE-means achieves an average 26% reduction in clustering loss compared to state-of-the-art baselines including Google’s LSH-based method and DP-Lloyd variants.
为解决现有差分隐私k均值聚类方法因直接求和导致敏感度随域大小增长的问题，本文提出PE-means，将私有演化（PE）算法扩展至聚类任务。PE-means通过常数敏感度的私有直方图引导聚类中心演化，避免直接处理私有数据。该方法引入专为聚类设计的演化算子及其他算法改进。实验表明，PE-means相较谷歌LSH算法及DP-Lloyd等当前最优方法平均降低26%的聚类损失。
MARVEL: Margin-Aware Robust von Mises-Fischer Expert Learning for Long-Tailed Out-of-Distribution Detection
Authors: A. S. Anudeep, Vaanathi Sundaresan
First: 2026-07-02T17:06:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:06:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
For clinical deployment, it is essential that automated diagnostic systems remain reliable when confronted with previously unseen cases, yet deep models routinely misclassify out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs with high confidence, underscoring the need for more robust OOD detection methods. Although substantial effort has been devoted to improving model robustness, most of the existing literature assumes balanced datasets, evaluates OOD detection on coarse or non-clinical OOD sources, or lacks comprehensive assessment across diverse OOD scenarios. To address the gaps, we propose a novel methodology trained on diverse and imbalanced medical datasets and evaluated across a clinically reflective OOD spectrum. Our framework comprises three key components: (1) a Nonlinear von Mises-Fisher (NvMF) classifier capable of learning non-linear decision boundaries, with theoretical proof of its asymptotic connection to cosine classifiers; (2) a multi-expert framework in which margin-aware NvMF classifiers specialise in different regions of label distribution to better handle imbalance; and (3) an outlier expert trained explicitly to distinguish inlier from outlier data, thereby strengthening OOD detection. Evaluation on RFMiD, ISIC2019, and NCTCRC datasets demonstrates consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving mean FPR95 reductions of 8.45%, 13.02%, and 36.90% respectively. These gains are further supported by comprehensive ablations that validated the contributions of each component. This enables reliable identification of unfamiliar cases for deferral to clinicians, supporting safer AI-assisted diagnosis in real-world workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/redboxup/MARVEL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MARVEL：面向长尾分布外检测的边际感知鲁棒冯·米塞斯-费舍尔专家学习
为实现临床部署，自动化诊断系统在面对前所未见病例时必须保持可靠性，但深度模型常以高置信度错误分类分布外（OOD）输入，凸显了对更鲁棒的OOD检测方法的需求。尽管已有大量研究致力于提升模型鲁棒性，但多数现有文献假设数据集平衡、在粗略或非临床的OOD源上评估OOD检测，或缺乏对多样OOD场景的全面评估。为弥补这些不足，我们提出一种在多样且不平衡的医学数据集上训练、并在临床相关OOD谱上评估的新方法。该框架包含三个关键组件：（1）非线性冯·米塞斯-费舍尔（NvMF）分类器，可学习非线性决策边界，并有理论证明其渐近等价于余弦分类器；（2）多专家框架，其中边际感知NvMF分类器专注于标签分布的不同区域，以更好地处理不平衡问题；（3）专门训练的异常值专家，用于区分内点与异常点，从而增强OOD检测能力。在RFMiD、ISIC2019和NCTCRC数据集上的评估显示，该方法相较现有最先进方法持续提升，分别实现平均FPR95降低8.45%、13.02%和36.90%。全面消融实验进一步验证了各组件的贡献。这使得系统能够可靠识别陌生病例并转交临床医生，支持真实世界工作流中更安全的AI辅助诊断。代码开源于https://github.com/redboxup/MARVEL。
Summary / 总结
To enhance reliability of automated diagnostic systems in clinical settings when encountering unseen cases, the paper proposes MARVEL, a margin-aware robust OOD detection framework trained on imbalanced medical datasets and evaluated across clinically relevant OOD scenarios. The method combines a nonlinear von Mises-Fisher classifier with theoretical grounding in cosine classifiers, a multi-expert architecture that assigns margin-aware NvMF classifiers to specialize in different label regions to handle imbalance, and an outlier expert explicitly trained to distinguish inliers from outliers. Evaluated on RFMiD, ISIC2019, and NCTCRC, MARVEL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing mean FPR95 by 8.45%, 13.02%, and 36.90% respectively, with ablation studies confirming the individual contributions of each component, enabling safer deferral of uncertain cases to clinicians.
为提升深度模型在临床部署中对未知病例的可靠识别能力，本文提出MARVEL框架，旨在解决现有模型常高置信度误判分布外输入的问题。该框架包含三个核心模块：非线性von Mises-Fisher分类器、基于边距感知的多专家系统以适应标签分布不平衡，以及专门训练用于区分内点与外点的异常专家。在RFMiD、ISIC2019和NCTCRC三个医学数据集上，MARVEL相较现有方法平均FPR95分别降低8.45%、13.02%和36.90%，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性，为AI辅助诊断在真实临床场景中的安全应用提供支持。
Automated grading of Linux/bash examinations using large language models: a four-level cognitive taxonomy approach
Authors: Manuel Alonso-Carracedo, Ruben Fernandez-Boullon, Pedro Celard, Francisco J. Rodriguez-Martinez, Lorena Otero-Cerdeira
First: 2026-07-02T17:01:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:01:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scalable and reliable grading of command-line examinations remains a challenge in computing education, where rising enrolments make manual marking difficult and rule-based autograders cannot handle partial credit, equivalent solutions, or syntactic variation. This paper evaluates whether four frontier Large Language Models (GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM) can approximate expert judgment when grading short Linux/bash command responses. The study adopts a four-level cognitive taxonomy that combines cognitive complexity and operational impact, ranging from information retrieval (L1) and basic file manipulation (L2) to structural operations (L3) and advanced system management (L4). The models were tested with two prompt variants, a minimal baseline and a rubric-enhanced version, on 1200 real responses from second-year Computer Engineering students independently graded by three expert instructors. Gemini~3.0 Pro with rubric-guided prompting achieved the highest human-AI agreement (ICC(3,1) = 0.888, MAE = 0.10, Bland-Altman bias = -0.014). Agreement declined consistently as taxonomy level increased, with the largest discrepancies at higher levels. Across all models, rubric quality had a larger effect than provider choice, with structured prompts consistently improving agreement. These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型按四层次认知分类法自动批改Linux/bash考试
命令行考试的可扩展且可靠的自动评分在计算机教育中仍具挑战性，因学生人数激增使人工批改困难，基于规则的自动评分器无法处理部分得分、等效解法或语法变体。本文评估了四款前沿大语言模型（GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini 和 GLM）在批改简短Linux/bash命令响应时是否能近似专家判断。研究采用结合认知复杂度与操作影响的四层次认知分类法，从信息检索（L1）和基础文件操作（L2）到结构化操作（L3）和高级系统管理（L4）。模型在1200份由三位专家独立评分的真实学生答卷上，测试了两种提示变体（最小基线与评分标准增强版）。Gemini 3.0 Pro配合评分标准引导提示达成最高的人机一致性（ICC(3,1) = 0.888，MAE = 0.10，Bland-Altman偏差 = -0.014）。一致性随分类层级升高而持续下降，高阶层级差异最大。所有模型中，评分标准质量的影响大于供应商选择，结构化提示始终提升一致性。结果表明，题目复杂度是预测LLM准确评分难度的可靠指标，同时建立了基于分类法的框架，用于判断哪些题目适合AI辅助评分、哪些需人工复核，并提供可迁移的评估协议与提示模板。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of scaling reliable grading for Linux/bash exams due to manual effort and limitations of rule-based systems, this study evaluates four large language models—GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM—using a four-level cognitive taxonomy to assess command complexity and operational impact. The models were tested with two prompt variants on 1200 real student responses, independently graded by three experts. Gemini 3.0 Pro with rubric-enhanced prompting achieved the highest agreement with human graders (ICC=0.888, MAE=0.10), while agreement decreased with higher taxonomy levels. Rubric quality consistently outperformed model provider choice, demonstrating that question complexity is a key factor in LLM grading accuracy and offering a taxonomy-based framework to guide AI-assisted grading.
为应对Linux/bash考试因学生人数激增导致的人工评分困难及规则系统无法处理部分得分和语义等价等问题，本研究评估了GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini和GLM四个大语言模型，采用四层认知分类法衡量认知复杂度与操作影响。在1200份真实学生答案上测试两种提示变体，由三位专家独立评分。结果显示，使用评分标准提示的Gemini 3.0 Pro与人类评分者一致性最高（ICC=0.888，MAE=0.10），且一致性随分类层级升高而下降。评分标准质量比模型提供商选择更显著影响评分一致性，表明题目复杂度是AI评分难度的关键预测因子，并建立了基于分类法的框架，用于判断哪些题目适合AI辅助评分、哪些需人工复核。
WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling
Authors: Yuquan Xue, Le Xu, Zeyi Liu, Zhenyu Wu, Zhengyi Gu, Xinyang Song, Bofang Jia, Ziwei Wang
First: 2026-07-02T17:00:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:00:37+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 9 figures, conference paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldSample：基于世界建模的闭环真实机器人强化学习
强化学习（RL）可通过试错交互使机器人在演示中未观察到的状态下持续改进，从而克服模仿学习（IL）的演示覆盖局限。然而，将RL部署于真实机器人仍受限于高昂的交互成本，因每次物理执行均昂贵且仅反映单一动作-结果路径。为应对该挑战，我们提出WorldSample，一种面向真实机器人RL的物理接地数据增强框架，构建物理-合成闭环，连接物理执行、世界模型生成与策略优化。WorldSample基于真实执行生成高保真合成转移，通过后训练世界模型大幅降低视觉幻觉。具体而言，WorldSample引入策略节奏学习（PPL），通过样本选择与调度调节训练过程，平衡有益增强与价值高估，缓解幻觉引发的噪声。在涉及接触密集与高精度的机器人操作任务实验中，WorldSample相较基线提升策略成功率28%，训练步数减少59%。此外，WorldSample相较仅演示后训练，世界模型视觉保真度在PSNR上提升19.4dB、SSIM提升0.47，验证了物理-合成闭环对策略与世界模型性能的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and limited coverage of real-world robot interactions in reinforcement learning, the paper introduces WorldSample, a closed-loop framework that integrates physical rollouts, world model generation, and policy improvement to reduce dependency on real data. It uses a post-trained world model to generate high-fidelity synthetic transitions grounded in real experiences, and employs Policy-Paced Learning to selectively schedule these samples, balancing augmentation benefits against overestimation and hallucination noise. Experimental results on manipulation tasks show a 28% improvement in policy success rate and 59% reduction in training steps compared to baselines, while also enhancing world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only training.
为降低真实机器人强化学习中物理交互的成本与覆盖局限，本文提出WorldSample框架，构建物理-合成闭环，结合真实滚出、世界模型生成与策略优化。该方法基于真实数据生成高保真合成过渡，再通过策略节奏学习动态筛选样本，平衡数据增强与价值过估计，缓解幻觉噪声。实验表明，在接触密集型操作任务中，策略成功率提升28%，训练步数减少59%，同时世界模型视觉保真度较仅用演示训练提升19.4dB PSNR和0.47 SSIM。
Physical surfaces make touch interactions in virtual reality precise, efficient, and bimanual
Authors: Wen Ying, Seongkook Heo
Venue: www
First: 2026-07-02T16:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:59:52+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted by the "International Journal of Human-Computer Studies (IJHCS)" [Project Link] https://wy-blacksheep.github.io/projects/physical-surface/ [Paper Link] https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S1071581926001254 [Video Link] https://youtu.be/e_nzOkZIIKA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Virtual reality (VR) systems can enable convenient hand-based interactions across diverse work scenarios. However, mid-air gestures lack tactile feedback and a physical reference surface to support the hand. This absence of haptic grounding can cause significant challenges in achieving precise and efficient touch interactions. This paper investigates the effect of different types of hand-grounded haptic feedback on the touch performance of VR tasks that demand high precision, such as selecting, tracing, and sketching. We compared three levels of haptic feedback: 1) No Haptic Feedback, where only visual feedback was provided; 2) Tactile Feedback, where users received vibrotactile and pressure feedback upon touching a virtual surface; 3) Physical Surface, where users interacted with a portable and tangible surface. Our study found that portable physical surfaces enabled the best selection precision, tracing efficiency, and sketch quality. Furthermore, participants showed increased bimanual hand utilization when engaging with a physical surface during tasks. These observed behaviors corresponded to participants' preference for interacting with physical surfaces, attributed to a better sense of confidence and control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：物理表面使虚拟现实中的触觉交互更精准、高效且支持双手操作
虚拟现实（VR）系统可支持多种工作场景下的手部交互，但空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参照面，导致难以实现精准高效的触觉交互。本文研究了不同类型的触觉反馈对高精度VR任务（如选择、描边和绘图）触觉表现的影响，比较了三种触觉反馈水平：1）无触觉反馈（仅视觉反馈）；2）触觉反馈（接触虚拟表面时提供振动与压力反馈）；3）物理表面（用户与便携实体表面交互）。研究发现，便携物理表面在选择精度、描边效率和绘图质量方面表现最佳，且参与者在使用物理表面时双手操作更频繁，这与其对物理表面的偏好一致，源于更强的信心与控制感。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the lack of tactile grounding in mid-air VR interactions, which hinders precision and efficiency. It evaluates three haptic conditions—no feedback, vibrotactile/pressure feedback, and a portable physical surface—for tasks requiring fine motor control. Results show that the physical surface significantly outperformed the others in selection accuracy, tracing speed, and sketch quality, while also promoting bimanual use, reflecting users’ preference for tangible interaction due to enhanced confidence and control.
该研究针对VR中空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参照面的问题，影响了交互的精确性和效率。实验对比了三种反馈方式——无触觉、振动与压力触觉反馈、以及便携式物理表面——在需要精细操作的任务中的表现。结果表明，物理表面在选择精度、轨迹效率和绘图质量上显著优于其他方式，同时促进双手协同操作，反映出用户在使用实体表面时更感自信和掌控力。
Self-Auditing Residual Drifting for Pathology-Preserving Accelerated Knee MRI
Authors: Qing Lyu, Jianxu Wang, Mohammad Kawas, Ge Wang, Christopher T. Whitlow
First: 2026-07-02T16:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:59:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging reduces acquisition time, but reconstruction from undersampled k-space can blur diagnostically relevant structures or introduce failures that are not captured by global image metrics. We propose SA-RDM-DC, a Self-Auditing Residual generative Drifting Model with Data Consistency for accelerated knee MRI. The method adapts the newly proposed generative drifting paradigm to accelerated MRI by training a physics-conditioned drift field from the zero-filled reconstruction toward the fully sampled residual correction. It predicts image- and missing-k-space residual corrections, enforces data consistency with acquired k-space, uses frequency-aware and residual drifting supervision to recover fine detail, and produces dense error maps and slice-level risk scores in the same inference pass. We evaluate SA-RDM-DC on multi-coil fastMRI knee data at acceleration factors of 4, 8, and 12, with fastMRI+ pathology annotations for region-level and classifier-based task preservation, and on SKM-TEA for zero-shot and fine-tuned protocol-shift evaluation. Compared with zero-filled reconstruction, UNet-image-SENSE, DC-UNet, Score-Diffusion, ELF-Diff, SENSE-VarNet, and MoDL baselines, SA-RDM-DC achieves the highest SSIM across fastMRI acceleration factors while retaining subsecond per-slice inference and avoiding the long sampling time of iterative diffusion baselines. In pathology-aware analysis, SA-RDM-DC preserves lesion-region structural fidelity and reduces meniscus prediction instability. Its self-auditing scores strongly identify high-error reconstructions on fastMRI and partially transfer as a selective-review signal under SKM-TEA protocol shift. These results support reconstruction evaluation that jointly considers image fidelity, pathology preservation, runtime, and case-specific reliability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于病理保持加速膝关节MRI的自审计残差漂移方法
加速磁共振成像可缩短采集时间，但由欠采样k空间重建可能模糊诊断相关结构或引入全局图像指标无法捕捉的失败。我们提出SA-RDM-DC，一种面向加速膝关节MRI的自审计残差生成漂移模型，结合数据一致性。该方法将新提出的生成漂移范式应用于加速MRI，通过训练从零填充重建到完全采样残差校正的物理条件漂移场。它预测图像和缺失k空间的残差校正，强制满足采集k空间的数据一致性，利用频域感知和残差漂移监督恢复细节，并在同一推理过程中生成密集误差图和切片级风险评分。我们在加速因子为4、8、12的多线圈fastMRI膝关节数据上评估SA-RDM-DC，结合fastMRI+病理标注以保持区域级和分类任务，同时在SKM-TEA数据上评估零样本和微调协议迁移性能。与零填充重建、UNet-image-SENSE、DC-UNet、Score-Diffusion、ELF-Diff、SENSE-VarNet和MoDL基线相比，SA-RDM-DC在所有fastMRI加速因子下均获得最高SSIM，同时保持每切片亚秒级推理速度，避免迭代扩散基线的长采样时间。在病理感知分析中，SA-RDM-DC保持病灶区域结构保真度并降低半月板预测不稳定性。其自审计评分能有效识别fastMRI中的高误差重建，并在SKM-TEA协议迁移下部分作为选择性复审信号。这些结果支持综合考虑图像保真度、病理保持、运行时间和病例特异性可靠性的重建评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve diagnostic structures in accelerated knee MRI while avoiding artifacts missed by global metrics, the authors propose SA-RDM-DC, a self-auditing residual drifting model that learns a physics-conditioned drift field from zero-filled reconstructions toward fully sampled residuals. It predicts image- and k-space residuals, enforces data consistency, and generates dense error maps and slice-level risk scores during inference. Evaluated on fastMRI knee data at 4x, 8x, and 12x acceleration with pathology annotations, SA-RDM-DC outperformed multiple baselines in SSIM while maintaining subsecond inference and avoiding iterative diffusion delays. It also preserved lesion structure fidelity, reduced meniscus prediction instability, and produced reliable self-auditing scores that flagged high-error reconstructions and transferred as review signals under protocol shifts.
为在加速膝关节MRI扫描的同时保持诊断病理结构，作者提出SA-RDM-DC模型，该模型通过从零填充重建向全采样残差学习物理条件漂移场。它预测图像与k空间残差校正，强制数据一致性，并在推理过程中生成密集误差图和切片级风险评分。在4x、8x、12x加速的fastMRI膝关节数据上，该模型在SSIM指标上优于多个基线方法，同时保持亚秒级推理速度，避免了迭代扩散模型的长采样时间。此外，它有效保留了病灶结构，降低了半月板预测的不稳定性，其自审计评分能准确识别高误差重建，并在协议迁移场景下作为选择性复审信号部分迁移。
QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition
Authors: Quoc Bao Phan, Tuy Tan Nguyen
First: 2026-07-02T16:54:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:54:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QFedAgent：面向多智能体活动识别的量子增强个性化联邦学习
联邦学习（FL）可在不共享原始数据的前提下实现分布式设备间的协同模型训练，适用于隐私敏感的机器人感知应用。然而，多智能体系统生成异构且非独立同分布（non-IID）的多模态传感器流，会降低传统FL算法性能，而经典融合模块则引入大量参数开销和通信成本。本文提出QFedAgent，一种面向多智能体活动识别的混合量子-经典个性化FL框架。该方法集成变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码与纠缠建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数，相较基于多层感知机的经典融合方案（33K参数）实现约10倍总参数缩减。在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，针对基于主体的非IID划分实验表明，平均测试准确率达97.7%，验证了参数高效的量子融合在保持竞争力的同时优于传统联邦学习基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for privacy-preserving yet efficient multi-agent activity recognition in robotic systems, this paper introduces QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized federated learning framework. It employs a variational quantum circuit to fuse accelerometer and gyroscope data via quantum state encoding and entanglement, drastically reducing parameters from 33K in classical MLPs to just 72, achieving roughly 10x compression. Evaluated on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID splits, QFedAgent achieved 97.7% mean test accuracy, demonstrating that quantum-enhanced fusion maintains competitive performance while significantly lowering computational and communication overhead.
为解决机器人系统中多智能体活动识别的隐私保护与效率问题，本文提出QFedAgent，一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架。该框架采用变分量子电路通过量子态编码与纠缠融合加速度计与陀螺仪数据，参数量从经典MLP的33K大幅压缩至仅72，实现约10倍压缩。在OPPORTUNITY数据集的按用户非独立同分布划分下，QFedAgent达到97.7%的平均测试准确率，证明量子增强融合在保持竞争力的同时显著降低计算与通信开销。
Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs
Authors: Francesca Pistilli, Simone Alberto Peirone, Giuseppe Averta
First: 2026-07-02T16:53:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:53:42+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://francescapistilli.github.io/GLEN
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Understanding human behavior while interacting with the surrounding world is crucial for many applications of embodied AI. First-person videos are particularly informative for this problem, as they well capture how activities reshape the scene over time. However, existing approaches often rely on implicit visual or language-aligned representations, disregarding structured reasoning over the scene dynamic. We argue that explicit, compositional and editable representations of human-environment interactions can play a crucial role for rich grounded activity understanding. To this end, we introduce SG-Ego, a large scale annotation set extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs, where relations triplets are consolidated over time into explicit time-evolving descriptions of the scene state. To reason over this representation, we propose GLEN, a graph-based model that operates over scene graph sequences to both align them with textual actions and model their temporal evolution. In addition, we formulate the activity-driven graph-edit forecasting (A-GEF) problem, a novel task that casts scene dynamics as a sequence of structured transformations conditioned on ongoing actions, enabling explicit reasoning about how scenes change over time. We validate our approach across multiple downstream tasks, spanning retrieval benchmarks as EgoMCQ and EgoCVR, as well as long-horizon reasoning benchmarks as EXPLORE-Bench and the newly introduced A-GEF. GLEN achieves strong results compared to raw video baselines and it excels in reasoning settings, typically addressed only with MLLMs, while enabling controllable and structured predictions of scene dynamics driven by human activities. We believe our results establish spatio-temporal scene graphs, together with models that reason over them, as strong compositional and interpretable representations for video understanding and potentially beyond.

标题：学习场景演化：利用场景图推理人类活动
理解人类在与周围世界互动时的行为，对具身AI的诸多应用至关重要。第一人称视频对此问题尤其有信息量，因为它们能很好地捕捉活动如何随时间重塑场景。然而，现有方法常依赖隐式视觉或语言对齐表示，忽视了对场景动态的结构化推理。我们认为，人类-环境交互的显式、组合式和可编辑表示，对深入理解接地活动至关重要。为此，我们引入SG-Ego，一个扩展Ego4D的大规模标注数据集，包含时空场景图，其中关系三元组随时间整合为显式的场景状态演化描述。为推理该表示，我们提出GLEN，一种基于图的模型，可在场景图序列上操作，同时将它们与文本动作对齐并建模其时间演化。此外，我们提出活动驱动的图编辑预测（A-GEF）问题，这是一个新颖任务，将场景动态建模为基于正在进行动作的结构化变换序列，从而实现对场景随时间变化的显式推理。我们在多个下游任务上验证了我们的方法，涵盖检索基准如EgoMCQ和EgoCVR，以及长时程推理基准如EXPLORE-Bench和新引入的A-GEF。GLEN相比原始视频基线表现优异，在通常仅由多模态大语言模型处理的推理场景中表现突出，同时支持由人类活动驱动的场景动态的可控和结构化预测。我们相信，我们的结果确立了时空场景图及其推理模型，作为视频理解（乃至更广泛领域）的强大组合式和可解释表示。
