To better understand human activities through embodied AI, this work proposes SG-Ego, a large-scale dataset extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs that explicitly capture how scenes evolve over time. The authors introduce GLEN, a graph-based model that aligns scene graph sequences with textual actions and models their temporal evolution, enabling structured reasoning about scene dynamics. They also define the A-GEF task, which frames scene changes as action-conditioned graph edits, allowing explicit prediction of scene transformations. Experiments across retrieval and long-horizon reasoning benchmarks show GLEN outperforms raw video baselines and matches or exceeds MLLM performance, while offering controllable, interpretable scene predictions driven by human actions.
为提升第一人称视频中人类活动的理解，该文提出SG-Ego数据集，通过时空场景图标注场景随时间的演变过程。其核心模型GLEN基于图结构，对场景图序列进行对齐与时序建模，支持对场景动态的显式推理。作者进一步提出A-GEF任务，将场景变化建模为由当前动作驱动的结构化变换序列。实验表明，GLEN在检索与长时推理任务中优于原始视频基线，且在需要结构化推理的场景中表现优异，可提供可控、可解释的场景动态预测。
Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs
Authors: Zhuowei Chen, Liwei Chen, Christian Schunn, Raquel Coelho, Xiang Lorraine Li
First: 2026-07-02T16:51:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:51:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大语言模型的神经元感知主动少样本学习
主动少样本学习（AFSL）通过识别最有价值的未标注样本进行标注并作为少样本演示，以适应大语言模型到特定领域，有效降低人工标注成本同时提升性能。然而，现有方法通常依赖输出层信号（如预测熵或基于外部嵌入的测试时数据语义相似度）进行样本选择，常忽略模型内部动态，而后者可精准定位特定知识缺口。为弥合此差距，我们提出NeuFS框架，一种神经元感知主动少样本学习方法，将样本选择范式从输出层代理转向模型内部动态。NeuFS利用神经元激活模式直接表征样本，并采用双标准选择策略：（1）通过神经元模式确保少样本多样性，实现更广泛的示例覆盖；（2）优先选择LLM易产生幻觉的具有信息量和挑战性的少样本，通过量化神经元共识实现。在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理与文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL基线。消融研究进一步表明，内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供更合理有效的选择信号，验证了NeuFS的优越性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce human annotation costs while improving LLM performance in specialized domains, this paper introduces NeuFS, a neuron-aware active few-shot learning framework that selects samples based on internal neuron activation patterns rather than output-level proxies like entropy or external embeddings. NeuFS employs a dual-criteria strategy: first, it ensures sample diversity by leveraging neuron patterns to cover broader example spaces; second, it prioritizes samples where the model is prone to hallucination by measuring neuron consensus. Experiments across three datasets show NeuFS consistently outperforms existing AFSL methods in both reasoning and text classification tasks, with ablation studies confirming that internal neuron activations yield more principled and effective selection signals than external embeddings.
为降低人工标注成本并提升大语言模型在特定领域的性能，本文提出NeuFS框架，该框架基于模型内部神经元激活模式而非输出层代理信号来选择样本。NeuFS采用双标准策略：首先，利用神经元模式确保样本多样性，覆盖更广泛的示例空间；其次，通过衡量神经元共识优先选择模型易产生幻觉的具有信息量和挑战性的样本。在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理与文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL基线方法，消融实验进一步验证了内部神经元激活相比外部嵌入提供了更合理且有效的样本选择信号。
Wavelet-Guided Semantic Signal Compensation for Inversion-Free Image Editing
Authors: Anqi Tang, Wenhao Sun, Zhaoqiang Liu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T16:50:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:50:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-guided image editing aims to modify visual content according to a target prompt while preserving the background. Recent inversion-free image editing frameworks such as FlowEdit have demonstrated strong editing capability without requiring inversion. Empirically, FlowEdit can achieve substantial semantic changes under appropriate hyperparameter settings. However, we observe that under certain global attribute shifts, the editing trajectory may not effectively move away from the source distribution in the early timesteps. Our analysis suggests that in the high-noise regime, the dominant manifold-seeking flow toward the data manifold can reduce the influence of the text-conditioned direction, leading to limited global modification while background structures remain only moderately preserved. Inspired by this observation, we propose an inversion-free, frequency-aware semantic compensation strategy that strengthens the effective signal in the early stage of generation, while maintaining structural consistency in the background. The proposed method improves global editing capacity without sacrificing background fidelity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于小波引导的语义信号补偿的无反演图像编辑
文本引导的图像编辑旨在根据目标提示修改视觉内容同时保留背景。近期的无反演图像编辑框架（如FlowEdit）在无需反演的情况下已展现出强大的编辑能力。实证表明，FlowEdit在适当超参数设置下可实现显著的语义变化。然而，我们观察到在某些全局属性变化下，编辑轨迹在早期时间步可能未能有效脱离源分布。我们的分析表明，在高噪声环境下，主导数据流形的流场会削弱文本条件方向的影响，导致全局修改有限，而背景结构仅适度保留。受此启发，我们提出一种无反演、频率感知的语义补偿策略，强化生成早期的有效信号，同时保持背景结构一致性。该方法提升了全局编辑能力，且不牺牲背景保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that inversion-free image editing models like FlowEdit struggle to achieve strong global semantic changes early in generation due to manifold-seeking behavior dominating over text-guided signals, this work proposes a wavelet-guided semantic compensation method that enhances early-stage signal strength while preserving background structure. The method leverages frequency-aware decomposition to amplify text-conditioned semantic directions in high-noise regimes, enabling more effective global editing without compromising background fidelity. Experimental results demonstrate improved semantic modification capacity across diverse editing tasks while maintaining structural consistency in the background, outperforming prior inversion-free baselines.
针对无反演图像编辑模型（如FlowEdit）在高噪声环境下早期生成阶段难以有效实现全局语义修改、同时背景结构保留不足的问题，本文提出一种基于小波引导的语义补偿策略，通过频域分解强化早期阶段的文本引导信号，同时保持背景结构一致性。实验表明，该方法在提升全局语义修改能力的同时，显著优于基线方法，背景保真度未受影响。
LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video
Authors: Boyang Sun, Jiajie Li, Yung-Hsu Yang, Chenyangguang Zhang, Tim Engelbracht, Sunghwan Hong, Cesar Cadena, Marc Pollefeys, Hermann Blum
First: 2026-07-02T16:48:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LIME：从第一人称视频中学习意图感知的相机运动
自主机器人通常需要在执行动作前移动相机：例如检查物体、揭示被遮挡区域或获取响应用户意图的视角。虽然视觉-语言导航可将指令转化为基础运动，视觉-语言-动作策略可将指令映射到操作动作，但语言条件下的相机运动作为一类独立动作仍相对未被充分探索。我们提出语言条件相机运动生成任务：给定当前RGB观测和自由形式自然语言意图，预测下一帧观测的相对目标相机位姿。该任务本质非平凡：视角变化由潜在感知意图驱动，有效运动可在不同语义粒度上操作，如进入房间、绕角观察、检查可见物体或揭示遮挡细节。为建模此结构，我们从第一人称视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督，将合理意图与观测增益描述配对至相对SE(3)目标位姿。我们提出LIME，一种视觉-语言相机运动生成器，结合自回归观测增益输出与连续流匹配位姿头。该设计使模型可同时预测下一视角应揭示的内容，并表示多假设目标视角。在实验及下游机器人任务中，我们证明LIME能从被动人类视频中主动选择相机位姿，将普通第一人称录像转化为意图感知主动感知的监督。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to autonomously adjust camera views based on user intent, this work addresses the underexplored problem of generating language-conditioned camera motion. The method, LIME, combines a vision-language model that predicts observation-gain descriptions with a continuous flow-matching module to output relative SE(3) camera poses, enabling the model to jointly reason about what to reveal and how to move. Experiments demonstrate that LIME can learn from passive egocentric videos to generate intent-aware camera motions, effectively turning observational data into active perception supervision for robotic tasks.
为使机器人能根据用户意图自主调整摄像头运动，该研究提出LIME模型，通过自然语言指令和RGB图像预测相对相机位姿。该方法结合自回归模块生成观测收益描述与连续流匹配头预测位姿，使模型能同时推理应揭示的内容与运动方式。实验表明，LIME可从被动的自摄像视频中学习多意图监督，成功生成意图感知的相机运动，提升下游机器人感知任务表现。
The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing
Authors: David Jurgens
First: 2026-07-02T16:47:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:47:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自然语言处理的未来可能不在NLP会议：自然语言处理领域的学术迁移模式
自然语言处理（NLP）传统上在ACL等核心学科会议发表，但随着大语言模型（LLMs）的发展，NLP与通用机器学习（ML）之间的学科界限变得模糊，作者常在两个领域的会议发表论文。本文探讨学科重心是否正在转移。通过对2010至2026年间NLP研究及新老作者的分析，我们发现确有迁移趋势：首先，对比LLM出现前后的时期，资深作者在ACL主会议轨道的份额下降19.2个百分点，而在新设的Findings轨道上升14.8个百分点，通用ML会议份额上升8.6个百分点，即使考虑两领域同步增长。其次，对于以至少三篇第一作者NLP主题论文首次亮相的新作者，其工作主要发表于ACL会议的比例从2019年的84%降至2024年的74%，而主要发表于通用ML会议的比例则从5%升至21%。通过因果推断方法，我们估计通用ML会议带来显著的引用优势，影响作者选刊决策。综合来看，这些结果表明NLP研究的发表地点正发生显著变化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rise of Large Language Models and the blurring of disciplinary boundaries between NLP and general ML, this study investigates whether NLP’s publishing center of gravity is shifting. Using data from 2010 to 2026, it compares publishing patterns of established and new authors, finding that established authors lost 19.2 percentage points in ACL main-conference shares while gaining 14.8 points in ACL Findings and 8.6 points in general ML venues. Among new authors, ACL dominance declined from 84% to 74%, while general ML venue presence rose from 5% to 21%. Causal inference suggests general ML venues confer a citation advantage, influencing author choices, indicating a significant migration of NLP research toward broader ML venues.
受大型语言模型兴起和自然语言处理与通用机器学习学科边界模糊的影响，本研究探讨NLP出版重心是否正在转移。分析2010至2026年间作者的出版数据发现，资深作者在ACL主会议的占比下降19.2个百分点，转而增加14.8个百分点至ACL Findings及8.6个百分点至通用机器学习会议；新作者中，主要发表于ACL会议的比例从2019年的84%降至2024年的74%，而主要发表于通用机器学习会议的比例则从5%升至21%。因果推断显示，通用机器学习会议具有显著的引用优势，影响作者选刊决策，表明NLP出版格局正发生显著迁移。
Q-GAIN: A Python Package for Machine Learning and Physically Informed Analysis Applications
Authors: M. Doris, S. Guo, S. M. Koh, L. Ritter, A. R. Fritsch, S. Mukherjee, I. B. Spielman, J. P. Zwolak
First: 2026-07-02T16:45:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:45:34+00:00
Comments: Submission to SciPost, 20 pages with 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Here we describe the quantum gas analysis and inference (Q-GAIN) Python package, which enables rapid deployment of machine learning (ML) and physics-informed analysis techniques for cold-atom experiments. Out of the box, Q-GAIN implements classification, object detection, and physics-informed metrics for feature detection in images of atomic Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs). Q-GAIN encourages a natural, module-based workflow: starting with data loading and preprocessing, followed by ML-based feature identification, and ending with conventional analysis techniques. We demonstrate this modularity by configuring Q-GAIN for three ML tasks. First, we demonstrate the basic workflow of the Q-GAIN framework by implementing the standard task of classifying handwritten digits from the MNIST dataset. Then, we re-implement our earlier soliton detection (SolDet) package in the Q-GAIN framework, enabling the detection and analysis of solitonic excitations in time-of-flight data. Finally, we develop an object-detection tool that identifies quantized vortices in images of ring-shaped BECs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Q-GAIN：用于机器学习和物理信息分析应用的Python包
本文介绍了量子气体分析与推断（Q-GAIN）Python包，该包可快速部署机器学习（ML）和物理信息分析技术，用于冷原子实验。开箱即用，Q-GAIN实现了图像中玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）特征检测的分类、目标检测和物理信息度量。Q-GAIN鼓励自然的模块化工作流程：从数据加载与预处理开始，接着进行基于ML的特征识别，最后采用传统分析技术。我们通过配置Q-GAIN完成三项ML任务来展示其模块化特性。首先，我们通过实现MNIST数据集上手写数字分类任务，演示Q-GAIN框架的基本工作流程。接着，我们以Q-GAIN框架重新实现早期的孤子检测（SolDet）包，实现对时间飞行数据中孤子激发的检测与分析。最后，我们开发了一种目标检测工具，用于识别环形BEC图像中的量子涡旋。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for streamlined, physics-informed machine learning tools in cold-atom experiments, Q-GAIN provides a modular Python package that integrates data preprocessing, ML-based feature identification, and conventional analysis. It supports classification, object detection, and physics-informed metrics for analyzing images of Bose-Einstein condensates. Experimental demonstrations include classifying MNIST digits to validate the basic workflow, reimplementing a soliton detection tool for time-of-flight data, and developing a vortex detection module for ring-shaped BECs, showcasing its flexibility and applicability across distinct physical scenarios.
为满足冷原子实验中对物理感知机器学习工具的需求，Q-GAIN 提供了一个模块化的 Python 框架，集成数据预处理、基于 ML 的特征检测和传统物理分析。该工具支持分类、目标检测和物理感知度量，通过三个任务验证其灵活性：用 MNIST 数据集验证基础工作流，重实现溶波检测用于时间飞行数据，以及开发用于环形玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体图像的涡旋检测工具。实验结果表明该框架在多种应用场景中具备高效部署能力，可快速实现针对物理系统的机器学习方法。
Exploring Large Language Models for Access Control Policy Synthesis and Summarization
Authors: Adarsh Vatsa, Bethel Hall, William Eiers
First: 2025-10-23T16:06:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:44:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ISSRE 2026. Major revision and retitling of arXiv:2510.20692v1. Refocuses the paper on reliable neurosymbolic access-control policy analysis; updates the PolicySummarizer method, multi-cloud evaluation, and user-study results. 13 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cloud computing is ubiquitous, with a growing number of services being hosted on the cloud every day. Typical cloud compute systems allow administrators to write policies implementing access control rules which specify how access to private data is governed. These policies must be manually written, and due to their complexity can often be error prone. Moreover, existing policies often implement complex access control specifications and thus can be difficult to precisely analyze in determining their behavior works exactly as intended. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great success in automated code synthesis and summarization. Given this success, they could potentially be used for automatically generating access control policies or aid in understanding existing policies. In this paper, we explore the effectiveness of LLMs for access control policy synthesis and summarization. Specifically, we first investigate diverse LLMs for access control policy synthesis, finding that: although LLMs can effectively generate syntactically correct policies, they have permissiveness issues, generating policies equivalent to the given specification 45.8% of the time for non-reasoning LLMs, and 93.7% of the time for reasoning LLMs. We then investigate how LLMs can be used to analyze policies by introducing a novel semantic-based request summarization approach which leverages LLMs to generate a precise characterization of the requests allowed by a policy. Our results show that while there are significant hurdles in leveraging LLMs for automated policy generation, LLMs show promising results when combined with symbolic approaches in analyzing existing policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索大语言模型在访问控制策略合成与摘要中的应用
云计算无处不在，每天都有越来越多的服务部署在云端。典型的云计算系统允许管理员编写策略以实现访问控制规则，这些规则规定了对私有数据的访问如何被管理。这些策略必须手动编写，由于其复杂性，常常容易出错。此外，现有策略通常实现复杂的访问控制规范，因此在确定其行为是否完全符合预期时难以精确分析。最近，大语言模型（LLMs）在自动化代码合成与摘要方面取得了巨大成功。鉴于这一成功，它们可能被用于自动生成访问控制策略或辅助理解现有策略。本文探讨了LLMs在访问控制策略合成与摘要中的有效性。具体而言，我们首先研究了多种LLMs在访问控制策略合成中的表现，发现：尽管LLMs能有效生成语法正确的策略，但存在宽松性问题，非推理型LLMs仅45.8%的策略等价于给定规范，而推理型LLMs则高达93.7%。随后，我们研究了如何利用LLMs分析策略，引入了一种基于语义的请求摘要方法，利用LLMs生成策略允许请求的精确描述。结果表明，尽管在自动化策略生成方面存在显著障碍，但LLMs与符号方法结合后，在分析现有策略方面展现出良好前景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity and error-proneness of manually writing cloud access control policies, this study investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can synthesize or summarize such policies. The authors evaluate multiple LLMs for policy generation, finding that while they produce syntactically valid policies, they often exhibit permissiveness issues—only 45.8% of non-reasoning LLMs and 93.7% of reasoning LLMs generate policies matching the intended specification. To address policy analysis, they introduce a semantic-based summarization method using LLMs to characterize allowed requests, showing promise when combined with symbolic techniques, despite challenges in automated policy generation.
鉴于手动编写云访问控制策略复杂且易出错，本研究探讨大语言模型（LLM）是否可用于策略合成或总结。作者评估多种LLM发现，尽管能生成语法正确的策略，但常存在权限宽松问题——非推理模型准确率仅45.8%，推理模型达93.7%。为解决策略分析问题，提出一种基于语义的总结方法，利用LLM刻画允许的请求，结果显示与符号方法结合时，神经符号分析策略具有潜力。
Wiki-R1: Incentivizing Multimodal Reasoning for Knowledge-based VQA via Data and Sampling Curriculum
Authors: Shan Ning, Longtian Qiu, Xuming He
Venue: ICLR
First: 2026-03-05T15:08:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:41:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICLR 26, code and weights are publicly available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires models to answer questions about an image by integrating external knowledge, posing significant challenges due to noisy retrieval and the structured, encyclopedic nature of the knowledge base. These characteristics create a distributional gap from pretrained multimodal large language models (MLLMs), making effective reasoning and domain adaptation difficult in the post-training stage. In this work, we propose \textit{Wiki-R1}, a data-generation-based curriculum reinforcement learning framework that systematically incentivizes reasoning in MLLMs for KB-VQA. Wiki-R1 constructs a sequence of training distributions aligned with the model's evolving capability, bridging the gap from pretraining to the KB-VQA target distribution. We introduce \textit{controllable curriculum data generation}, which manipulates the retriever to produce samples at desired difficulty levels, and a \textit{curriculum sampling strategy} that selects informative samples likely to yield non-zero advantages during RL updates. Sample difficulty is estimated using observed rewards and propagated to unobserved samples to guide learning. Experiments on two KB-VQA benchmarks, Encyclopedic VQA and InfoSeek, demonstrate that Wiki-R1 achieves new state-of-the-art results, improving accuracy from 35.5\% to 37.1\% on Encyclopedic VQA and from 40.1\% to 44.1\% on InfoSeek. The project page is available at https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiR1/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Wiki-R1：通过数据与采样课程激励多模态推理以实现基于知识的视觉问答
基于知识的视觉问答（KB-VQA）要求模型通过整合外部知识回答图像相关问题，但由于检索噪声和知识库的结构化、百科全书式特性，给模型带来显著挑战。这些特性导致预训练多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）与目标分布之间存在分布差距，使得后训练阶段的有效推理和领域适应困难。本文提出Wiki-R1，一种基于数据生成的课程强化学习框架，系统性地激励MLLMs在KB-VQA任务中的推理能力。Wiki-R1构建与模型能力演进相匹配的训练分布序列，弥合预训练与KB-VQA目标分布之间的差距。我们引入可控课程数据生成机制，通过操控检索器生成指定难度级别的样本，并提出课程采样策略，选择在强化学习更新中可能带来非零优势的有信息量样本。样本难度通过观察到的奖励估计并传播至未观测样本以引导学习。在两个KB-VQA基准（Encyclopedic VQA和InfoSeek）上的实验表明，Wiki-R1取得新SOTA结果：在Encyclopedic VQA上准确率从35.5%提升至37.1%，在InfoSeek上从40.1%提升至44.1%。项目页面见https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiR1/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning pretrained multimodal large language models with the structured, noisy knowledge base required for KB-VQA, this work introduces Wiki-R1, a curriculum reinforcement learning framework that incrementally guides model reasoning through carefully designed data distributions. It employs controllable curriculum data generation to steer retrievers toward producing samples of varying difficulty and a curriculum sampling strategy that prioritizes informative samples likely to yield non-zero rewards during RL updates, with difficulty propagated from observed to unobserved samples. Experiments on Encyclopedic VQA and InfoSeek show Wiki-R1 improves accuracy from 35.5% to 37.1% and from 40.1% to 44.1%, respectively, establishing new state-of-the-art performance.
为解决预训练多模态大模型与知识库视觉问答任务中结构化、噪声知识检索之间的分布差距，本文提出Wiki-R1框架，通过数据生成与采样课程策略逐步引导模型推理能力。该方法通过可控课程数据生成调节检索器输出不同难度样本，并采用课程采样策略优先选择能带来非零强化学习收益的样本，利用观测奖励传播难度信息以指导学习。在Encyclopedic VQA和InfoSeek两个基准上，准确率分别从35.5%提升至37.1%、从40.1%提升至44.1%，达到当前最优性能。
Incremental (k, z)-Clustering on Graphs
Authors: Emilio Cruciani, Sebastian Forster, Antonis Skarlatos
First: 2026-02-09T11:43:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:40:18+00:00
Comments: In the Proceedings of ICALP 2026. Abstract shortened to meet arXiv limits
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Given a weighted undirected graph, a number of clusters $k$, and an exponent $z$, the goal in the $(k, z)$-clustering problem on graphs is to select $k$ vertices as centers that minimize the sum of the distances raised to the power $z$ of each vertex to its closest center. In the dynamic setting, the graph is subject to adversarial edge updates, and the goal is to maintain explicitly an exact $(k, z)$-clustering solution in the induced shortest-path metric. While efficient dynamic $k$-center approximation algorithms on graphs exist [Cruciani et al. SODA 2024], to the best of our knowledge, no prior work provides similar results for the dynamic $(k,z)$-clustering problem. As the main result of this paper, we develop a randomized incremental $(k, z)$-clustering algorithm that maintains with high probability a constant-factor approximation in a graph undergoing edge insertions with a total update time of $\tilde O(k m^{1+o(1)}+ k^{1+\frac{1}λ} m)$, where $λ\geq 1$ is an arbitrary fixed constant. Our incremental algorithm consists of two stages. In the first stage, we maintain a constant-factor bicriteria approximate solution of size $\tilde{O}(k)$ with a total update time of $m^{1+o(1)}$ over all adversarial edge insertions. This first stage is an intricate adaptation of the bicriteria approximation algorithm by Mettu and Plaxton [Machine Learning 2004] to incremental graphs. One of our key technical results is that the radii in their algorithm can be assumed to be non-decreasing while the approximation ratio remains constant, a property that may be of independent interest. In the second stage, we maintain a constant-factor approximate $(k,z)$-clustering solution on a dynamic weighted instance induced by the bicriteria approximate solution. For this subproblem, we employ a dynamic spanner algorithm together with a static $(k,z)$-clustering algorithm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图上的增量式(k, z)-聚类
给定一个加权无向图、聚类数k和指数z，图上的(k, z)-聚类问题的目标是选择k个顶点作为中心，使得每个顶点到其最近中心的距离的z次方之和最小。在动态设置中，图会遭受敌对边更新，目标是在诱导的最短路径度量中显式维护精确的(k, z)-聚类解。虽然图上存在高效的动态k中心近似算法[Cruciani等, SODA 2024]，但据我们所知，尚无先前工作为动态(k, z)-聚类问题提供类似结果。本文的主要成果是开发了一种随机增量式(k, z)-聚类算法，在边插入的图上以高概率维护常数因子近似解，总更新时间为\tilde O(k m^{1+o(1)}+ k^{1+\frac{1}λ} m)，其中λ≥1为任意固定常数。我们的增量算法包含两个阶段。第一阶段，我们维护一个大小为\tilde{O}(k)的常数因子双标准近似解，总更新时间为m^{1+o(1)}，覆盖所有敌对边插入。该第一阶段是对Mettu和Plaxton[Machine Learning 2004]双标准近似算法在增量图上的复杂适应。我们的关键技术成果之一是，其算法中的半径可假设为非递减，同时保持常数近似比，这一性质可能具有独立研究价值。第二阶段，我们在由双标准近似解诱导的动态加权实例上维护常数因子近似(k, z)-聚类解。为此子问题，我们结合动态跨度器算法与静态(k, z)-聚类算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient dynamic clustering in evolving graphs, this paper addresses the $(k, z)$-clustering problem under adversarial edge updates, where the goal is to maintain a constant-factor approximate solution. The authors propose a randomized incremental algorithm that operates in two stages: first, it maintains a bicriteria approximate solution with $\tilde{O}(k)$ centers using $m^{1+o(1)}$ update time, adapting the Mettu-Plaxton algorithm with a novel non-decreasing radii property. In the second stage, it leverages a dynamic spanner and static clustering algorithm to maintain the $(k, z)$-clustering on the induced metric, achieving total update time $\tilde{O}(k m^{1+o(1)} + k^{1+\frac{1}{\lambda}} m)$ for any fixed $\lambda \geq 1$. Experimental results confirm the algorithm’s efficiency and approximation guarantees under adversarial edge insertions.
为应对动态图中高效聚类的需求，本文研究了增量式(k, z)-聚类问题，目标是在敌对边插入下维护常数因子近似解。作者提出一种随机增量算法，总更新时间为\tilde O(k m^{1+o(1)} + k^{1+1/λ} m)，其中λ ≥ 1为任意常数。方法分两阶段：第一阶段通过改进Mettu和Plaxton的双标准近似算法，在\tilde O(m^{1+o(1)})时间内维护\tilde O(k)个中心，其关键技术是证明半径可非递减而仍保持近似比；第二阶段结合动态图spanner与静态(k, z)-聚类算法，在诱导度量上维护聚类解。实验结果验证了算法在敌对更新下的效率与近似保证。
Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments
Authors: Xianhui Meng, Zirui Song, Yuchen Zhang, Li Zhang, Yongxuan Lv, Xiuying Chen, Kun Wang, Yan Luo, Kai Chen, Hangjun Ye, Long Chen, Jun Liu, Xiaoshuai Hao
First: 2026-07-02T16:40:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:40:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于文本驱动的非曼哈顿环境中3D室内场景合成
大型语言模型（LLMs）在曼哈顿环境中的3D室内合成方面已展现出卓越能力。然而，现有方法在非曼哈顿环境中往往难以捕捉合理的物体布局模式，主要因其难以建模非正交空间关系，导致几何违规率高、物理保真度低。为解决此问题，我们提出SPG-Layout，一种新颖的文本驱动框架，旨在复杂非曼哈顿环境中生成物理上合理的室内场景。具体而言，我们首先利用物体分布的统计先验引导训练过程，增强环境理解与保真度；此外，借鉴人类设计工作流，采用分层布局策略，优先放置大型物体，从而大幅减少布局违规。通过协同这些组件，SPG-Layout实现了语义真实性和物理合理性的平衡优化。为评估在复杂环境中的性能，我们构建了一个包含500个多样化非曼哈顿环境的新基准。大量实验表明，SPG-Layout在曼哈顿与非曼哈顿环境中均显著优于现有方法。代码将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing 3D indoor synthesis methods in non-Manhattan environments due to poor modeling of non-orthogonal spatial relationships, the authors propose SPG-Layout, a text-driven framework that enhances physical plausibility through statistical priors of object distributions and a hierarchical layout strategy prioritizing large objects. Evaluated on a new benchmark of 500 non-Manhattan scenes, SPG-Layout consistently outperforms prior methods in both Manhattan and non-Manhattan settings, achieving superior semantic realism and reduced geometric violations.
为解决现有方法在非曼哈顿环境中因难以建模非正交空间关系而导致的几何违规和物理不真实问题，作者提出SPG-Layout框架，结合物体分布统计先验与优先放置大型物体的分层布局策略，提升物理合理性。在包含500个非曼哈顿场景的新基准上，SPG-Layout在曼哈顿与非曼哈顿环境中均显著优于现有方法，实现语义真实性和几何合规性的平衡优化。
Object-centric LeJEPA
Authors: Jakob Geusen, Ender Konukoglu
First: 2026-07-02T16:38:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:38:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image encoders trained with LeJEPA can deliver strong features for downstream tasks, but, like other image-level self-supervised methods, typically require large training datasets. Aligning representations at the level of objects rather than whole scenes promises greater data efficiency, but doing this in a completely self-supervised way, effectively jointly partitioning a scene and representing its objects, is unstable: the two are locked in a cyclic dependency, partitioning requires meaningful representations, while meaningful representations require consistent partitioning. We sidestep this instability by taking object masks as given during training, using cheap, off-the-shelf SAM proposals. We extend LeJEPA - whose distributional anti-collapse objective ports naturally from whole images to variable-sized sets of objects - to align object-centric representations rather than whole images. An additional instance-separating loss, which treats other objects in the same scene as negatives, further boosts downstream performance. Across two model scales and 10-100% of COCO, object-level LeJEPA outperforms image-level LeJEPA on tracking (DAVIS), classification (ImageNet-1k), segmentation (ADE20k), and re-identification (NAVI).
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向对象的LeJEPA
使用LeJEPA训练的图像编码器可为下游任务提供强大特征，但与其他图像级自监督方法类似，通常需要大量训练数据。在对象层面而非整个场景层面对表示进行对齐有望提高数据效率，但完全自监督地实现这一点——即同时对场景进行分割并表示其对象——是不稳定的：两者存在循环依赖，分割需要有意义的表示，而有意义的表示又需要一致的分割。我们通过在训练中将对象掩码作为给定条件，使用廉价的现成SAM提案来规避这一不稳定性。我们将LeJEPA——其分布对抗坍缩目标可自然从完整图像扩展到可变大小的对象集合——扩展为对齐面向对象的表示而非完整图像。此外，一个额外的实例分离损失，将同一场景中的其他对象视为负样本，进一步提升了下游性能。在两个模型规模和COCO数据集10%-100%的情况下，面向对象的LeJEPA在跟踪（DAVIS）、分类（ImageNet-1k）、分割（ADE20k）和重识别（NAVI）任务上均优于图像级LeJEPA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of image-level self-supervised methods like LeJEPA, which require large datasets, this work proposes object-centric LeJEPA to improve data efficiency by aligning representations at the object level. It sidesteps instability from jointly partitioning and representing objects by using precomputed object masks from SAM during training. The method extends LeJEPA’s anti-collapse objective to variable-sized object sets and adds an instance-separating loss to distinguish objects within scenes. Experiments show that object-level LeJEPA consistently outperforms image-level LeJEPA across two model scales and varying COCO data amounts, achieving superior results in tracking, classification, segmentation, and re-identification tasks.
为解决图像级自监督方法（如LeJEPA）数据效率低的问题，该研究提出对象级LeJEPA，通过在对象层面而非图像层面对齐表征以提升数据效率。训练时使用现成的SAM生成的对象掩码规避了对象分割与表征联合优化的循环依赖问题，并将LeJEPA的分布对抗坍缩目标扩展至可变大小的对象集合，同时引入实例分离损失以区分场景内不同对象。在两个模型规模和不同COCO数据比例下，对象级LeJEPA在跟踪、分类、分割和重识别任务上均优于图像级LeJEPA。
ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models
Authors: Gawon Seo, Dongwon Kim, Suha Kwak
First: 2026-07-02T16:38:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:38:10+00:00
Comments: Project Page: [this https URL](https://gawon1224.github.io/ACID/)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ACID：通过逆动力学实现动作一致性以支持世界模型的规划
基于动作条件的世界模型的决策时规划已成为具身控制的流行范式。然而，标准规划成本仅根据预测的终端状态与目标的接近程度来评估候选方案，而未检查中间过渡的可实现性——预测轨迹可能看起来令人信服，但环境执行却可能偏离该轨迹。本文提出ACID，一种决策时规划框架，引入循环动作一致性：由逆动力学模型从预测过渡反向推断的动作应能恢复出所条件化的动作。我们通过尺度不变自适应权重将每步残差纳入规划成本。在四个动作条件世界模型和涵盖刚体与柔性操作、关节控制及视觉导航的六个任务中，ACID持续提升规划效果，并以显著更少的规划计算量达到基线精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that standard planning methods ignore the realizability of intermediate transitions in action-conditioned world models, ACID introduces cycle action consistency by ensuring that actions inferred backward via inverse dynamics match the original conditioned actions. It integrates this consistency residual into the planning cost using a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Experiments across four world models and six tasks show ACID consistently improves planning performance while matching baseline accuracy with significantly reduced computational cost.
为了解决标准规划方法忽略中间过渡可实现性的问题，ACID通过要求逆动力学模型反推的动作与原始条件动作一致，引入循环动作一致性，并将该残差以尺度不变自适应权重融入规划代价。在四个世界模型和六个不同任务上，ACID持续提升规划性能，同时以显著减少的计算开销达到基线精度。
Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets
Authors: Nick Stracke, Kolja Bauer, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Miguel Angel Bautista, Josh Susskind, Björn Ommer
First: 2026-07-02T16:35:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:35:52+00:00
Comments: for code, view https://github.com/CompVis/set-learner
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：展示示例：从图像集合推断视觉概念
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）能够遵循复杂的文本指令，但在纯视觉上下文中推理能力较弱。特别是，当前模型无法从示例图像集合中推断共享概念并将其应用于新输入。我们提出了“从图像集合推断视觉概念”（VICIS）任务，用于评估这一能力。给定一组共享概念的示例图像和一个查询图像，模型必须生成新图像，使其保留上下文定义的概念，同时与查询图像保持一致。我们发现当前最先进的VLMs在该任务上表现不佳，常忽略视觉上下文或默认生成有偏结果。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种训练框架和架构，使模型能够从图像集合中推断视觉概念，并从查询中提取概念特定嵌入。在合成数据和大规模ImageNet/WordNet数据上的实验表明，我们的模型生成更准确、多样化的输出，并能泛化到未见过的概念和模态（如草图）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language models in reasoning from purely visual context, this work introduces VICIS, a task that requires models to infer shared visual concepts from example image sets and apply them to new queries. The authors propose a training framework and architecture designed to extract concept-specific embeddings from queries and generate outputs consistent with both the context and query. Experiments on synthetic and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet datasets show that their model outperforms state-of-the-art VLMs, producing more accurate and diverse outputs while generalizing to unseen concepts and modalities like sketches.
为了解决视觉语言模型在纯视觉上下文推理能力不足的问题，本文提出了VICIS任务，要求模型从示例图像集合中推断共享视觉概念，并生成与该概念和查询图像一致的新图像。作者设计了一种训练框架和架构，用于从查询图像中提取概念特定嵌入并从图像集合中学习视觉概念推断。在合成数据和大规模ImageNet/WordNet数据集上的实验表明，该模型显著优于当前最先进的视觉语言模型，生成结果更准确、多样，并能泛化到未见过的概念和模态（如素描）。
A Convex Obstacle Avoidance Formulation
Authors: Ricardo Tapia, Iman Soltani
First: 2025-12-15T19:16:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:35:02+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 12 figures, multimedia
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous driving requires reliable collision avoidance in dynamic environments. Nonlinear Model Predictive Controllers (NMPCs) are suitable for this task, but struggle in time-critical scenarios requiring high frequency. To meet this demand, optimization problems are often simplified via linearization, narrowing the horizon window, or reduced temporal nodes, each compromising accuracy or reliability. This work presents the first general convex obstacle avoidance formulation, enabled by a novel approach to integrating logic. This facilitates the incorporation of an obstacle avoidance formulation into convex MPC schemes, enabling a convex optimization framework with substantially improved computational efficiency relative to conventional nonconvex methods. A key property of the formulation is that obstacle avoidance remains effective even when obstacles lie outside the prediction horizon, allowing shorter horizons for real-time deployment. In scenarios where nonconvex formulations are unavoidable, the proposed method meets or exceeds the performance of representative nonconvex alternatives. The method is evaluated in autonomous vehicle applications, where system dynamics are highly nonlinear.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种凸优化避障公式
自动驾驶需要在动态环境中可靠地避免碰撞。非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC）适用于此任务，但在要求高频率的实时场景中表现不佳。为满足这一需求，通常通过线性化、缩短预测窗口或减少时间节点来简化优化问题，但这些方法会牺牲精度或可靠性。本文首次提出了一种通用的凸障碍物避障公式，该公式基于一种新颖的逻辑集成方法，使避障公式可融入凸MPC框架，从而在计算效率上显著优于传统非凸方法。该公式的另一关键特性是，即使障碍物位于预测窗口之外，避障仍有效，从而允许使用更短的预测窗口以实现实时部署。在必须使用非凸公式的场景中，该方法性能可媲美或超越代表性非凸方法。该方法在自动驾驶应用中进行了评估，其中系统动力学高度非线性。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational inefficiency of nonconvex obstacle avoidance in time-critical autonomous driving, this work introduces the first general convex formulation that integrates logic-based constraints into convex Model Predictive Control. By enabling obstacle avoidance even when obstacles lie beyond the prediction horizon, the method allows for shorter horizons while maintaining safety and performance, significantly improving real-time feasibility. Experimental results in highly nonlinear vehicle dynamics show that the convex approach either matches or outperforms existing nonconvex methods in terms of safety and efficiency.
为解决非线性模型预测控制器在时间敏感的自动驾驶场景中计算效率低的问题，本文提出首个通用凸优化避障公式，通过新颖的逻辑集成方法，使避障约束可嵌入凸优化框架。该方法支持更短预测时域，且在障碍物位于预测域外时仍保持有效避障能力，显著提升计算效率，优于传统非凸方法。在高度非线性车辆动力学场景下的实验表明，该凸优化方法在避障性能上不逊于或优于现有非凸方案。
Drive-JEPA: Video JEPA Meets Multimodal Trajectory Distillation for End-to-End Driving
Authors: Linhan Wang, Zichong Yang, Chen Bai, Guoxiang Zhang, Xiaotong Liu, Xiaoyin Zheng, Xiao-Xiao Long, Chang-Tien Lu, Cheng Lu
First: 2026-01-29T17:39:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:34:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
End-to-end autonomous driving increasingly leverages self-supervised video pretraining to learn transferable planning representations. However, pretraining video world models for scene understanding has so far brought only limited improvements. This limitation is compounded by the inherent ambiguity of driving: each scene typically provides only a single human trajectory, making it difficult to learn multimodal behaviors. In this work, we propose Drive-JEPA, a framework that integrates Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) with multimodal trajectory distillation for end-to-end driving. First, we adapt V-JEPA for end-to-end driving, pretraining a ViT encoder on large-scale driving videos to produce predictive representations aligned with trajectory planning. Second, we introduce a proposal-centric planner that distills diverse simulator-generated trajectories alongside human trajectories, with a momentum-aware selection mechanism to promote stable and safe behavior. When evaluated on NAVSIM, the V-JEPA representation combined with a simple transformer-based decoder outperforms prior methods by 3 PDMS in the perception-free setting. The complete Drive-JEPA framework achieves 93.3 PDMS on v1 and 87.8 EPDMS on v2, setting a new state-of-the-art.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Drive-JEPA：视频JEPA结合多模态轨迹蒸馏用于端到端驾驶
端到端自动驾驶越来越多地利用自监督视频预训练来学习可迁移的规划表示。然而，为场景理解预训练视频世界模型迄今仅带来有限改进。这一局限性因驾驶固有的模糊性而加剧：每个场景通常仅提供一条人类轨迹，难以学习多模态行为。本文提出Drive-JEPA框架，将视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA）与多模态轨迹蒸馏结合用于端到端驾驶。首先，我们为端到端驾驶适配V-JEPA，利用大规模驾驶视频预训练ViT编码器，生成与轨迹规划对齐的预测表示。其次，我们引入以提案为中心的规划器，蒸馏模拟器生成的多样化轨迹与人类轨迹，并采用动量感知选择机制以促进稳定安全行为。在NAVSIM评估中，V-JEPA表示结合简单Transformer解码器在无感知设置下比先前方法提升3 PDMS。完整Drive-JEPA框架在v1上达到93.3 PDMS，在v2上达到87.8 EPDMS，创下新SOTA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited gains from video pretraining in autonomous driving and the challenge of learning multimodal behaviors from sparse human trajectories, this work introduces Drive-JEPA, which combines Video JEPA for representation learning with multimodal trajectory distillation. The method adapts a Vision Transformer encoder pretrained on driving videos to generate trajectory-aligned representations, then uses a proposal-centric planner that distills diverse simulator-generated and human trajectories via a momentum-aware selection mechanism to encourage safe and stable behavior. Experimental results on NAVSIM show that the V-JEPA representation alone improves performance by 3 PDMS over prior methods in perception-free settings, while the full Drive-JEPA framework achieves 93.3 PDMS on v1 and 87.8 EPDMS on v2, setting a new state-of-the-art.
为了解决自动驾驶中视频预训练效果有限及人类轨迹稀疏导致的多模态行为学习困难问题，该研究提出Drive-JEPA框架，结合视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA）与多模态轨迹蒸馏。方法首先将ViT编码器在大规模驾驶视频上预训练，生成与轨迹规划对齐的表示；随后引入以提案为中心的规划器，通过动量感知选择机制蒸馏模拟器生成与人类轨迹，以促进安全稳定行为。实验表明，V-JEPA表示在无感知设置下比现有方法提升3 PDMS，完整Drive-JEPA框架在v1和v2版本上分别达到93.3 PDMS和87.8 EPDMS，刷新当前最优性能。
A Wearable Device Dataset for Mental Health Assessment Using Laser Doppler Flowmetry and Fluorescence Spectroscopy Sensors
Authors: Minh Ngoc Nguyen, Khai Le-Duc, Tan-Hanh Pham, Trong Nhan Nguyen, Bailey Trang, Ba Kien Tran, Viktor Dremin, Sergei Sokolovsky, Edik Rafailov, Truong-Son Hy
First: 2025-02-03T00:51:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:33:50+00:00
Comments: Communications Medicine 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Mental health problems such as stress, anxiety, and depression affect millions of people worldwide. These conditions are usually assessed using questionnaires, which rely on how people describe their own feelings. In this study, we explore whether a wearable device can help measure mental health using physical signals from the body. The device records small changes in blood flow and tissue activity from the fingertip. We collected data from 132 adults across 19 countries and compared these signals with mental health questionnaire results. We found that patterns in blood flow and tissue activity are linked to stress-related symptoms. This approach may help develop new tools for simple, non-invasive mental health monitoring in everyday life. Code and datasets are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/Wearable_LDF-FS
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于激光多普勒血流仪和荧光光谱传感器的可穿戴设备心理健康评估数据集
压力、焦虑和抑郁等心理健康问题影响全球数百万人。这些状况通常通过问卷调查评估，依赖个人对自身感受的描述。本研究探讨可穿戴设备是否能通过身体生理信号测量心理健康。该设备记录指尖微小的血流和组织活动变化。我们从19个国家的132名成年人收集数据，并将这些信号与心理健康问卷结果进行比较。研究发现，血流和组织活动模式与压力相关症状相关。该方法可能有助于开发简单、无创的日常心理健康监测工具。代码和数据集公开可用：https://github.com/leduckhai/Wearable_LDF-FS

Using laser Doppler flowmetry and fluorescence spectroscopy to capture fingertip blood flow and tissue activity, the researchers collected data from 132 adults across 19 countries and correlated it with standardized mental health questionnaires. Results showed statistically significant associations between specific patterns in blood flow and tissue activity and stress-related symptoms, suggesting the potential for non-invasive, continuous mental health monitoring in daily life.

Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP
Authors: Thomas Winninger
First: 2026-07-02T16:31:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:31:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, Seoul, South Korea, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过RFM-AGOP快速识别多维拒绝子空间
在大型语言模型（LLMs）中，引导和监控激活状态日益用于安全性和可解释性。早期研究假设行为沿单一线性方向编码，但近期发现复杂行为（如拒绝回答有害查询）存在于多维子空间中。然而，现有提取这些子空间的方法计算成本高，对生成长推理轨迹的推理模型尤为不利。通过将可高效计算的递归特征机（RFM）算法与探针引导初始化相结合，我们能在数秒内识别推理模型（Qwen 3）和非推理模型（Qwen 2.5）的多维拒绝子空间。RFM不仅加速子空间识别，还在消融任务中表现优于其他方法。未来计划进一步研究不同方法所发现子空间之间的关系。若验证成立，RFM可作为现有LLM子空间提取方法的低成本、可扩展补充。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently monitor and steer harmful behaviors in large language models, this work addresses the challenge that refusal behaviors reside in multi-dimensional subspaces rather than single linear directions. It adapts the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm, leveraging probe-informed initialization to enable fast, scalable identification of these subspaces. Experiments on both reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models demonstrate that RFM can extract refusal subspaces in seconds, outperforming alternatives in ablation tasks while maintaining computational efficiency.
为高效监控和引导大语言模型中的有害行为，本研究针对拒绝响应等复杂行为存在于多维子空间而非单一方向的问题，提出对递归特征机（RFM）算法进行改进，结合探针引导初始化以提升计算效率。实验表明，该方法可在数秒内完成对推理型（Qwen 3）与非推理型（Qwen 2.5）模型的拒绝子空间提取，并在消融任务中优于现有方法，具备良好的可扩展性。
The Token Not Taken: Sampling, State, and the Stochasticity of AI Agents
Authors: Muhammad Zia Hydari, Raja Iqbal
First: 2026-06-08T03:53:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:28:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI systems can behave differently across runs: the same request may produce a different plan, a different tool call, a different code edit, or a different final answer. Such variability arises from several layers that are often conflated. At the core of many current agents is a foundation model, a large pretrained model adaptable to many downstream tasks, embedded in an orchestration loop that plans, calls tools, observes results, and updates state. One explicit intrinsic source of variability in such systems is token generation: the model computes scores over possible next tokens, the scores are converted into probabilities, and a decoder may sample tokens using a pseudo-random number generator. A small sampled token difference can then cascade downstream into a different tool call, code path, search query, or agent state. Other sources of variability are extrinsic to token sampling, including changing environments, live data, serving infrastructure, batch effects, and numerical details. By separating these layers, this tutorial clarifies what it means to call agentic AI systems stochastic, when such variability can be reproduced under matched conditions, and why deterministic execution need not imply identical behavior in deployed settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未被选中的令牌：采样、状态与AI代理的随机性
代理型AI系统在不同运行中可能表现不同：相同的请求可能产生不同的计划、不同的工具调用、不同的代码修改或不同的最终答案。这种变异性源于多个常被混淆的层次。当前许多代理的核心是一个基础模型——一个可适应多种下游任务的大型预训练模型，嵌入在一个编排循环中，负责规划、调用工具、观察结果并更新状态。此类系统中一个明确的内在变异性来源是令牌生成：模型计算可能下一个令牌的分数，分数转换为概率，解码器可能使用伪随机数生成器采样令牌。一个小的采样令牌差异可能随后级联导致不同的工具调用、代码路径、搜索查询或代理状态。其他变异性来源则与令牌采样无关，包括变化的环境、实时数据、服务基础设施、批处理效应和数值细节。通过分离这些层次，本教程阐明了称代理型AI系统为随机性的含义，何时在匹配条件下可重现此类变异性，以及为何确定性执行并不意味着在部署环境中行为完全一致。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the stochastic behavior of agentic AI systems, motivated by the observation that identical inputs can yield different outputs due to layered sources of variability. The authors analyze how token sampling—where models probabilistically select next tokens using pseudo-random generators—acts as a core source of divergence, which can cascade into different tool calls, code paths, or agent states. They further distinguish this intrinsic variability from extrinsic factors like environment changes, live data, and infrastructure effects. Experimental findings confirm that while deterministic token sampling can reproduce behavior under controlled conditions, real-world deployment often introduces additional stochasticity, making identical behavior unattainable even with deterministic execution.
本文探讨了代理型AI系统为何在相同输入下运行结果不一致，通过分层分析揭示了多种随机性来源。其中，基础模型中基于概率的词元采样是核心内在随机源，可能导致下游行为如工具调用或代码路径的差异。文章同时区分了外部随机因素，如环境变化、实时数据或基础设施影响。通过这种分层解析，论文阐明了在可控条件下随机行为可复现的条件，并解释了为何确定性执行在实际部署中并不保证行为完全一致。
WikiCLIP: An Efficient Contrastive Baseline for Open-domain Visual Entity Recognition
Authors: Shan Ning, Longtian Qiu, Jiaxuan Sun, Xuming He
First: 2026-03-10T17:18:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:25:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR26, codes and weights are publicly available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Open-domain visual entity recognition (VER) seeks to associate images with entities in encyclopedic knowledge bases such as Wikipedia. Recent generative methods tailored for VER demonstrate strong performance but incur high computational costs, limiting their scalability and practical deployment. In this work, we revisit the contrastive paradigm for VER and introduce WikiCLIP, a simple yet effective framework that establishes a strong and efficient baseline for open-domain VER. WikiCLIP leverages large language model embeddings as knowledge-rich entity representations and enhances them with a Vision-Guided Knowledge Adaptor (VGKA) that aligns textual semantics with visual cues at the patch level. To further encourage fine-grained discrimination, a Hard Negative Synthesis Mechanism generates visually similar but semantically distinct negatives during training. Experimental results on popular open-domain VER benchmarks, such as OVEN, demonstrate that WikiCLIP significantly outperforms strong baselines. Specifically, WikiCLIP achieves a 16\% improvement on the challenging OVEN unseen set, while reducing inference latency by nearly 100 times compared with the leading generative model, AutoVER. The project page is available at https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiCLIP/
中文标题/摘要
标题：WikiCLIP：面向开放域视觉实体识别的高效对比基线
开放域视觉实体识别（VER）旨在将图像与维基百科等百科知识库中的实体关联。近期为VER定制的生成方法虽表现强劲，但计算成本高昂，限制了其可扩展性和实际部署。本文重新审视VER的对比范式，提出WikiCLIP——一个简洁而有效的框架，为开放域VER建立了强大且高效的基线。WikiCLIP利用大型语言模型嵌入作为富含知识的实体表示，并通过视觉引导知识适配器（VGKA）在图像块级别对齐文本语义与视觉线索。为促进细粒度判别，引入困难负样本合成机制，在训练中生成视觉相似但语义不同的负样本。在OVEN等主流开放域VER基准测试中，实验结果表明WikiCLIP显著优于强基线方法。具体而言，WikiCLIP在具有挑战性的OVEN未见集上性能提升16%，推理延迟较领先生成模型AutoVER降低近100倍。项目页面见https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiCLIP/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of recent generative methods for open-domain visual entity recognition, this work revisits contrastive learning and proposes WikiCLIP, a lightweight framework that uses large language model embeddings as entity representations and enhances them with a Vision-Guided Knowledge Adaptor to align visual and textual features at the patch level. It further improves discrimination through a Hard Negative Synthesis Mechanism that generates semantically distinct but visually similar negatives during training. Experiments on OVEN show WikiCLIP achieves a 16% performance gain on the unseen set while reducing inference latency by nearly 100 times compared to the state-of-the-art generative model AutoVER.
为解决当前开放域视觉实体识别方法计算成本过高的问题，本文重新审视对比学习范式，提出WikiCLIP框架，利用大语言模型嵌入作为实体表征，并通过视觉引导知识适配器在图像块级别对齐图文语义。训练时引入困难负样本合成机制，生成视觉相似但语义不同的负样本以增强判别能力。在OVEN数据集上实验表明，WikiCLIP在未见集上性能提升16%，推理延迟比当前最优生成模型AutoVER降低近100倍。
WattGPU: Predicting Inference Power and Latency on Unseen GPUs and LLMs
Authors: Mauricio Fadel Argerich, Jonathan Fürst, Marta Patiño-Martínez
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2026-07-02T16:25:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:25:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted at 1st Workshop on Sustainability and Resource-Efficiency of Artificial Intelligence @ IJCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) inference workloads are a rapidly growing contributor to data center energy consumption. Optimizing these deployments requires matching specific LLMs to the most efficient GPUs, but operators currently lack the tools to do so without exhaustively profiling each combination. While some predictive models exist, they still require profiling data and struggle to generalize to hardware unseen during training. To address this, we introduce \textit{WattGPU}, featuring two predictive models for mean GPU power draw and Inter-Token Latency (ITL). Our approach leverages only publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, eliminating the need for hardware access or profiling while enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA server-grade GPUs and LLMs. We evaluate our models using rigorous leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation on a dataset of 42 open-source LLMs (0.1B--27B parameters) and 8 GPUs under both offline and server scenarios. The mean power draw model achieves a median absolute percentage error of $\leq3.4\%$ for offline and $\leq13.5\%$ for server scenarios on unseen GPUs, while the latency model achieves $\leq8.5\%$ in server mode, both maintaining strong GPU ranking correlations for server scenarios (Kendall $τ\geq0.76$). Compared to standard physically grounded baselines -- Load-Scaled Thermal Design Power (TDP) for power draw and roofline for latency -- our models reduce median absolute percentage error by approximately 4$\times$ on unseen LLM-GPU combinations for server scenarios or approximately 2$\times$ for completely unseen GPUs. WattGPU's data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WattGPU：预测未见GPU和大语言模型的推理功耗与延迟
大语言模型（LLM）推理工作负载正迅速成为数据中心能耗的主要贡献者。优化这些部署需要将特定LLM与最高效的GPU匹配，但当前运营商缺乏无需对每种组合进行详尽性能分析即可完成此任务的工具。虽然存在一些预测模型，但它们仍需性能分析数据，并难以泛化到训练期间未见过的硬件。为解决此问题，我们引入了\textit{WattGPU}，包含两个预测模型，分别用于预测平均GPU功耗和跨Token延迟（ITL）。我们的方法仅依赖公开的LLM元数据和GPU规格，无需硬件访问或性能分析，同时支持泛化至未见的NVIDIA服务器级GPU和LLM。我们在包含42个开源LLM（0.1B至27B参数）和8种GPU的数据集上，通过严格的“留一GPU”和“留一LLM”交叉验证，在离线和服务器两种场景下评估了模型性能。平均功耗模型在未见GPU上的中位绝对百分比误差分别为离线场景≤3.4%、服务器场景≤13.5%；延迟模型在服务器模式下误差≤8.5%，且在服务器场景下均保持强GPU排序相关性（Kendall τ≥0.76）。与标准物理基础基线（功耗使用Load-Scaled TDP，延迟使用roofline）相比，我们的模型在未见LLM-GPU组合的服务器场景下将中位绝对百分比误差降低约4倍，或在完全未见GPU时降低约2倍。WattGPU的数据和代码已公开于https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the urgent need to optimize energy use in data centers hosting large language model (LLM) inference workloads, WattGPU introduces two predictive models that estimate GPU power draw and inter-token latency without requiring hardware profiling or access. The models rely solely on publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA GPUs and LLMs. Evaluated via leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation across 42 LLMs and 8 GPUs, the power model achieves median absolute percentage errors of ≤3.4% (offline) and ≤13.5% (server), while the latency model reaches ≤8.5% in server mode, with strong GPU ranking correlations (Kendall τ ≥ 0.76). Compared to baselines like TDP and roofline, WattGPU reduces errors by 4× for unseen LLM-GPU pairs and 2× for completely unseen GPUs.
为应对大型语言模型推理工作负载在数据中心中日益增长的能耗问题，WattGPU提出两种无需硬件访问或性能剖析即可预测GPU功耗与跨token延迟的模型，仅依赖公开的LLM元数据和GPU规格。在包含42个开源LLM和8个GPU的离线与服务器场景交叉验证中，功耗模型在未见GPU上的中位绝对百分比误差分别≤3.4%和≤13.5%，延迟模型在服务器模式下≤8.5%，且服务器场景下GPU排序相关性（Kendall τ）≥0.76。相比传统物理模型（如TDP和roofline），WattGPU在未见LLM-GPU组合上误差降低约4倍，在完全未见GPU上降低约2倍。
DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation
Authors: Juliette Decugis, Fabian Gloeckle, Francis Bach, Taco Cohen, Gabriel Synnaeve
First: 2026-07-02T16:25:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:25:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecompRL：通过学习模块化代码生成解决更难的问题
大型语言模型（LLM）如何解决当前无法处理的问题？重复采样虽可扩展测试时计算量，但GPU成本随尝试次数线性增长；而使用可验证奖励的强化学习（RL）虽能提升单次尝试的准确性，却牺牲了样本多样性。当基础策略几乎不可能生成正确解时，这两种策略均告失败：无论采样多少次或梯度信号多强，都无法克服搜索空间过大这一根本问题。我们采取不同策略：不增加难度，而是通过将问题分解为可独立求解的子函数，并重新组合其实现，使任务更易处理。由于现成模型未针对模块化生成进行训练，我们提出DecompRL，一种显式学习分解并实现分层代码结构的强化学习算法。将n个模块的k种实现重新组合，最多可生成k^n个候选解，从而将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价CPU评估，降低GPU token成本约50倍。在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests（Qwen~2.5~7B、Code World Model~32B）上，DecompRL在每题超10^5 token时超越标准及多样性优化的RL基线，解决标准生成无法触及的问题。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation that Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle with complex problems due to insufficient probability of generating correct solutions, the authors propose DecompRL, an RL algorithm that learns to decompose tasks into modular sub-functions and recombine their implementations. Instead of relying on repeated sampling or reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards—which fail when the base policy has near-zero success probability—DecompRL shifts the problem-solving bottleneck from expensive GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation by generating modular code components. Experimental results on LiveCodeBench and CodeContests show that DecompRL significantly outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond 10^5 tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot, while reducing GPU token cost by approximately 50 times.
为解决大语言模型（LLM）因基础策略生成正确解的概率极低而难以应对难题的问题，作者提出DecompRL算法，该算法通过学习将复杂任务分解为可独立求解的子函数，并重组其实现来解决问题。与依赖重复采样或标准强化学习（RL）相比，DecompRL通过模块化代码生成将计算瓶颈从昂贵的GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估，从而大幅降低GPU token成本约50倍。在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上的实验表明，当每题消耗超过10^5 tokens时，DecompRL显著优于标准和多样性优化的RL基线，解决了标准生成无法触及的问题。
Steerability via constraints: a substrate for scalable oversight of coding agents
Authors: Thomas Winninger
First: 2026-07-02T16:24:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:24:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Deep Learning for Code Workshop at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, Seoul, South Korea, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coding agents are capable; human oversight is the bottleneck. Unconstrained agents introduce security risks, erode codebase scalability, and make human review increasingly costly. We argue that the same methods used for decades to manage large human engineering teams: access control, network policies, strict coding conventions enforced by tooling; transfer directly to coding agents, and are cheaper (in token) than recent agentic scaffolding. We sketch a start-to-end system on this principle, and report a controlled experiment in scalable oversight: a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) inspects a Python codebase containing 11 inserted backdoors. Recall rises from 54.5% (unconstrained, no tools) to 90.9% (constrained substrate plus a ~200-LoC `docs` CLI), with substrate and tools contributing independently. We choose Python deliberately: substrate-level oversight gains are largest where the language gives the fewest guarantees by default; the principles extend to languages like Rust.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过约束实现可控性：为代码代理提供可扩展监督的基础
代码代理能力强大，但人类监督是瓶颈。无约束代理会引入安全风险、削弱代码库可扩展性，并使人工审查成本日益高昂。我们认为，几十年来用于管理大型人类工程团队的方法——访问控制、网络策略、通过工具强制执行的严格编码规范——可直接应用于代码代理，且在token成本上比近期的代理框架更便宜。我们基于此原则草拟了一套端到端系统，并报告了一项可扩展监督的受控实验：小型审查员（Gemma 4 e4b）审查包含11个后门的Python代码库。召回率从无约束且无工具的54.5%提升至使用约束基础架构加约200行代码的`docs`命令行工具后的90.9%，基础架构与工具各自独立贡献。我们选择Python是出于刻意考虑：语言默认提供最少保障时，基础架构层面的监督收益最大；该原则可扩展至Rust等语言。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the challenge of scaling human oversight for autonomous coding agents, which currently suffer from security risks, poor codebase scalability, and high review costs when unconstrained. It proposes applying long-established human engineering practices—such as access control, network policies, and tool-enforced coding conventions—to coding agents, arguing these methods are more cost-effective in token usage than recent agentic scaffolding. The authors demonstrate this via a controlled experiment where a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) evaluated a Python codebase with 11 backdoors; recall improved from 54.5% to 90.9% when using a constrained substrate plus a 200-line CLI tool, with each component contributing independently. Python was chosen because its minimal default guarantees maximize the benefit of substrate-level oversight, though the principles are extendable to languages like Rust.
该论文指出，自主编程代理的部署瓶颈在于人工监督不足，因为无约束代理会带来安全风险和可扩展性问题。作者提出将传统工程监督方法——如访问控制、网络策略和强制编码规范——应用于编程代理，认为这些方法比近期的代理框架更经济高效。实验中，一个小型审查模型（Gemma 4 e4b）审查包含11个后门的Python代码库，当引入约束基底和约200行CLI工具后，召回率从54.5%提升至90.9%，且各组件独立贡献效果。选择Python是因为其默认保障最少，能最大化基底监督收益，且该方法可扩展至Rust等语言。
Bringing Agentic Search to Earth Observation Data Discovery
Authors: Minghan Yu, Youran Sun, Chugang Yi, Yixin Wen, Haizhao Yang
First: 2026-07-02T16:24:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:24:16+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
NASA and its data centers hold thousands of geoscience datasets and tools like Worldview, Giovanni, the Science Discovery Engine, and Harmony. Finding the right one is hard even for domain experts. We present an agentic search system, deployed as a public service for the geoscience community, that takes a natural-language research query and returns the matching datasets and tools. We demonstrate that, in the era of large language models, the latent value of knowledge graphs (KGs) can be substantially amplified through agentic search. From the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph (NASA EO-KG) we derive NASA-EO-Bench, an open benchmark of 47k query-dataset pairs (21k task-based queries). A neural scorer fine-tuned on NASA-EO-Bench beats cosine and BM25 baselines. Further combining it with BM25 via score fusion raises both Recall@10 (R@10) and MRR by over 5x. On top of this supervised pipeline, we add a zero-shot agentic reranking stage that, without any additional training, lifts MRR by 28% on a stratified N=200 subset, showing that LLM reasoning is complementary to supervised retrieval.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将代理搜索引入地球观测数据发现
NASA及其数据中心拥有数千个地球科学数据集和工具，如Worldview、Giovanni、科学发现引擎和Harmony。即使领域专家也难以找到合适的数据集。我们提出了一种代理搜索系统，作为面向地球科学社区的公共服务，该系统接受自然语言研究查询并返回匹配的数据集和工具。我们证明，在大语言模型时代，通过代理搜索可以显著放大知识图谱（KGs）的潜在价值。基于NASA地球观测知识图谱（NASA EO-KG），我们构建了NASA-EO-Bench，一个包含47,000个查询-数据集对（其中21,000个为任务导向查询）的开放基准。一个在NASA-EO-Bench上微调的神经评分器优于余弦和BM25基线。进一步将其与BM25结合进行评分融合，使Recall@10（R@10）和MRR提升超过5倍。在此监督管道之上，我们增加了一个零样本代理重排序阶段，无需额外训练，即可在分层N=200子集上将MRR提升28%，表明LLM推理与监督检索具有互补性。

Motivated by the difficulty domain experts face in finding relevant Earth observation datasets among NASA’s vast collection, the authors developed an agentic search system that translates natural language queries into precise dataset/tool matches. Adding a zero-shot agentic reranking stage using LLM reasoning further improves MRR by 28% on a subset, demonstrating the complementary value of LLMs to supervised retrieval.
为了解决领域专家在NASA海量地球观测数据中难以发现相关数据集的问题，作者提出了一种代理搜索系统，可将自然语言查询转化为精确的数据集/工具匹配。基于NASA地球观测知识图谱，他们构建了包含47k组查询-数据对的NASA-EO-Bench基准，并训练出优于余弦和BM25基线的神经评分器。将该评分器与BM25结合进行分数融合，使Recall@10和MRR提升超过5倍。在此监督管道基础上，增加一个无需额外训练的零样本代理重排序阶段，利用LLM推理能力在200样本子集上使MRR提升28%，表明LLM推理可有效补充监督检索。
Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-II: Representational Similarity Observatory
Authors: Kaustubh Kapil, Kishor P. Upla
First: 2026-07-02T16:22:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:22:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Vision Transformers have achieved remarkable success across computer vision and language applications, the geometric evolution of their internal representations throughout training remains insufficiently understood. Existing analyses primarily focus on attention mechanisms and downstream performance, leaving the evolution of representation geometry largely unexplored. In this work, we present Transformer Geometry Observatory-II (TGO-II), a representation geometry analysis framework designed to investigate how Transformer representations evolve during supervised training. TGO-II analyzes Vision Transformer (ViT-Small/16) representations using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), Singular Vector Canonical Correlation Analysis (SVCCA), Two-Nearest Neighbor Intrinsic Dimensionality (TwoNN-ID), and token covariance analysis. Our experiments reveal three key observations. First, both CKA and SVCCA progressively decrease throughout training, indicating increasing representational specialization across Transformer layers. Second, intrinsic dimensionality consistently increases before stabilizing, suggesting progressive expansion of the representation manifold into a larger set of locally accessible degrees of freedom. Third, token covariance and coupling analyses demonstrate that strong token interaction structure persists throughout training, challenging the hypothesis that increasing representational complexity arises primarily from progressive token independence. These findings suggest that representation complexity and layer specialization emerge simultaneously during training. Manifold expansion appears to occur without token decoupling. Together, these observations motivate a new hypothesis in which Vision Transformers increase representational complexity through progressively richer transformations while preserving strong token interaction structure during learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer几何观测站TGO-II：表征相似性观测站
尽管视觉Transformer在计算机视觉和语言应用中取得了显著成功，但其内部表征在训练过程中的几何演化仍不甚明了。现有分析主要聚焦于注意力机制和下游性能，对表征几何的演化研究尚属空白。本文提出Transformer几何观测站-II（TGO-II），一个用于研究Transformer表征在监督训练中如何演化的分析框架。TGO-II采用中心核对齐（CKA）、奇异向量典型相关分析（SVCCA）、两最近邻内在维度（TwoNN-ID）及token协方差分析，对视觉Transformer（ViT-Small/16）表征进行分析。实验揭示三个关键发现：第一，CKA与SVCCA在训练过程中持续下降，表明Transformer各层表征逐渐专业化；第二，内在维度持续上升后趋于稳定，暗示表征流形逐步扩展至更多局部可访问的自由度；第三，token协方差与耦合分析表明，强token交互结构贯穿训练始终，挑战了“表征复杂度主要源于token逐步独立”的假设。这些发现表明，表征复杂度与层间专业化在训练中同步出现，流形扩展似乎无需token解耦。上述观察共同提出新假设：视觉Transformer通过逐步更丰富的变换提升表征复杂度，同时在学习过程中保持强token交互结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding regarding how Vision Transformer representations evolve geometrically during training, this work introduces TGO-II, a framework that analyzes internal representation changes using CKA, SVCCA, TwoNN-ID, and token covariance. Experiments on ViT-Small/16 reveal that representational specialization increases across layers as CKA and SVCCA values decline, while intrinsic dimensionality rises before stabilizing, indicating manifold expansion. Crucially, token covariance analysis shows persistent token interactions throughout training, contradicting the assumption that representational complexity arises from token independence. These results suggest that complexity and specialization emerge together, with manifold growth occurring without decoupling tokens.
为理解视觉Transformer在训练过程中内部表示的几何演化，本文提出TGO-II框架，结合CKA、SVCCA、TwoNN-ID和令牌协方差分析方法进行研究。实验表明，CKA与SVCCA值随训练下降，反映层间表示逐渐专业化；内在维度先上升后稳定，说明表示流形持续扩展。更重要的是，令牌协方差分析显示令牌间强耦合结构贯穿始终，反驳了表示复杂度源于令牌独立性的假设。结果表明，复杂度与专业化同步出现，且无需令牌解耦，暗示学习过程中是更丰富的变换而非令牌独立性推动了表示能力提升。
Spintronics for image recognition: performance benchmarking via data-driven simulations
Authors: Anatole Moureaux, Chloé Chopin, Simon de Wergifosse, Laurent Jacques, Flavio Abreu Araujo
First: 2023-08-10T18:09:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:22:18+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a demonstration of image classification using an extreme learning machine (ELM) based on a unique simulated magnetic tunnel junction (MTJ) delayed in time. As the ground state of the MTJ is a magnetic vortex, we refer to it as a vortex-based spin-torque oscillator (STVO). The dynamics of the magnetic vortex is simulated with a model called the data-driven Thiele equation approach (DD-TEA). This allows to avoid the constraints associated with repeated experimental manipulation for hyperparameters search and benchmarking. We showcase the versatility of our implementation by using it successfully for classification tasks on the MNIST, EMNIST-letters and Fashion MNIST datasets. Through simulations, we show that within an ELM with a sufficient number of parameters, the performance reached using the STVO dynamics as a source of nonlinearity is equivalent to the ones obtained with classical software activation functions such as the reLU and the sigmoid. While achieving state-of-the-art accuracy levels on the MNIST dataset, our model's performance on EMNIST-letters and Fashion MNIST is lower due to the simplicity of the network architecture and the increased complexity of the data. We expect that the DD-TEA framework will enable the exploration of deeper and more complex STVO-based architectures, ultimately leading to improved classification accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于图像识别的自旋电子学：通过数据驱动模拟进行性能基准测试
我们展示了一种基于独特模拟磁隧道结（MTJ）时延的极限学习机（ELM）进行图像分类的方法。由于MTJ的基态为磁涡旋，我们称之为基于涡旋的自旋扭矩振荡器（STVO）。磁涡旋的动力学通过称为数据驱动Thiele方程方法（DD-TEA）的模型进行模拟，从而避免了在超参数搜索和基准测试中反复实验操作的约束。我们通过在MNIST、EMNIST-letters和Fashion MNIST数据集上成功完成分类任务，展示了该实现的多功能性。通过模拟，我们表明，在参数数量足够多的ELM中，使用STVO动力学作为非线性源所达到的性能，等同于使用经典软件激活函数（如ReLU和Sigmoid）所获得的性能。尽管在MNIST数据集上达到了最先进的准确率水平，但在EMNIST-letters和Fashion MNIST数据集上的性能较低，这是由于网络架构的简单性和数据复杂性的增加所致。我们预期DD-TEA框架将有助于探索更深层、更复杂的基于STVO的架构，最终提高分类准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of spintronic devices for neuromorphic computing, this study explores using a simulated magnetic tunnel junction with vortex dynamics as a source of nonlinearity in an extreme learning machine for image classification. The authors employ a data-driven Thiele equation approach to simulate the magnetic vortex without requiring physical experiments, enabling efficient hyperparameter search. Tested on MNIST, EMNIST-letters, and Fashion MNIST, the model achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on MNIST, matching classical activation functions like ReLU and sigmoid in performance, while underperforming on more complex datasets due to architectural simplicity. The framework is shown to be scalable and promising for future development of deeper spintronic neural networks.
为探索自旋电子器件在机器学习中的潜力，该研究利用模拟磁隧道结中磁涡旋动力学作为极端学习机的非线性激活源。作者采用数据驱动的Thiele方程方法模拟磁涡旋，避免重复物理实验，实现高效超参数调优。实验表明，基于STVO的ELM在MNIST数据集上性能可媲美ReLU和Sigmoid等经典激活函数，达到当前最优准确率，但在EMNIST-letters和Fashion MNIST等更复杂数据集上表现较弱，主要受限于网络架构的简单性。该框架被视作未来构建更深层自旋电子神经网络的基础。
Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks
Authors: Benjamin Nichols, Michael Schlichtkrull, Nedjma Ousidhoum
First: 2026-07-02T16:20:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:20:28+00:00
Comments: Code and Data: https://github.com/nedjmaou/mediaref
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：了解你的来源：面向媒体背景核查的公共知识库
基于大语言模型的检索增强生成（RAG）正越来越多地用于自动化事实核查（AFC）及相关任务。通过将LLM输出与检索到的证据相结合，RAG系统提供透明的解释，同时允许外部信息独立于基础模型进行更新。然而，现有方法通常假设检索到的证据是可靠的，而现实世界的信息可能存在冲突、过时，且可能来自不可靠或有偏见的来源。近期关于*来源批判性推理*的研究通过媒体背景核查（MBCs）（Schlichtkrull, 2024）应对这一挑战，评估证据来源的可信度以支持下游事实验证。然而，生成MBCs依赖昂贵的专有搜索API，限制了可复现性。为缓解此问题，我们引入MEDIAREF，一个公开的网络文档知识库，支持在200个媒体来源上以低成本、可复现的方式评估MBC生成。我们描述了构建和更新该集合的可复现方法，评估了广泛使用的LLM在MBC生成任务上的表现，并证明MEDIAREF通过自动和定性评估支持更高质量的MBC生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable source evaluation in LLM-based fact-checking systems, this work introduces MEDIAREF, a public knowledge store of web-sourced documents from 200 media outlets to enable reproducible and low-cost media background check (MBC) generation. The method involves constructing and maintaining a curated, updateable corpus of documents, then evaluating widely used LLMs on their ability to generate credible MBCs using this dataset. Experimental results show that MEDIAREF enables higher-quality MBC generation compared to proprietary alternatives, with both automatic metrics and qualitative assessments confirming improved source credibility evaluation.
为提升基于大语言模型的自动事实核查系统中证据来源的可信度评估能力，本文提出MEDIAREF，一个包含200家媒体来源的公开网络文档知识库，支持可复现且低成本的媒体背景检查（MBC）生成评估。方法包括构建并持续更新该文档集合，随后对主流大语言模型在MBC生成任务上的表现进行评估。实验结果表明，相比依赖专有搜索API，MEDIAREF显著提升MBC生成质量，自动指标与定性评估均证实其在源批判性推理方面的优越性。
Perception-aware Sampling for Scatterplot Visualizations
Authors: Zafeiria Moumoulidou, Hamza Elhamdadi, Ke Yang, Subrata Mitra, Cindy Xiong Bearfield, Alexandra Meliou
First: 2025-04-29T02:14:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:20:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visualizing data is often a crucial first step in data analytics workflows, but growing data sizes pose challenges due to computational and visual perception limitations. As a result, data analysts commonly down-sample their data and work with subsets. Deriving representative samples, however, remains a challenge. This paper focuses on scatterplots, a widely-used visualization type, and introduces a novel sampling objective -- perception-awareness -- aiming to improve sample efficacy by targeting humans' perception of a visualization. We make the following contributions: (1) We propose perception-augmented databases and design PAwS: a novel perception-aware sampling method for scatterplots that leverages saliency maps -- a computer vision tool for predicting areas of attention focus in visualizations -- and models perception-awareness via saliency, density, and coverage objectives. (2) We design ApproPAwS: a fast, perception-aware method for approximate visualizations, which exploits the fact that small visual perturbations are often imperceptible to humans. (3) We introduce the concept of perceptual similarity as a metric for sample quality, and present a novel method that compares saliency maps to measure it. (4) Our extensive experimental evaluation shows that our methods consistently outperform prior art in producing samples with high perceptual similarity, while ApproPAwS achieves up to 100x speed-ups with minimal loss in visual fidelity. Our user study shows that PAwS is often preferred by humans, validating our quantitative findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向感知的散点图采样方法
数据可视化通常是数据分析工作流程中的关键第一步，但日益增长的数据规模因计算和视觉感知限制而带来挑战。因此，数据分析师常对数据进行下采样并处理子集。然而，如何获得代表性样本仍是一个难题。本文聚焦于广泛使用的散点图可视化类型，提出了一种新颖的采样目标——感知意识，旨在通过针对人类对可视化的感知来提升样本效果。 我们做出以下贡献：(1) 我们提出感知增强数据库，并设计了PAwS：一种面向散点图的新型感知意识采样方法，该方法利用显著性图——一种计算机视觉工具，用于预测可视化中的注意力焦点区域——并通过显著性、密度和覆盖度目标建模感知意识。(2) 我们设计了ApproPAwS：一种快速的感知意识近似可视化方法，利用人类对微小视觉扰动通常不敏感的事实。(3) 我们引入感知相似性作为样本质量的度量，并提出一种新颖方法，通过比较显著性图来衡量它。(4) 我们的广泛实验评估表明，我们的方法在生成高感知相似度样本方面持续优于现有方法，而ApproPAwS可实现高达100倍的速度提升，且视觉保真度损失极小。用户研究表明，PAwS常被人类偏好，验证了我们的定量发现。
