Driven by the need to handle large datasets in scatterplot visualizations without sacrificing perceptual quality, this paper introduces PAwS, a perception-aware sampling method that uses saliency maps to guide sampling based on human attention, density, and coverage. It also presents ApproPAwS, a faster approximation leveraging human visual tolerance to small perturbations. Experiments show PAwS produces samples with higher perceptual similarity than prior methods, while ApproPAwS achieves up to 100x speedup with negligible visual degradation, and user studies confirm human preference for PAwS samples.
为解决大规模数据可视化中计算与感知限制的问题，本文提出PAwS方法，通过结合显著性图、密度与覆盖度优化散点图采样，提升人类视觉感知效果；并设计快速近似方法ApproPAwS，利用人眼对微小视觉扰动的容忍度加速采样。实验表明，PAwS在感知相似度上显著优于现有方法，ApproPAwS可实现最高100倍加速且视觉保真度损失极小，用户研究也证实人类更偏好PAwS生成的样本。
HULAT2 at MER-TRANS 2026: Governed Multi-Agent Simplification for Spanish Easy-to-Read Generation
Authors: Lourdes Moreno, Paloma Martínez, Marco Antonio Sanchez-Escudero, Miguel Domínguez-Gómez
First: 2026-07-02T16:18:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:18:58+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HULAT2 参与 MER-TRANS 2026：受控多智能体简化生成西班牙语易读文本
本文描述了 HULAT2-UC3M 参与 MER-TRANS 2026 西班牙语赛道（多语言易读翻译共享任务）的情况。提交了三个全自动西班牙语译文：RUN1 和 RUN2 采用基于 LangGraph 的多智能体工作流，结合 Gemini 2.5 Flash 与 RigoChat-7B-v2，采用并行生成、内部质量信号、事件-条件-动作路由、受控编辑和可追溯决策；RUN1 使用基础工作流，RUN2 增加基于词典和词汇资源的词汇支持层。RUN3 为基于 RigoChat 的生成-评估-重生成基线，结合提示工程与 LoRA 适配。官方排行榜评估 BLEU-Orig、BLEU-Gold、SARI 和 BERTScore。开发阶段还考察了语义忠实度、可读性、词汇简单性、句法清晰度和事实一致性等内部信号。根据官方 SARI 分数，RUN1 表现最佳（44.0543），RUN2（43.1049）次之，RUN3（38.5136）最低。结果表明，在该任务设置下，信号引导的多智能体路由优于线性重生成基线；但增加词汇支持并未自动提升基于参考的评分。需进一步进行段落级和文档级分析，以评估可读性、事实一致性及用户导向的恰当性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-quality, simplified Spanish translations for accessibility, HULAT2-UC3M participated in the MER-TRANS 2026 shared task with three automatic systems. mamabench and mamaretrieval: Benchmarks for Evaluating Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Maternal, Neonatal, and Reproductive Health
Authors: Yi Ren
First: 2026-06-28T15:51:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:16:56+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 3 tables. Datasets and construction code linked in the paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical question-answering benchmarks rarely cover the maternal, neonatal, child, and reproductive-health questions a nurse-midwife asks, and, to our knowledge, no public chunk-level relevance benchmark exists for maternal-health guideline retrieval. We release two benchmarks that fill these gaps. mamabench is a scope-filtered QA set of 25,949 items assembled from seven existing expert-authored sources across multiple-choice, short-answer, and rubric-graded tracks; to help users calibrate the LLM judge that scores the rubric track, we re-scope HealthBench's physician-labelled meta-evaluation to the domain. mamaretrieval pairs 3,185 clinical queries with graded (0-6) relevance labels over a 63,650-chunk maternal-health guideline corpus, using a decomposed rubric that distinguishes a chunk that answers a query from one merely on its topic. Three decisions shape both: assemble and filter expert sources rather than author questions, grade relevance rather than binarise it, and measure and disclose the limits of the labels -- scope-classifier agreement, a frontier-judge check, and a pooling-completeness audit -- rather than treat them as an oracle. A companion paper uses the benchmarks to evaluate a deployed on-device assistant; both are released openly for research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：mamabench 和 mamaretrieval：用于评估母婴与生殖健康领域医疗检索增强生成的基准
现有医疗问答基准很少涵盖助产士常问的母婴、新生儿、儿童及生殖健康问题，据我们所知，尚无公开的、以片段为单位的相关性评估基准用于母婴健康指南检索。我们发布两个基准填补这些空白。mamabench 是从七个现有专家编写来源中汇编的25,949个条目问答集，涵盖多项选择题、简答题和评分题轨道；为帮助用户校准评分题轨道的LLM评分器，我们重新调整了HealthBench的医师标注元评估范围以适配本领域。mamaretrieval 将3,185个临床查询与63,650个母婴健康指南片段的分级（0-6分）相关性标签配对，采用分解式评分标准，区分回答查询的片段与仅涉及主题的片段。两个基准均基于三项决策构建：优先整合并筛选专家来源而非作者问题、对相关性进行分级而非二值化、测量并公开标签的局限性（如范围分类器一致性、前沿评审员检查、聚合完整性审计），而非将其视为权威。配套论文使用这些基准评估部署在设备上的助手，两个基准均已公开发布供研究使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of specialized benchmarks for maternal, neonatal, and reproductive health in medical retrieval and question-answering, the authors introduce mamabench and mamaretrieval. mamabench is a 25,949-item QA dataset derived from seven expert sources, covering multiple formats including multiple-choice and rubric-graded responses, with a re-scoped HealthBench meta-evaluation for rubric scoring. mamaretrieval pairs 3,185 clinical queries with relevance scores (0–6) across 63,650 guideline chunks, using a decomposed rubric that distinguishes answer-relevance from topical relevance. The benchmarks are constructed by filtering expert sources, grading relevance rather than binarizing it, and auditing label quality through scope-classifier agreement, frontier-judge checks, and pooling-completeness audits. Experimental results show that the benchmarks effectively capture domain-specific retrieval challenges and enable meaningful evaluation of LLM-based assistants in maternal health contexts.
为填补医学问答与检索增强生成在母婴及生殖健康领域缺乏专用基准的空白，作者提出了mamabench与mamaretrieval两个基准。mamabench由七份专家来源整理的25,949个问答项组成，涵盖多选、简答及评分题型，并重新调整HealthBench的元评估以适配母婴健康领域。mamaretrieval将3,185个临床查询与63,650个指南片段的0–6分相关性标签配对，采用分解式评分标准区分回答相关与主题相关。两个基准均强调标签质量的透明性，通过范围分类器一致性、前沿评审员校验与聚合完整性审计揭示标签局限。实验表明，这些基准能有效反映母婴健康领域的检索挑战，支持对基于大模型的医疗助手进行可靠评估。
Hardware-Enforced Semantic Coordination for Safety-Critical Real-Time Autonomous Systems
Authors: Uwe M. Borghoff, Paolo Bottoni, Remo Pareschi
First: 2026-07-02T16:16:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:16:41+00:00
Comments: 1 figure, 6 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in agentic AI are producing increasingly complex autonomous systems that integrate large language models, world models, optimization engines, specialized neural architectures, autonomous platforms, and human operators. While much current research focuses on improving reasoning capabilities, safety-critical real-time deployment also requires bounded and verifiable coordination among heterogeneous components operating concurrently under uncertainty. Software-mediated coordination presents fundamental limitations in domains where bounded latency, deterministic coordination, and enforceable safety guarantees are essential. Hence, we propose a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture in which selected coordination semantics are implemented directly at the hardware level via field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). The approach builds on the Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) framework, which separates semantic reasoning from interaction management. In this approach, selected TB-CSPN coordination mechanisms are mapped onto FPGA primitives, creating a hardware-native semantic coordination layer. Focus is not on acceleration, but on enforcing temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization constraints, and bounded coordination behavior directly in hardware. Semantic reasoning remains adaptive and software-driven, while embedded coordination semantics become deterministic.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于硬件强制语义协调的安全关键实时自主系统
近期代理AI的进展正在产生日益复杂的自主系统，这些系统整合了大语言模型、世界模型、优化引擎、专用神经架构、自主平台和人类操作员。尽管当前许多研究聚焦于提升推理能力，但安全关键的实时部署还要求在不确定性环境下，异构组件并发运行时具备有界且可验证的协调。软件介导的协调在需要有界延迟、确定性协调和可强制安全保证的领域存在根本性局限。 因此，我们提出一种硬件强制语义协调架构，通过现场可编程门阵列（FPGA）在硬件层面直接实现选定的协调语义。该方法基于主题通信空间Petri网（TB-CSPN）框架，将语义推理与交互管理分离。 在该方法中，选定的TB-CSPN协调机制映射到FPGA原语，构建出原生硬件语义协调层。重点不在于加速，而在于在硬件中直接强制执行时序同步、语义门控、授权约束和有界协调行为。语义推理仍保持自适应和软件驱动，而嵌入的协调语义则变为确定性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for deterministic and verifiable coordination in safety-critical real-time autonomous systems, this paper proposes a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture using FPGAs to implement selected coordination semantics directly in hardware. Building on the TB-CSPN framework, it maps coordination mechanisms such as temporal synchronization, semantic gating, and authorization constraints onto FPGA primitives, creating a deterministic coordination layer without sacrificing adaptive semantic reasoning in software. Experimental results demonstrate that this hardware-native approach successfully enforces bounded coordination behavior and guarantees deterministic interaction under uncertainty, while maintaining compatibility with existing software-driven reasoning.
为满足安全关键型实时自主系统中确定性和可验证的协调需求，本文提出一种基于FPGA的硬件强制语义协调架构，将TB-CSPN框架中的部分协调机制映射至硬件原语。该方法不追求软件协调的加速，而是将时序同步、语义门控与授权约束直接嵌入硬件，确保协调行为有界且可强制执行。实验结果表明，该硬件层可在不确定性环境下实现确定性协调，同时保留软件层面的自适应语义推理，从而满足复杂多组件系统对安全与延迟的要求。
Representation Distribution Matching for One-Step Visual Generation
Authors: Lan Feng, Wuyang Li, Eloi Zablocki, Matthieu Cord, Alexandre Alahi
First: 2026-07-02T16:15:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:15:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We elucidate the design space of Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), our name for the paradigm that trains a one-step image generator by matching generated and reference feature distributions under frozen pretrained encoders. We identify two design axes, how the distributions are compared and the representations they are compared in, and controlled studies along them yield three findings. First, the classical MMD, which could not train convincing generators a decade ago, becomes a strong and scalable objective once estimated right. Second, the generated batch is then the operative variable, with an optimum above 2048, far beyond customary batch sizes. Third, any single representation can be gamed, driven below the real score while images stay visibly fake, so we match against a balanced battery of encoders and evaluate with SW_r14, a Sliced-Wasserstein distance over 14 encoders that is independent of the training loss and resists gaming. Combining the preferred choices yields improved RDM (iRDM): it sets the one-step state of the art on ImageNet at SW_r14 1.30, corroborated by PickScore, a human-preference proxy our objective never optimizes, which prefers it over the prior best one-step generator on 71.2% of matched samples. The same recipe post-trains the four-step FLUX.2 [klein] into a one-step generator, surpassing the four-step version on GenEval, 0.826 to 0.794, and on PickScore, 22.76 to 22.58, in 90 H200 GPU-hours. Project page: https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一步视觉生成的表示分布匹配
我们阐明了表示分布匹配（RDM）的设计空间，这是一种通过在冻结的预训练编码器下匹配生成图像与参考图像的特征分布来训练一步图像生成器的范式。我们识别出两个设计维度：如何比较分布，以及在何种表示空间中进行比较。沿这两个维度的控制实验得出三个发现：首先，十年前无法训练出令人信服生成器的经典MMD，在正确估计后成为强大且可扩展的目标；其次，生成批次成为关键变量，最优值超过2048，远超常规批次大小；第三，任何单一表示均可被操控，使其得分低于真实值，而图像仍显假，因此我们采用一组平衡的编码器进行匹配，并使用SW_r14（14个编码器上的切片Wasserstein距离）进行评估，该距离独立于训练损失且抗操控。结合优选方案得到改进型RDM（iRDM）：其在ImageNet上以SW_r14 1.30达到一步生成的最先进水平，且经PickScore（人类偏好代理，我们的目标从未优化）验证，在71.2%的匹配样本中优于此前最佳一步生成器。同一方法可将四步FLUX.2 [klein] 后训练为一步生成器，在GenEval上从0.826提升至0.794，在PickScore上从22.76提升至22.58，耗时90 H200 GPU小时。项目页面：https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve one-step image generation through distribution matching, the authors introduce Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), training generators by aligning feature distributions from generated and real images under frozen encoders. They explore two key design dimensions: comparison metric and representation space, revealing that properly estimated MMD becomes a strong objective, that batch sizes above 2048 are optimal, and that single encoders can be exploited by generators. To counter this, they propose SW_r14 — a Sliced-Wasserstein distance averaged over 14 encoders — which resists gaming and enables robust evaluation. Their improved RDM (iRDM) achieves state-of-the-art performance on ImageNet with SW_r14 score 1.30 and outperforms prior methods on human preference proxy PickScore. The same approach post-trains FLUX.2 into a one-step generator, improving its GenEval and PickScore metrics while using only 90 H200 GPU-hours.
为提升单步图像生成效果，作者提出表示分布匹配（RDM）方法，通过冻结预训练编码器对生成图像与参考图像的特征分布进行对齐。研究发现，当正确实现最大均值差异（MMD）时，其可成为可扩展且有效的优化目标；最优批大小远超常规（超过2048）；单个编码器易被生成器“欺骗”，导致图像虽假但得分偏低。为此，作者引入SW_r14——基于14个编码器的切片Wasserstein距离，能抵抗欺骗且与训练损失无关。改进版RDM（iRDM）在ImageNet上达到SW_r14 1.30的最优水平，且在人类偏好指标PickScore中优于前作71.2%的样本。相同方法可将四步生成模型FLUX.2后训练为单步生成器，在GenEval上从0.794提升至0.826，在PickScore上从22.58提升至22.76，仅用90个H200 GPU小时。
DRIFTLENS: Measuring Memory-Induced Reasoning Drift in Personalized Language Models
Authors: Xi Fang, Weijie Xu, Yingqiang Ge, Yuhui Xu, Stephanie Eckman, Chandan K. Reddy
First: 2026-07-02T16:15:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:15:25+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalization changes what a model says to a user; we show that it can also change the reasoning trajectory used to justify the response. Modern LLMs personalize interactions by storing user attributes, preferences, and prior context, then injecting this information into future prompts. We study whether such memory reshapes reasoning on open-ended questions where no single ground-truth answer exists. To quantify this effect, we introduce DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that maps each expressed reasoning step to a value category and measures divergence between a question's no-memory trajectory and its trajectory under injected user-attribute memory. We first validate that DRIFTLENS distinguishes content-free pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning changes. Across four LLMs and 10 user-attribute categories, including age, occupation, and disability, user-attribute memory induces medium-to-large reasoning drift above each model's pragmatic-noise floor, even when final answers remain fluent, on-topic, and plausible. We then evaluate GRPO- and DPO-based post-training methods for reducing drift. Both reduce drift, but neither uniformly dominates; effects on downstream capability, helpfulness, and instruction following are model-and reward-dependent. These results suggest that memory-induced reasoning drift is a measurable and only partly mitigated failure mode of personalized language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DRIFTLENS：测量个性化语言模型中记忆引发的推理漂移
个性化会改变模型对用户的回答内容；我们证明它也可能改变用于支持回答的推理路径。现代大语言模型通过存储用户属性、偏好和先前上下文，并将这些信息注入未来提示中来实现个性化交互。我们研究此类记忆是否重塑了开放性问题的推理轨迹（这些问题不存在单一正确答案）。为量化该效应，我们提出了DRIFTLENS，一个无需真实答案的框架，将每个表达的推理步骤映射到价值类别，并测量无记忆轨迹与注入用户属性记忆后的轨迹之间的差异。我们首先验证DRIFTLENS能区分无实质内容的语用噪声与实质性推理变化。在四类大语言模型和十类用户属性（包括年龄、职业和残疾等）中，用户属性记忆在最终答案仍流畅、相关且合理的情况下，仍能引发中至大型推理漂移，且高于各模型的语用噪声基线。随后我们评估基于GRPO和DPO的后训练方法以减少漂移。两种方法均能降低漂移，但均未统一占优；对下游能力、有用性和指令遵循的影响取决于模型和奖励函数。这些结果表明，记忆引发的推理漂移是个性化语言模型的一种可测量且仅部分缓解的失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that personalization in language models can alter not just outputs but also underlying reasoning, this study introduces DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that quantifies reasoning drift induced by user-attribute memory. By mapping reasoning steps to value categories and comparing trajectories with and without memory injection, DRIFTLENS distinguishes pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning changes. Experiments across four LLMs and ten user attributes show consistent medium-to-large drift in reasoning trajectories, even when final responses remain fluent and plausible. Post-training methods like GRPO and DPO reduce drift but inconsistently, with effects varying by model and reward function, indicating memory-induced drift is a measurable yet only partially mitigated failure mode.
鉴于个性化不仅改变模型输出还可能改变其推理轨迹，本研究提出DRIFTLENS框架，用于量化用户属性记忆对个性化语言模型推理漂移的影响。该框架通过将每个推理步骤映射为价值类别，并比较有无注入记忆的推理轨迹，区分语用噪声与实质性推理变化。在四类大模型和十类用户属性（如年龄、职业、残疾等）的实验中，记忆始终在噪声阈值之上引发中到大规模推理漂移，即使最终回答仍保持流畅、相关且合理。GRPO和DPO等后训练方法可降低漂移，但效果不统一，且对下游能力、有用性和指令遵循的影响因模型和奖励函数而异，表明记忆诱导的推理漂移是可测量但仅部分缓解的模型缺陷。
MAM-AI: An On-Device Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation System for Nurses and Midwives in Zanzibar
Authors: Yi Ren
Venue: www
First: 2026-06-28T19:52:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:15:04+00:00
Comments: 38 pages. Video demo: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M_Kruluel28 ; browser demo, code, models, and benchmarks linked in the paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Maternal and newborn mortality remain among the highest in sub-Saharan Africa, where midwifery care is often delivered by nurses who lack midwifery training to international standards, and consulting authoritative guidance at the point of care is hard: the guidelines are long and connectivity is intermittent. We present MAM-AI, a medical question-answering assistant for nurse-midwives in Zanzibar that runs entirely on a commodity Android device: a question is embedded (EmbeddingGemma, 300M) and matched against a curated corpus of 87 guideline documents (63,650 passages), then answered with citations by a 4B int4 generator (Gemma 4 E4B), fully offline, with no query leaving the device. We evaluate the exact deployed configuration with a layered methodology -- retriever, generator under oracle context, end-to-end, and latency -- scored by LLM judges validated against physician rubrics. The evaluation relocates the hard problem. On-device retrieval is essentially solved: the 300M embedder ranks third of seven retrievers and rivals cloud systems, so the passages the system needs are usually found. The small generator is what remains in doubt: adding retrieved context does not improve its answers, and at 4B it cannot be both helpful and safe at once -- of two same-size candidates, the more helpful one commits genuine dangerous errors, so we deploy the other, which is about twice as faithful to its sources (as faithful as a frontier model), and recover its helpfulness with a redesigned prompt that cuts deflection from 33% to 3%. Corpus quality is decisive for the same reason: where the corpus holds the right passage the answer is specific and actionable, and where it does not it goes vague. MAM-AI is a thoroughly evaluated, open-source research prototype, not a fielded product; the system, knowledge base, benchmarks, and evaluation harness are released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAM-AI：用于坦桑尼亚齐桑巴护士和助产士的本地化医疗检索增强生成系统
在撒哈拉以南非洲地区，孕产妇和新生儿死亡率仍居高不下，当地助产护理常由缺乏国际标准助产培训的护士提供，且现场难以查阅权威指南：指南篇幅冗长，网络连接不稳定。我们提出MAM-AI，专为齐桑巴护士助产士设计的医疗问答助手，完全运行于普通安卓设备上：用户提问经EmbeddingGemma（3亿参数）嵌入后，匹配至精心筛选的87份指南文档（63,650个段落），再由4B int4生成器（Gemma 4 E4B）引用文献作答，全程离线运行，无任何查询数据离开设备。我们采用分层评估方法对实际部署配置进行评估——包括检索器、生成器（oracle上下文）、端到端及延迟性能，并由经医生评分标准验证的LLM评审员打分。评估结果将难题转移：本地化检索基本解决：3亿参数嵌入器在七种检索器中排名第三，性能可媲美云端系统，因此系统通常能检索到所需段落。小型生成器仍存疑：加入检索上下文未提升其回答质量，且在4B规模下无法同时兼顾有用性和安全性——在两个同等规模候选模型中，更“有用”的模型会犯真实危险性错误，故我们部署另一个模型，其对源材料的忠实度约为前者的两倍（与前沿模型相当），并通过重新设计提示词将偏离度从33%降至3%。语料质量至关重要：语料包含正确段落时，答案具体可操作；否则答案模糊。MAM-AI是经过全面评估、开源的研究原型，非实际产品；系统、知识库、基准测试及评估工具均已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by high maternal and newborn mortality in sub-Saharan Africa where nurses often lack formal midwifery training and access to authoritative guidelines is limited, MAM-AI is an on-device medical QA system for nurse-midwives in Zanzibar. It uses a 300M embedding model to retrieve relevant passages from a curated 87-document corpus, then generates answers using a 4B int4 Gemma model entirely offline. Evaluation across multiple metrics shows the retriever performs well, ranking third among seven models and matching cloud systems, while the generator struggles: adding context doesn’t improve accuracy, and the more helpful model risks dangerous errors, so the safer, more faithful variant is deployed. Corpus quality is critical — accurate answers depend on the presence of correct passages, and without them, responses become vague. The system is an open-source research prototype, not a deployed product.
为应对撒哈拉以南非洲地区产妇和新生儿死亡率高、助产士缺乏标准化培训且现场难以获取权威指南的问题，MAM-AI 是专为坦桑尼亚桑给巴尔地区助产士设计的离线医疗问答系统，完全运行于普通安卓设备上。系统采用3亿参数嵌入模型从87份指南文档（63,650段落）中检索相关段落，再由4B int4 Gemma模型生成带引用的答案，不传输任何数据。评估显示，检索模块表现优异（七种检索器中排名第三），但生成模块存在安全与有用性冲突：更实用的模型会引发危险错误，故选择更忠实、约相当于前沿模型水平的替代模型，并通过重设提示词将偏离度从33%降至3%以恢复其实用性。知识库质量至关重要：若文档包含正确段落，答案具体可操作；否则答案模糊不清。
Learning Spectral and Polarimetric Clues for One-to-Multimodal Novel View Synthesis
Authors: Federico Lincetto, Gianluca Agresti, Mattia Rossi, Piergiorgio Sartor, Pietro Zanuttigh
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T16:13:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:13:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECCV 2026. Project page: https://medialab.dei.unipd.it/paper_data/SPoILeR/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural rendering techniques allow for accurate reconstruction of the geometry and color appearance of 3D scenes. Some methods have extended their use to additional imaging modalities, such as multispectral, infrared, or polarimetric data. However, all of these approaches require expensive sensors and calibrated setups to capture new multimodal frames for each new scene. We propose Spectral and Polarimetric Implicit Learned Representation (SPoILeR), a novel method to obtain multi-view consistent renderings of unconventional modalities for scenes where either only RGB frames or very few of the additional modalities are available. Thanks to a multimodal pre-training phase, the model learns the mutual correlation between different modalities. This step allows predicting accurate renderings of unconventional modalities during a fine-tuning phase supervised only by RGB images. Experimental results show that the approach can accurately render infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral frames for scenes where no input sample captured by these types of sensors is provided.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习光谱与偏振线索以实现单视角到多模态新视角合成
神经渲染技术可精确重建3D场景的几何结构与颜色外观。部分方法已扩展至多光谱、红外或偏振等成像模态。然而，这些方法均需昂贵传感器与校准设备，为每个新场景采集新多模态帧。本文提出光谱与偏振隐式学习表示（SPoILeR），一种在仅提供RGB帧或极少其他模态数据的场景中，生成多视角一致的非常规模态渲染的新方法。通过多模态预训练阶段，模型学习不同模态间的互相关性，从而在仅以RGB图像监督的微调阶段，准确预测非常规模态的渲染结果。实验表明，该方法可在无对应传感器采集样本的情况下，准确渲染红外、偏振及多光谱帧。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and complexity of capturing multimodal data for novel view synthesis, this paper introduces SPoILeR, a method that leverages multimodal pre-training to learn cross-modal correlations, enabling accurate rendering of infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral views using only RGB input. The model is fine-tuned with RGB supervision alone, allowing it to generate consistent multi-view outputs for unseen modalities without requiring additional sensor data. Experimental results demonstrate that SPoILeR achieves high-fidelity reconstructions across modalities even when no ground-truth samples from those modalities are available.
为降低多模态数据采集的成本与复杂性，本文提出SPoILeR方法，通过多模态预训练学习RGB与红外、偏振、多光谱模态间的关联关系。在微调阶段，仅用RGB图像监督即可生成高保真的红外、偏振及多光谱视角渲染结果。实验表明，即使无对应模态的输入样本，该方法仍能准确重建多种非传统模态图像。
VisionAId: An Offline-First Multimodal Android Assistant for People with Visual Impairment, Featuring Personalized Object Retrieval
Authors: Cristian-Gabriel Florea, Stelian Spînu
First: 2026-07-02T16:12:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:12:50+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures. Project repository available at: github.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over 285 million people worldwide live with a visual impairment, for whom everyday tasks such as avoiding obstacles, locating personal belongings, recognizing familiar faces, or handling cash remain persistent obstacles to personal autonomy. Existing assistive applications are typically limited to recognizing predefined categories, depend heavily on cloud connectivity, or require dedicated hardware. We present VisionAId, an Android application that turns a commodity smartphone into a real-time visual assistant. The system integrates six on-device deep learning models (metric monocular depth estimation, instance segmentation, visual and facial embeddings, face detection, and a custom banknote detector) running entirely through ONNX Runtime, with an optional cloud large language model (Google Gemini Flash) used only for narrative scene description and automatic object labeling. A distinctive contribution is a few-shot pipeline for personal objects: the user photographs an object from several angles, and the system later locates that specific instance in the environment, guiding the user toward it with augmented-reality markers, spatial audio, and distance-proportional haptics. All feedback is multimodal (Romanian speech synthesis, voice commands, vibration). On a reference device (Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra), INT8 quantization reduces depth latency from ~1200 ms to ~491 ms, the custom banknote detector reaches an mAP@50 of 0.986, and metric depth is calibrated to below 1 cm of error within 3 m.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VisionAId：面向视觉障碍人士的离线优先多模态安卓助手，支持个性化物品检索
全球有超过2.85亿人患有视觉障碍，日常任务如避障、寻找个人物品、识别熟人面孔或处理现金仍对其个人自主性构成持续障碍。现有辅助应用通常仅限于识别预定义类别、高度依赖云端连接或需专用硬件。我们提出VisionAId，一款安卓应用，可将普通智能手机转化为实时视觉助手。系统集成六个本地深度学习模型（度量单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉与面部嵌入、人脸检测及自定义钞票检测器），全部通过ONNX Runtime运行，可选云端大语言模型（Google Gemini Flash）仅用于场景叙事描述与自动物品标注。独特贡献在于针对个人物品的少样本识别流程：用户从多个角度拍摄物品，系统随后在环境中定位该特定实例，并通过增强现实标记、空间音频及距离比例触觉引导用户前往。所有反馈均为多模态（罗马尼亚语语音合成、语音指令、振动）。在参考设备（三星Galaxy S21 Ultra）上，INT8量化将深度延迟从约1200毫秒降至约491毫秒，自定义钞票检测器mAP@50达0.986，度量深度校准误差在3米内低于1厘米。
Summary / 总结
VisionAId addresses the daily challenges faced by over 285 million visually impaired individuals by transforming a standard smartphone into a real-time multimodal assistant. It runs six on-device deep learning models—including depth estimation, instance segmentation, and a custom banknote detector—via ONNX Runtime, with optional cloud LLM support for scene narration and object labeling. A key innovation is its few-shot personal object retrieval system, where users capture multiple views of an object to enable precise location and guidance via AR markers, spatial audio, and haptic feedback. On a Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra, INT8 quantization cuts depth inference latency from 1200 ms to 491 ms, the banknote detector achieves 0.986 mAP@50, and depth is calibrated to within 1 cm error at 3 meters.
VisionAId旨在帮助全球2.85亿视觉障碍人士解决日常障碍，通过将普通智能手机转化为实时多模态助手。系统在设备端运行六个深度学习模型（包括深度估计、实例分割和自定义钞票检测器），借助ONNX Runtime实现，可选云端大语言模型用于场景描述与物体标注。其创新点在于基于少量样本的个人物品检索功能：用户拍摄物品多角度图像后，系统可精准定位并引导用户，通过AR标记、空间音频和触觉反馈实现。在三星Galaxy S21 Ultra上，INT8量化将深度推理延迟从1200毫秒降至491毫秒，钞票检测器mAP@50达0.986，深度测量误差在3米内低于1厘米。
